ṣaṣṭha karma pratya
iso— isake bāda cacayaṃbāī-cāra ādi prakṛtika baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ, ikkudayā – eka eka prakṛti ke udaya vāle, havaṃti - hote haiṃ, sabjevi - sabhī aṃdhovarame-baṃdha ke abhāva meṃ, vibhī, sahara usī prakāra, uvayābhāve - - udaya ke abhāva meṃ, vi-mī, vā - vikalpa, hojjā — hote hai |
"
gāthā - bāīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sāta se lekara dasa taka ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sāta se lekara no taka, satraha prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ chaha se lekara nau taka aura teraha prakatika baṃdhana meṃ pā~ca se lekara āṭha ---
nau prakṛtika aṃdhasthāna meṃ cāra se lekara utkṛṣṭa sāta prakṛtiyoṃ taka ke cāra udayasthāna hote haiṃ tathā pā~ca prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ do prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya jānanā cāhiye |
isake bāda ( pā~ca prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke bāda ) cāra ādi ( 4,3,2.1 ) prakṛtika baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ eka prakṛti kā udaya hotā hai | baṃdha ke abhāva meṃ bhī isī prakāra eka prakṛti kā udaya hotā hai| udaya ke abhāva meṃ bhī mohanīya kī sattā vikalpa se hotī hai|
viśeṣārtha - - pūrva meṃ mohanīya karma ke bāīsa, ikkīsa ādi prakṛtika dasa baṃdhasthāna batalāye haiṃ| yahāṃ tīna gāthāoṃ meṃ ukta sthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ kitanī kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai, isako spaṣṭa kiyā hai|
sarvaprathama bāīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ udayasthānoṃ kā kathana karate hue kahā hai-sāta prakṛtika, āṭha prakṛtika, no prakṛtika aura dasa prakṛtika, ye cāra udayasthāna haiṃ| jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jā rahā hai |

saptatikā prakaraṇa
sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna isa prakāra hai ki eka midhyātva, dūsarī apratyākhyānāvaraṇa krodha ādi meṃ se koī eka tīsarī pratyākhyānāvaraṇa krodha ādi meṃ se koī eka cauthī saṃjvalana krodha ādi meṃ se koī eka pā~cavīṃ chaṭhī rati athavā hāsya, rati ke sthāna para arati śoka aura sātavīṃ tīnoṃ vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda, ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya bāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle mithyādṛṣṭi jīva ko niyama se hotā hai|

udaya ho to puruṣa veda ke udaya ke sātha hotā hai aura yadi napuṃsaka veda kā udaya hai to usake sātha ina tāra kā banā hotā hai | isa prakāra pratyeka veda ke udaya ke sātha cāra-cāra bhaṃga prāpta ho jāte haiṃ, jo kula milākara bāraha hote haiṃ | ye bāraha bhaṃga hāsya aura rati ke udaya ke sātha bhī hote haiṃ aura yadi hāsya aura rati ke sthāna meṃ śoka aura arati kā udaya huā to unake sātha bhī hote haiṃ | isa prakāra bāraha ko do se guṇā karane para caubīsa bhaṃga ho jāte haiṃ|
pūrva meṃ batāī gaī cauvīsa bhaṃgoṃ kī gaṇanā isa prakāra bhī kī jā sakatī hai vi hāsya-rati yugala ke sātha strī veda kā eka bhaṃga tathā śoka-arati yugala ke sātha strīveda kā eka bhaṃga, isa prakāra strīveda ke sātha do bhaṃga tathā isī prakāra puruṣaveda aura napuṃsaka veda ke sātha bhī do-do bhaṃga hoṃge| kula milākara ye chaha bhaṃga hue| ye chahoṃ bhaṃga, koṣa ke udaya meṃ krodha ke sātha hoṃge| krodha ke bajāya māna kā udaya hone para māna ke sātha hoṃge | māna ke sthāna para māyā kā udaya hone para māyā ke sātha bhī hoṃge aura māyā ke sthāna para lobha kā udaya hone para lobha ke sātha bhī hoṃge | isa prakāra se pūrvokta chahoṃ bhaṃgoṃ ko krodha, māna, māyā aura lobha ina cāra se guṇita karane para kula caubīsa bhaṃga hue | arthāt krodha ke chaha bhaṃga, māna ke chaha bhaṃga, māyā ke chaha bhaṃga aura lobha ke chaha bhaṃga | yaha eka caubīsī huī|
ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya meṃ bhaya, jugupsā aura anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka meṃ se koī eka kaṣāya, isa prakāra ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se kramaśaḥ eka-eka prakṛti ke udaya ko milāne para āṭha prakṛtika udaya tīna prakāra se prāpta hotā hai | sāta prakṛtika udaya meṃ bhaya ko milāne se pahalā āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya, sāta prakṛtika udaya meṃ jugupsā ko milāne se dūsarā āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya aura anantānubaṃdhī krodhādi

saptatikā prakaraṇa
meṃ se kisī eka ko milāne se tīsarā āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya, isa taraha āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna ke tīna prakāra samajhanā cāhie | ataḥ ina bhaṃgoṃ kī tīna cauvosiyā~ hotī haiṃ | ve isa prakāra haiṃ
--
pūrvokta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya meṃ bhaya kā udaya milāne para āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya ke sātha bhaṃgoṃ kī pahalī cauvīsī huī | pūrvokta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya meṃ jugupsā kā udaya milāne para āṭha ke udaya ke sātha bhaṃgoṃ kī dūsarī caubīsī tathā pūrvokta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya meṃ anantānubaṃdhī krodhādi meṃ se kisī eka prakṛti ke udaya ko milāne para āṭha ke udaya ke sātha bhaṃgoṃ kī tīsarī caubīsī prāpta hotī hai | isa prakāra āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate bhaṃgoṃ kī tīna caubīsī hotī haiṃ|
sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ aura bhaya va jugupsā ke udaya se prāpta hone vāle āṭha prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ meṃ anantānuvandhī kaṣāya catuṣka ko grahaṇa na karane tathā bandhāvali ke bāda hī anantānubandhī ke udaya ko mānane ke sambandha meṃ jijñāsāoṃ kā samādhāna karate haiṃ | ukta jijñāsāoṃ sambandhī ācārya malayagiri kṛta ṭīkā kā aṃśa isa prakāra hai-
"manu mithyādṛṣṭeravaśyamanansānubandhināmudayaḥ sambhavati tat kathamiha midhyādṛṣṭiḥ saptodaye aṣṭobaye vā kasmiridantānubandhyudayarahitaḥ proktaḥ ? ucyate--iha samyagṛṣṭinā satā kenacit prathamato'nantānubandhino visaṃyojitāḥ, esā ca sa viśrātto na midhyātvāvikṣayātha udyuktavām tathāvidhasāmanyabhāvāt tataḥ kālāntare mithyātvaṃ gataḥ san midhyātvaramayatī sūyo'pyanasānubandhino badhnāti tatobarapālikā yāvat nāthāpyatikrāmati tāvat teṣāmuvayo na bhavati, bandhāvalikāyāṃ sthatikrāntāyāṃ bhavediti |

-
praśna – jabaki mithyādṛṣṭi jīva ke anantānuvandhī catuṣka kā udaya niyama se hotā hai, taba yahā~ sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ tathā bhaya yā jugupsā meṃ se kisī eka ke udaya se prāpta hone vāle pūrvokta do prakāra ke āṭha prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ meṃ use anantānubandhī ke udaya se rahita kyoṃ batāyā hai ?
65
samādhāna - jo samyagdṛṣṭi jīva anantānubandho catuṣka kī visaṃyojanā karake raha gayā| kṣapaṇā ke yogya sāmagrī na milane se usane mithyātva ādi kā kṣaya nahīṃ kiyā | anantara kālāntara meṃ vaha mithyātva ko prāpta huā ataḥ vahā~ usane midhyātva ke nimitta se punaḥ anantānuarat catuṣka kā vadha kiyā| aise jīva ke eka āvalikā pramāṇakāla taka anantānubandho kā udaya nahīṃ hotā kintu āvalikā ke vyatīta ho jāne para niyama se hotā hai | ata: mithyādṛṣṭi jīva ke anantānubandhī ke udaya se rahita sthāna bana jāte haiṃ| isī kāraṇa se sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ aura bhaya yā jugupsā ke udaya se prāpta hone vāle āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ anantānubandhī kā udaya nahīṃ batāyā hai |
"namu karya bandhāvalikātikrame'pyudayaḥ saṃbhavati ? pato'bādhā kālakṣaye satyudayaḥ, abādhākālaścānantānubandhināṃ jaghanyenāntarmuhartam utkarṣeṇa tu catvāri varṣa sahasrāṇīti, naṃṣa śeṣaḥ, yato bāghasamayāvārabhya teṣāṃ tāvat sattā bhavati, sattāyāṃ ca satyāṃ badhe pravartamāne tadagratā tadmatāyāṃ ca śeṣa samānajātīyaprakṛtivalika sahakāntiḥ saṃkamadhca dalikaṃ padmaprakṛtirūpatayā pariṇamate tataḥ saṃkramālikāyāmatītāyāmunayaḥ, tato baghāvalikāyāmatītāyāmuyo'bhidhīyamāno na virudhyate | 1

saptatikā prakaraṇa
tathā utkṛṣṭa abādhākāla cāra hajāra varṣa hai| ataḥ baṃdhāvali ke bāda hī anantānubandhī kā dāra se sambhata hai?
samāṣāma-baṃdha samaya se hī anantānubandhī kī sattā ho jātī hai aura sattā ke ho jāne para pravartamāna bandha meṃ patadgrahatā ā jātī hai aura patadgrahāne ko prāpta ho jāne para śeṣa samāna jātīya prakṛti dalikoṃ kā saṃkramaṇa hotā hai jo patadgraha prakṛti rūpa se pariṇata ho jātā hai jisakā saṃkramāvali ke bāda udaya hotā hai | ata: āvalikā ke bāda anantānubandhī kā udaya hone lagatā hai, ata: yaha kahanā birodha ko prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai| ___ ukta śaṃkā samādhāna kā yaha tātparya hai ki anantānubandhī catuṣka visaṃyojanā prakṛti hai aura vaise to visaṃyojanā kṣaya hī hai, kintu visaṃyojanā aura kṣaya meṃ yaha antara hai ki visaṃyojanā ke ho jāne para kālāntara meṃ yogya sāmagrī ke milane para visaṃyojita prakṛti kī puna: sattā ho sakatī hai vinnu kṣaya ko prāpta prakṛti kī punaḥ sattā nahīṃ hotī hai| sattā do prakāra se hotī hai -baṃdha se aura saṃkrama se, kintu baṃdha aura saṃkrama meṃ anyonya sambandha hai | jisa samaya jisakā baṃdha hotā hai, usa samaya usameṃ anya sajātīya prakṛti dalika kā saṃkramaṇa hotā hai | aisī prakṛti ko patadgraha prakṛti kahate haiṃ | patadgraha prakṛti kā artha hai ākara paḍa़ne vāle karmadala ko grahaṇa karane vālī prakṛti | aisā niyama hai ki saṃkrama se prāpta hue karma-dala kā maṃkramābali ke bāda udaya hotā hai| jisase anantānuvandhī kā eka āvalī ke bāda udaya mānane meṃ koī āpatti nahīṃ hai | yadyapi navīna baṃdhāvali ke bāda abādhākāla ke bhītara bhī apakarṣaṇa ho sakatā hai aura yadi aisī prakṛti udaya-prāpta huī ho to uma aṃparpita karmadala kā udayasamaya se nirapekṣa bhī ho sakatā hai, ata: navīna baṃdhe hue karmadala kā

vāīsa prakṛtika baṃsthāna meṃ udavasthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karane ke bāda aba ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ udayasthāna batalāte haiṃ ki'nava ikkavīsa sattāi udayaṭhāṇāī - arthāt ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sāta prakṛtika, āṭha prakṛtika aura nau prakṛtika ye tīna udayasthāna haiṃ| ve isa prakāra hai--inameṃ anantānubandhī, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa aura saṃjvalana prakāra kī krodhādi cāra kaṣāyoṃ meṃ se koī eka jāti kī cāra kaṣāyeṃ, tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda aura do yugaloṃ meṃ se koī eka yugala, ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ niyama se hotā hai| yahā~ bhī pūrvokta

saptatikā prakaraṇa
krama se bhaṃgoṃ ko eka caubīsī prāpta hotī hai| isa sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bhaya ke yā jugupsā ke milā dene para āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna do prakāra se prāpta hotā hai | isa prakāra āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna ke do vikalpa hote haiṃ| yahā~ eka vikalpa meṃ eka caubīsī aura dūsare vikalpa meṃ eka caubīsī, isa prakāra āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī do caubīsī hotī haiṃ | nau prakṛtika udayasthāna pūrvokta sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ yugapada bhaya aura jugupsā ko milāne se prāpta hotā hai| yaha eka hī prakāra kā hone se isameṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī eka caubīsī prāpta hotī hai|
isa prakāra ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna kī eka, āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna kī do aura nau prakṛtika udayasthāna kī eka, kula milākara bhaṃgoṃ ko cāra caubīsī hotī haiṃ|
yaha ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ke hī hotā hai aura sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi ke do bheda haiṃ-śreṇigata aura aśreṇigata | jo jīva upaśamaṇi se gira kara sāsādana guṇasthāna ko prāpta hotā hai, use śreṇigata sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi kahate haiṃ tathā jo upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi jīva upaśamaśreṇi caḍha़ā hī nahīṃ kintu anantānubandhī ke udaya se sāsādana bhāva ko prāpta ho gayā, vaha aśreṇigata sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi kahalātā hai | yahā~ jo ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sāta, āṭha aura nau prakṛtika, yaha tīna udayasthāna batalāye haiṃ ke aśreṇigata sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi jīva kī apekṣā samajhanā cāhiye |

ayocyate-yā miṣyāvaṃ pratyabhimukho ma māyāpi mipyāstra pratipāti tavānīmanamtānubadhyupamarahito'pi sāsāpanasteṣāṃ matena bhaviṣyatīti limanāyutam ? tarapustam, evaṃ sati tasya pavādīni navapalāni pavāyuśyatvāmāmi bhaveyuḥ na bhavanti, putra pratiṣepāta, tarānabhyupagamāya, sasmānamantātu. bagyubamarahitaḥ sāsāyano ma bhavatītyavāyaṃ pratyeyam |' ___ praśna-jisa samaya koī eka jīva mithyātva ke abhimukha to hotā hai kintu mithyātva ko prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai, usa samaya una ācāryoṃ ke matānusāra usake anantānubaṃdhī ke udaya ke binā bhī sāsādana guṇasthāna kī prāpti ho jāyegī | aisā māna liyā jānā ucita hai |
samādhāna-yaha mānanā ṭhīka nahīṃ hai, kyoṃki aisā mānane para usake chaha prakṛtika, sāta prakṛtika, āṭha prakṛtika aura nau prakṛtika, ye cāra udayasthāna prāpta hote haiṃ | kintu āgama meṃ aisā batāyā nahīṃ hai aura ve ācārya bhī aisā nahīṃ mānate haiṃ | isase siddha hai ki anantānubaṃdhī ke udaya ke binā sāsādana samyaksva kī prāpti nahīṃ hotī hai|

sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ anantānubaṃdhī ko choḍa़kara apratyā khyānāvaraṇa ādi tīna prakāroṃ ke krodhādi kaṣāya catuṣkoṃ meṃ se koī eka krodhādi, tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda, do yugaloṃ meṃ se koī eka yugala aura sabhya mithyātva ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā niyama se udaya rahatā hai| yahā~ bhī pahale ke samāna bhaṃgoṃ kī eka caubīsī prāpta hotī hai | isa sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bhaya yā jugupsā ke milāne se āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha sthāna do prakāra

caubīsī upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi aura kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke aura cāra caubīsī vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke hotī haiṃ |
pā~ca prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ saṃjvalana kodha, māna, māyā aura lobha inameṃ se koī eka tathā tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda, isa prakāra do prakṛtiyoṃ kā eka udayasthāna hotā hai- paṃcavibaṃdhage puṇa udao dohaṃ |' isa sthāna meṃ cāroṃ kaṣāyoṃ ko tīnoṃ vedoṃ se guṇita karane para bāraha bhaṃga hote haiṃ | ye bāraha bhaṃga nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke pāṃca bhāgoṃ meṃ se pahale bhāga meṃ hote haiṃ |
pā~ca prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke bāda ke jo cāra, tīna, do aura eka prakṛtika baṃsthāna haiṃ, unameṃ eka-eka prakṛti vālā udayasthāna hotā hai | arthāt ina udayasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ eka-eka prakṛti kā udaya hotā hai--' ittī caubaṃdhāī ikkekakudayā havaṃti samve vi|' jisakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce karate haiṃ |
pā~ca prakṛtika baṃghasthāna meṃ se puruṣaveda kā baṃdhaviccheda aura udayaviccheda eka sātha hotā hai, ataḥ cāra prakṛtika baṃdha ke samaya cāra saṃjvalanoṃ meṃ se kisī eka prakṛti kā udaya hotā hai | isa prakāra yahā~ cāra bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ| kyoṃki koī jīva saṃjvalana krodha ke udaya se śreṇi ārohaṇa karate haiṃ, koī saṃjvalana māna ke udaya se, koī saṃjvalana māyā ke udaya se aura koī saṃjvalana lobha ke udaya se śreṇi caḍha़te haiṃ| isa prakāra cāra bhaṃga hote haiṃ |
yahā~ para kitane ho ācārya yaha mānate haiṃ ki bāra prakṛtika baṃdha ke saṃkrama ke samaya tīna vedoṃ meṃ se kisī eka veda kā udaya hotā hai | ataḥ unake mata se cāra prakṛtika baṃdha ke prathama kāla meṃ do prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai aura isa prakāra cāra kaṣāyoṃ ko tona vedoṃ se guṇita

nau prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ 'chatraka' - bhaṃgoṃ kī kula chaha caubīsī hotī haiṃ| ve isa prakāra haiṃ- bāīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ jo nau prakṛtika udayasthāna hai, usakī tīna caubīsī hotī haiṃ| ikkīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke samaya jo no prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai, usakī eka caubīsī miśra guṇasthāna meṃ satraha prakṛtika baṃsthāna ke samaya jo mau prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai, usake bhaṃgoṃ kī eka caubīsī aura cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ satraha prakṛtika baṃdha ke samaya 'udayasthāna hotā hai, usake bhagoṃ kī eka caubīsī | prakṛtika udayasthāna ke bhago ko kula chaha bīsī huī|

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha caubīsī hotī hai tathā cāra, tīna, do aura eka prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke tathā abandha ke samaya eka prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kramaśa: cāra, tīna, do, eka para eka gaṃgore haiṃ| inakā hoḍa़ gyāraha hai| ata: eka prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kula gyāraha bhaṃga hote haiṃ| ____ isa prakāra se gāthā meṃ mohanīya karma ke saba udayasthānoṃ meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī cauvīsī aura phuṭakara maṃgoṃ ko spaṣṭa kiyā gayā hai|

ukta nausau paṃcānava bhaṃgoṃ meṃ se yathāsaṃbhava kisī na kisī eka bhaṃga se mohita honā kahā gayā hai| ___ mohanīyakarma kī mithyātva, anantānubandhī kādha, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa krodha, pratyāsthānāvaraṇa krodha ādi pratyeka prakṛti ko pada kahate haiṃ aura unake samudāya kā nāma padavṛnda hai| isī kā dūsarā nāma prakṛtivikalpa bhī hai | arthāt dasa prakṛtika ādi udayasthānoṃ meṃ jitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā grahaṇa kiyā gayā hai, ve saba pada haiṃ aura unake bheda se jitane bhaṃga hoṃge, ve saba padavṛnda yā prakṛtivikalpa kahalāte haiṃ | yahā~ unake kula bheda 6971 batalāye haiṃ| jisakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa isa prakāra hai
dasa prakṛtika udayasthāna eka hai, ataḥ usakī dasa prakṛtiyāṃ huī| nau prakṛtika udayasthāna chaha haiṃ ata: unakī 646--54 prakṛtiyā~ huī 1 āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna myāraha haiṃ ata: unakī aṭhāsī prakṛtiyā~ huī | sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna dasa haiṃ ata: unako sattara prakṛtiyā~ huī | chaha prakṛtika udayasthāna sāta haiṃ ataḥ unakī bayālīsa prakṛtiyā~ huī| pāṃca prakṛtika udayasthāna cāra haiṃ ata: unakī bīsa prakṛtiyā~ huī | cāra prakṛtika udayasthāna ke eka hone se usakī cāra prakṛttiyā~ huī aura do prakṛtika udayasthāna eka hai ata: usakī do prakṛtiyā~ huīṃ| ina saba prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne para 10 54+88+70+42 +2+4+2=kula' joḍa़ 290 hotā hai|
ukta 260 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ caubīsa-caubīsa bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ ata: 260 ko 24 se guṇita karane para kula 6960 hote haiṃ | isa saṃkhyā meṃ eka prakṛtika udayasthāna ke gyāraha bhaṃga sammilita nahīṃ haiṃ | ataḥ una gyāraha bhaṃgoṃ ke milāne para kula saṃkhyā 6971 ho jātī hai | yahā~ yaha viśeṣa jānanā cāhiye ki pahale jo matāntara se cāra

prakṛtika baṃdha ke saṃkamakāla ke samaya do prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bāraha bhaṃga batalāye the, unako sammilita karake yaha udayasthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā aura padasaṃkhyā batāī hai| arthāt udayasthānoṃ meṃ se matāntara vāle bāraha bhaṃga kama kara diye jāyeṃ to 683 udayavikalpa hote haiṃ aura dvi-prakṛtika udayasthāna ke bāraha bāraha bhaṃga kama kara diye jāyeṃ to padoṃ kī kula saṃkhyā 6647 hotī hai| viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa āge kī gāthā meṃ kiyā jā rahā hai| aba bāraha bhaṃgoṃ ko choḍa़kara udayasthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā aura padasaṃkhyā kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ|
navatesīyasahi udayavigappehi mohiyā jīvā| auṇasarisīyālā paryāvavasaehi vinnayā ||20||
śamā-navatesīyasahi-nau so tirāsī, udayavieppaihiudayavikalpoṃ se. mohiyā-mohita hue, jovā-jība, avatarisīyālā-unahattara sau saṃtālīsa, pavivasahi-padoṃ ke samūha, vileyā-jānanā cāhiye |
gāthārya-saṃsārī jīva nauso tirāsī udayavikalpoṃ se aura unahattara sau saiṃtālīsa pada samudāyoṃ se mohita ho rahe haiṃ, aisā jānanā cāhiye|
viśeṣārtha—pūrva gāthā meṃ matāntara kī apekṣā udayavikalpoṃ aura padavṛndoṃ kī saṃkhyā batalāī hai | isa gāthā meṃ vamata se udayavikalpoṃ aura padavṛndoṃ kī saṃkhyā kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa karate haiṃ|
pichalī gāthā meṃ udayavikalpa 665 aura padavṛnda 6671 batalāye haiṃ aura isa gāthā meṃ udayavikalpa 153 aura padavṛnda 6947 kahe haiṃ | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai-cāra prakṛtika baṃdha ke saṃkrama ke samaya do prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai, yadi isa matāntara ko mukhyatā na dī jāye aura unake mata se do prakṛtika udayasthāna ke udayavikalpa aura

6971 aura 6947 | jaba cāra prakṛtika bandha ke samaya kucha kāla taka do prakṛtika udaya hotā hai, taba isa mata ko svīkāra kara lene para 6671 padavṛnda hote haiṃ aura isa mata ko choḍa़ne para 6947 padavṛnda hote haiṃ| paṃcasaṃgraha saptatikā meṃ ye donoṃ saṃkhyāyeṃ batāī haiṃ. kintu inake atirikta sātha hī cāra prakāra ke padavṛnda aura batalāye haiṃ| unameṃ pahalā prakāra 6640 kā hai, jisameṃ bandhābandha ke bheda se eka prakṛtika udaya ke 11 bhaṃga na hokara kula 4 bhaṃga liye jāte haiṃ | isa prakāra 6947 meṃ se 7 bhaṃga kama hokara 6640 saṃkhyā hotī hai| śeṣa tīna prakāra ke padavṛnda guṇasthāna bheda se batāye haiṃ jo kramaśaḥ 84778483 aura 8507 hote haiṃ |
go0 karmakāṇḍa meṃ padavṛnda ko prakṛtivikalpa saṃjñā dī hai| udayavikalpoṃ kī taraha ye prakṛtivikalpa bhī punarukta aura apunarukta do prakāra ke batāye haiṃ| punarukta udayavikalpoṃ kī apekṣā inakī saṃkhyā 8507 aura apunarukta udayavikalpoṃ kī apekṣā inakī saṃkhyā 6941 batāī hai| paṃcasaṃgraha saptatikā meṃ jo 6640 padavṛnda batalāye haiṃ, unameṃ guṇasthāna bheda se 1 bhaṃga aura milā dene para 6941 prakṛtivikalpa ho jāte haiṃ | kyoṃki paṃcasaṃgraha saptatikā meṃ eka prakṛtika "udayasthāna ke kula cāra bhaṃga liye gaye haiṃ aura karmakāṇḍa' meṃ guṇasthāna bheda se pā~ca liye gaye haiṃ| jisase eka bhaṃga baḍha़ jātā hai |
ūpara jo kathana kiyā gayā hai usameṃ jo saṃkhyāoṃ kā antara dikhatā hai, vaha vivakṣābhedakṛta hai, mānyatābheda nahīṃ hai |

saptatikā prakaraṇa
120
matoṃ se udayavikalpoṃ aura prakṛtivikalpoṃ ke bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana karane ke bāda aba udayasthānoṃ ke kāla kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ|
dasa ādika jitane udayasthāna aura unake bhaṃga batalāye haiṃ, unakā jaghanyakāla eka samaya aura utkṛṣṭakāla antarmuhūrta hai |"
cāra prakṛtika udayasthāna se lekara dasa prakṛtika udayasthāna taka ke pratyeka udayasthāna meṃ kisī eka veda aura kisī eka yugala kā udaya hotā hai aura veda tathā yugala kā eka muharta ke bhītara avaśya hī parivartana ho jātā hai| isī bāta ko paṃcasagraha kī mūla ṭīkā meṃ bhī batalāyā hai
"ovena yugalena vā avaśyaṃ muhūrtābārasaḥ parāvartitavyam |" arthāt eka muhūrta ke bhītara kisī eka veda aura kisī eka yugala kā avaśya parivartana hotā hai|
isase niścita hotā hai ki ina cāra prakṛtika ādi udayasthānoṃ kā aura unake bhaṃgoṃ kā jo utkṛṣṭakāla antarmuhūrta kahā hai, vaha ṭhīka hai | do aura eka prakṛtika udayasthāna bhī adhika se adhika antarmuhūrta kāla taka pāye jāte haiṃ| ata: unakā bhī utkṛṣṭakāla antarmuhūrta hī hai |
ina saba udayasthānoṃ kā jaghanyakāla eka samaya isa prakāra samajhanā cāhiye ki jaba koī jīva kisī vivakṣita udayasthāna meṃ yā usake kisī eka vivakṣita bhaṃga meṃ eka samaya taka rahakara dūsare samaya meṃ mara kara yā parivartana kama se kisī anya guṇasthāna ko prāpta hotā hai taba usake guṇasthāna meṃ bheda ho jātā hai, bandhasthāna bhī badala jātā hai aura guṇasthāna ke anusāra usake udayasthāna aura usake bhaṃgoṃ meṃ bhī antara paḍa़ jātā hai| ataḥ saba udayasthānoṃ aura usake saba bhaṃgoṃ kā jaghanyakāla eka samaya prāpta hotā hai |
1 iha daśāvaya udayāstabhaṃgāraca javanyata ekasāmayikā utkarṣata āntamīhūrtikaḥ | - saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 170

paṃcaṣiha-pāṃca prakṛtika bandhasyāna meṃ, babihesaṃ... pāra prakṛtika bandhasthāna meṃ, chakka-chaha-chaha, sesesa-bākī ke bandhasthānoṃ meṃ, jāṇa-jāno, paṃceca-pāṃca hī, patteya-pattema-pratyeka meṃ, (eka-eka meṃ), pattāra-cāra, 4-aura, saṃbhavocchee-bandha kā viccheda hone para bhī|
gāyā--bāīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna meṃ tīna, ikkīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna meṃ aṭṭhāīsa prakṛti vālā eka, satraha prakṛtika bandhasthāna meṃ chaha, teraha prakṛtika aura nau prakṛtika bandhasthāna meṃ pāṃca-pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ 1

aba yahā~ do gāthāoṃ meṃ mohanīya karma ke bandhasthāna aura sattāsthānoṃ ke paraspara saṃvedha kā nirdeśa kiyā gayā hai| sātha hī bandhasthāna, udayasthāna aura sattāsthānoṃ ke paraspara saṃvedha kā kathana karanā āvaśyaka hone se bandhasthāna aura sattāsthānoṃ ke paraspara saṃvedha ko batalāte hue prāpta hone vāle udayasthānoṃ kā bhī ullekha kareṃge| ___ mohanīya karma ke vāīsa, ikkīsa, satraha, teraha, nau, pā~ca, cāra, tīna, do aura eka prakṛtika kula dasa bandhasthāna haiṃ| unameṃ kramaśa: sattāsthānoṃ kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa karate haiṃ |
'tinna va ya bāvīse'-bāīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke samaya tīna sattāsthāna hote haiṃ 28, 27 aura 26 prakṛtika | jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa isa prakāra hai-bāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha mithyāṣṭi jīva ko hotā hai aura usake udayasthāna cāra hote haiṃ-7, 8, 6 aura 10 prakṛtika | inameṃ se 7 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke samaya 28 prakṛtika satāsthāna hotā hai | kyoṃki sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna anantānubandhī ke udaya ke binā hī hotā hai aura mithyātva meṃ anantānubandhī ke udaya kā abhāva usī jīva ke hotā hai, jisane pahale samyagdṛṣṭi rahate anantānubandhī catuṣka kī visaṃyojanā kī aura kālāntara meṃ pariṇāmayaśa mithyātva meṃ jākara mithyātva ke nimitta se puna: anantānubandhī ke bandha kā prārambha kiyā ho | usake eka āvalī pramāṇa kāla taka anantānubandhī kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai| kintu aise jīva ke niyama se bhaṭṭhāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā pāī jātī hai| jisase sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ eka aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika udayasthāna hī hotā hai|

prakṛtika hī hotā hai - igavīse aṭṭhavīsa | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki ikkīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi ko hī hotā hai aura sāsādana samyaktva upaśama samyaktva se cyuta hue jīva ko hotā hai, kintu aise jīva ke darśanamohanīya ke tīnoṃ bhedoṃ kī sattā avaśya pāī jātī hai, kyoṃki yaha jīva samyaktva guṇa ke nimitta se mithyātva ke tīna bhāga kara detā hai, jinheṃ kramaśa: mithyātva samyagmithyātva aura samyaktva kahate haiṃ | ataḥ isake darśana mohanīya ke ukta tīnoṃ bhedoṃ kī sattā niyama se pāī jātī hai| yahā~ udayasthāna sāta, āṭha aura nau prakṛtika, ye tīna hote haiṃ| ataḥ ikkīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke samaya tīna udayasthānoṃ ke rahate hue eka aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika ho sattāsthāna hotā hai |"
satraha prakṛtika bansthāna ke samaya chaha sattāsthāna hote hai - 'sattarase chacceva' jo 28,27,26,24,23, 22 aura 21 prakṛtika hote haiṃ | saha prakṛtika bandhasthāna samyagmithyādṛṣṭi aura avirata samyagdṛṣṭi, ina do guṇasthānoṃ meṃ hotā hai|
inameṃ se samyagmithyādṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke 7 5 aura 6 prakṛtika yaha tīna udayasthāna hote haiṃ aura avirala samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke cāra udayasthāna hote haiṃ- 6, 7, 8 aura prakṛtika | inameṃ se chaha prakṛtika
1 ekaviṃśatibandho hi sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭe bhaṃvati, sāsādanatvaṃ ca jīvasyopaśamikasamyaktvāt pracyavamānasyopajāyate, samyaktvaguṇena ca midhyātvaṃ tridhākṛtam, tayā - samyaktvaṃ miśraṃ mithyātvaṃ ca tato darśanatrikasyāpi satkarmatayā prāpyamāṇatvād ekaviṃśatibaṃdhe triṣvapyudayasthāneṣvaṣṭāviṃśatirekaṃ sattāsthānaṃ bhavati | - saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, 10 171 saptadaśabandhī hi iyānāṃ bhavati, tadyathā— samyagmithyādṛṣṭināma viratasamyagdṛṣṭīnāṃ ca | tatra samyagmithyādṛṣṭīnāṃ trībhyudayasthānāni tadyathāsapta aṣṭo naba aviratasamyagdṛṣṭināṃ catvāri tadyathā ṣaṭsapta aṣṭau nava | - saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭokā, pṛ0 171

saptatikā prakaraṇa udayasthāna upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi yā kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ko hī prāpta hotā hai | upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ko aṭṭhāīsa aura caubīsa prakṛtika ye do sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna prathamopaśama samyaktva ke samaya hotā hai tathā jisane anantānubaṃdhī kī ubalanā kī usa aupaśamika avirata samyamhaṣṭi ke caubīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna hotā hai kintu kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ke ikkīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna hī hotā hai| kyoṃki anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka aura darśanamohatrika ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke kṣaya hone para hī usakī prāpti hotī hai| isa prakāra chaha prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ 28, 24 aura 21 prakṛtika, ye tīna sattāsthāna hote haiṃ|
samyagmidhyāiṣṭi jīvoṃ ke sāta prakṛtika jagaśāna ke rahane 28, 27 aura 24 ye tīna sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| inameṃ se aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā vālā jo jīva samyagmithyātva ko prāpta hotā hai, usake aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna hotā hai, kintu jisa mithyādṛṣṭi ne samyaktva kī udvalanā karake sattāīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna ko prāpta kara liyā kintu abhī sampagmithyātva kī udvalanā nahīṃ kī, vaha yadi mithyātva se nivṛtta hokara pariṇāmoṃ ke nimitta se samyagmithyātva guṇasthāna ko prāpta hotā hai to usa samyagmithyādṛṣṭi jīva 1 kṣāyikasamyagdṛṣṭīnāṃ tvekaviṃśatireva, kṣāyika hi samyaktvaṃ saptakakṣaye bhavati, saptakakṣaye ca janturekaviṃśatisatkarmeti |
–saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 172 2 samyagmicyādṛṣṭi ke 27 prakṛtika sattāsthāna hone ke mata kā ullekha
digambara paramparā meṃ dekhane meṃ nahīṃ āyā hai| go. karmakāṃḍa meṃ vedakakāla kā nirdeśa kiyā gayā hai, usa kāla meṃ koī bhī mithyāiṣṭi jīva vedaka samyaghaṣṭi yā samyagmidhyāhṛṣṭi ho sakatā hai, para yaha kāla samyaksya kī udvalanā ke cālū rahate hue nikala jātā hai| ata: vahā~ 27 prakṛtika sattā vāle ko na to vedaka samyaktva kī prāpti batalāī hai aura na samyagmithyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna kī|

ṣaṣṭha karma abhya
?*
ke sattāisa ki sattā hotā hai tathā samaṣṭi rahate hue jisane anantānubandhī kī visaṃyojanā kī hai, vaha yadi pariṇāmavaśāt samyagmithyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna ko prāpta karatā hai to usake caubīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna pāyā jātā hai| aisā jīva cāroṃ gatiyoṃ meṃ pāyā jātā hai| kyoṃki cāroṃ gatiyoṃ kā samyagdṛṣṭi jīva anantānubandhī kī visaṃyojanā karatā hai | 1
karmaprakṛti meṃ kahā bhī hai
"bagayā pajjattā timdi vi saṃjoyaṇe vijayaṃti | karaṇehi tīhi sahiyā naṃtarakaraṇaṃ javasamo vā || 2 arthāt cāroṃ gati ke paryāpta jīva tīna karaṇoṃ ko prāpta hokara anantānubaṃdha kī visaṃyojanā karate haiṃ, kintu inake anantānubaṃdhī kā antarakaraṇa aura upaśama nahīṃ hotā hai |
yahā~ viśeṣatā itanī hai ki avirata samyagdṛṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ cāroṃ gati ke jīva, deśavirati meṃ tiryaṃca aura manuṣya jīva tathā sarvavirati meṃ kevala manuṣya jīva anantānubandhī catuṣka ko visaṃyojanā karate haiṃ | anantānubaṃdha kī bisaṃyojanā karane ke bāda kitane hī jīva pariṇāmoṃ ke vaṃśa se samyagmithyātva guṇasthāna ko bhī prāpta hote haiṃ | jisase samyagmithyādṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke caubīsa prakṛtika sattāsthāna hotā hai, yaha siddha huā |
lekina avirata samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ke sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna rahate 28, 24, 23, 22 aura 21, ye pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| inameṃ se 20
1 yataścatugaṃtikā api samyadṛṣṭayo'nantānubaṃdhino visaṃyojayanti | - saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 172

ph
atra 'tinni vi' ti aviratā deśaviratāḥ sarvaviratā vā yathāyogamati | - saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 172

saptatikā prakaraṇa
aura 24 prakṛtika to upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi aura vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke hote haiṃ, kintu yaha viśeṣatā hai ki 24 prakṛtika sattāsthāna, jisane anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka kī visaṃyojanā kara dī hai, usako hotā hai|' 23 aura 22 prakṛtika sattāsthāna vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke hī hote haiṃ| kyoṃki āṭha varṣa yā isase adhika āyu vālā jo vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva kṣapaṇā ke liye udyata hotā hai, usake anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka aura mithyātva kā kṣaya ho jāne para 23 prakṛtika satāsthāna hotā hai aura phira usī ke samyagmithyātva kā kṣaya ho jāne para 22 prakṛtika sattāsthāna hotā hai | yaha 22 prakṛtika sattā vālā jīva samyaktva prakṛti kā kṣaya karate samaya jaba usake antima bhāga meṃ rahatā hai aura kadācit usane pahale parabhava sambandhī āyu kā baṃdha kara liyā ho to mara kara cāroṃ gatiyoṃ meṃ utpanna hotā hai | kahā bhī hai
"paddhvago u maṇūso niṭṭho ghausu vi gaīsu | ___ arthāt darśanamohanīya kī kṣapaṇā kā prārambha kevala manuṣya hī karatā hai, kintu usakī samāpti cāroṃ gatiyoṃ meṃ hotī hai|
isa prakāra 22 prakṛtika sattāsthāna cāroṃ gatiyoṃ meṃ prāpta hotā hai kintu 21 prakṛtika sattāsthāna kṣādhika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ko hī prāpta hotā hai| kyoṃki anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka aura darśanamohatrika, ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā kṣaya hone para hī kṣāyika samyagdarśana hotā hai |
isī prakāra āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate hue bhī samyagmidhyā---.1 navaramanantānubandhivisaṃyojanānantaraṃ sā avamantacyā |
–saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 172 2 sa ca dvāviṃśatisatkarmā samyaktvaṃ kṣapayan taccaramagrāse vartamāna: kaścit
pūrvāyurukaḥ kālamapi karoti, kālaṃ ca kṛtvā catasuṇā gatīnāmanyatamasyāṃ gatātpadyate |
--sapātikā prakaraṇa ṭokā, pṛṣṭha 172

pāṭha karmamanva
126 dṛṣṭi aura avirata samyagdaṣṭi jīvoṃ ke kramaśaḥ pūrvokta tīna aura pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| nau prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate hue bhī isī prakāra jānanā cāhiye, lekina itanī viśeṣatā hai ki aviratoṃ ke nau prakṛtika udayasthāna vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke hī hotā hai aura vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke 28, 24, 23 aura 22 prakṛtika, ye cāra sasāsthāna pāye jāte haiṃ, ata: yahāṃ bhī ukta cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ |
satraha prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna sambandhī ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki samyagmithyādṛṣṭi ke 17 prakṛtika eka baṃdhasthāna aura 7, 8, 9 prakṛtika ye tīna udayasthāna tathā 26, 27 aura 24 prakṛtika, ye tīna sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | avirata sambandāṣṭra meṃ upaśama samvādṛṣṭi ke 15 prakṛtika eka baṃdhasthāna aura 6, 7, 8 prakṛtika tīna uvayasthāna tathaḥ 28 aura 24 prakṛtika do sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi ke eka 17 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna tathā 6,7 aura 8 prakṛtika, ye tīna udaya sthāna tathā 21 prakṛtika eka sattāsthāna hotā hai| vedaka samyagduṣita ke 17 prakṛtika eka baṃdhasthāna tathā 7, 8 aura ha prakṛtika tīna udaya rasthāna tathā 28, 24, 23 aura 22 prakṛtika cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ saṃveṣa bhaṃgoṃ kā pūrva meṃ nirdeśa kiyā jā cukā hai, ata: yahāṃ kisa kitane baṃdhādi sthāna hote haiṃ, isakā nirdeśa mātra kiyā hai|
teraha aura nau prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke rahate pāṃca-pāṃca sattāsthā hote haiṃ-'tera navagaṃdhagesu paṃceva tāṇāī| ve pāṃca sattāsthāna 28, 2' 23, 22 aura 21 prakṛtika hote haiṃ| pahale teraha prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna sattāsthānoṃ ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ|
teraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha deśāviratoṃ ko hotā hai aura veśavira do prakāra ke hote haiṃ-tiyaṃca aura manuṣya |' tithaṃca deśaviratoṃ | 1 tatra trayodaśabandhakā deśaviratā: te ca vidhā-tiyaco manuṣyāśca |
-sapātikā prakaraṇīkā, pṛ015

saptavikā prakaraṇa
unake cāroṃ hī udayasthānoṃ meṃ 28 aura 24 prakṛtika, ye do sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | 28 prakṛtika sattāsthāna to upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi aura bevaka samyagdṛṣṭi, ina donoṃ prakāra ke hī tiryaca deśaviratoṃ ke hotā hai | usameṃ bhī jo prathama samyaktva ko utpanna karane ke samaya hī deśanirasa kī jāpta kara detā hai, basa deśavira ke samyaktva ke rahate hue 25 prakṛtika sattāsthāna hotā hai | kyoṃki antarakaraṇa kāla meṃ vidyamāna koī bhī aupaśamika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva deśavirata ko prāpta karatā hai aura koī manuṣya sarvavirata ko bhī prāpta karatā hai, aisā niyama hai| jaisāki śataka bṛhaccūrṇi meṃ kahā bhī hai
samasammaddiṭṭī ansarakaraṇe ṭhio koī vesabira koī emattāpamattabhāvaṃ pi gaccha, sāsaravarṇā puma na kimavi lahaī |
arthāt antarakaraṇa meṃ sthita koī upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi jīva deśavirati ko prāpta hotā hai aura koī pramattasaṃyama aura apramattabhāva ko bhī prāpta hotā hai, parantu sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi jīva inameṃ se kisī ko bhī prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai|
-
isa prakāra upaśama samprahṛṣṭi jīva ko deśavirati guṇasthāna kī prāpti ke bāre meṃ batāyā ki vaha kaise prāpta hotā hai | kintu vedaka samyaktva ke sātha deśavirati hone meṃ koī viśeṣa bādhā nahīṃ hai | jisase deśavirati guṇasthāna meṃ vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi ke 28 prakṛtika sattāsthāna bana hī jātā hai | kintu 24 prakṛtika sattāsthāna anantānubaṃdhī kī visaṃyojanā karane vāle tiryaṃcoṃ ke hotā hai, aura ve vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi hote haiṃ| kyoṃki tiyaṃcagati meṃ aupaśamika samyagdṛṣṭi ke
1
jayaghavalā ṭīkā meṃ svāmī kā nirdeśa karate samaya cāroṃ gatiyoṃ ke jīvoṃ ko 24 prakṛtika sattāsthāna kā svāmī batalāyā hai| isake anusāra pratyeka gati kā upaśama samyagdṛṣṭi jīva anantānubandhī kī visaṃyojanā kara sakatā hai| karmaprakṛti ke upaśamanā prakaraṇa gā0 31 se bhī isī mata kī puṣṭi hotī hai| yahā~ cāroṃ gati ke jīvoṃ ko anantānubaṃdhī kī visaṃyojanā karane vālā batāyā hai|

ṣaṣṭha karma grantha
131
24 prakṛtika sattāsthāna kī prāpti saṃbhava nahīṃ hai | ina do sattāsthānoṃ ke atirikta tica deśavirata ke śeṣa 23 ādi saba sattāsthāna nahīṃ hote haiṃ, kyoṃki ve kṣāyika samyavaśva ko utpanna karane vāle jīvoṃ ke hī hote haiṃ aura tithaṃca kṣāyika samyagdarśana ko utpanna nahīṃ karate haiṃ| ise to kevala manuṣya ho utpanna karate haiṃ |"
teīsa prakṛtika ādi sattāsthāna tiryaṃcoṃ ke nahīṃ mānane ko lekara jijñāsu praśna pūchatā hai
MAR
"jaya manuṣyāḥ kṣāyikasamyakramamutpādya yathā sirdhapakhante tatharā tiraracī:pyekaviṃśatiḥ prāpyata eva tat kathamucyate śeṣāṇi trayovidārayādīni sarvāṇi na sambhavanti ? iti tad ayuktam, yataḥ kṣāyikasamyaṣṭistirpaśu na saṃpadevardhāmuṣkeṭa madhye samutpate ke na ca tatra deśaviratiḥ, tababhāvācca na trayośabandhakatvam atra trayodaśbandhe sattāsthānāni visyamānāni tala eka vidhātirapi trayodaśabaje tiryakṣu na prāpyate |
praśna--- yaha ṭhīka hai ki tiryaṃcoṃ ke 23 prakṛtika sattāsthāna nahīṃ hotā hai, tathāpi jaba manuṣya kṣāyika samyagdarśana ko utpanna karate hue yā utpana karake tiyaṃcoṃ meṃ utpanna hote haiṃ taba ticoṃ ke bhī 22 aura 21 prakṛtika sattāsthāna pāye jāte haiṃ| ataḥ yaha kahanā yukta nahīṃ hai ki tiryaṃcoṃ ke 23 ādi prakṛtika sattāsthāna nahīṃ hote haiṃ|
uttara - yadyapi yaha ṭhīka hai ki kṣāyika samyaktva ko utpanna karane bālā 22 prakṛtika sattā vālā jīva yā kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva mara kara tiryaṃcoṃ meṃ utpala hotā hai, kintu yaha jīva saṃsthāta varṣa kī āyu vāle tiryacoṃ meṃ utpanna na hokara asaṃkhyāta varṣa kī āyu vāle tithaṃcoṃ
1 śeṣāṇi tu sarvāpyapi trayoviṃśatyādīni sattāsthānāni tiraścāṃ na sambhavanti tāni hi śrāmikasamyaktvamutpādayataḥ prāpyante na ca tiryacaḥ yāyika samyakasyamutpādayati, kintu manuṣyā eka |
3
- saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 173

saptatikā prakaraṇa
meṃ hī utpanna hotā hai aura unake deśa virati nahīṃ hotī hai aura deśavirati ke na hone se unake teraha prakṛtika baṃṣasthāna nahīṃ pāyā jātā hai | parantu yahā~ teraha prakṛtika baṃṣasthāna meṃ sattāsthānoṃ kā vicāra kiyā jā rahā hai | ata: ūpara jo yaha kahā gayā hai ki tiyaṃtroṃ ke 23 ādi prakṛtika sattāsthāna nahīṃ hote haiṃ, vaha 13 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna kī apekṣā se ṭhīka hī kahā gayā hai | cūṇi meṃ bhī kahā hai--
egavīsā tirisane saṃjayā'saṃjaesu na saṃbhavai | kahaṃ ?. bhaggā-saṃjabāsā esu tiriksesu lāgasammaṭṭhiī ME asalenamāsāueṣu umajeṣamā, tassa resavira masthi|
arthāt-tiryaṃca saṃyatāsaṃyatoṃ ke 21 prakṛtika sattāsthāna nahīṃ hotā, kyoṃki kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva saṃkhyāta varṣa kī āyu vāle tiyaṃcoṃ meṃ utpanna nahīṃ hotā hai | asaṃkhyāta varṣa kī āyu vāle tiyaṃcoṃ meṃ utpanna hotā hai, kintu vahā~ unake deśavirati nahīṃ hotī hai |
isa prakāra se tiryaṃcoṃ kī apekṣā vicāra karane ke bāda aba manuṣyoṃ ko apekṣā vicāra karate haiṃ| ___ jo deśavirata manuṣya haiṃ, unake pāṃca prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate 28, 24 aura 21 prakṛtika, ye tīna sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| chaha prakṛtika aura sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate pratyeka meṃ 28, 24, 23, 22 aura 21 prakṛtika, ye pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna ke rahate 28, 24, 23 aura 22 prakṛtika ye cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| udayasthānagata prakṛtiyoṃ ko dhyāna meṃ rakhane se inake kāraṇoṃ kā niścaya sugamatāpūrvaka ho jātā hai| arthāt jaise avirata samya. mhaṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ kathana kiyā gayā hai, vaise hī yahā~ bhī samajha lenā cāhime | ata: alaga se kāthana na karake kisa udayasthāna meṃ kitane sattāsthāna hote haiṃ, isakā sirpha saṃketamātra kiyā gayā hai |

1
saptatikā prakaraṇa
aba mohanīya karma ke kathana kā upasaṃhāra karake nāmakarma ko kahane kī pratijñā karate haiṃ |
dasanavapannarasāī baṃdhovasantapayariṭhāṇāī | bhaṇiyāī mohaṇijje itto nāmaṃ paraṃ vockhaṃ || 23||' ghoya
māṇvārtha -- vasana vapaśnarasāī- dasa, nau aura pandraha, santapayaṭhāṇā, udaya aura sattā prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthāna, bhaniyā kahe, mohaṇije mohanīya karma ke, iso - isase, nāma-nāmakarma ke paraṃ āge vocchaṃ kahate haiṃ |
gārtha - mohanīya karma ke baṃdha, udaya aura sattā prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthāna kramaśaḥ dasa, nau aura pandraha kahe| aba āge nāmakarma kā kathana karate haiṃ|
vivīdhārtha - mohanīya karma ke bandha, udaya aura sattāsthānoṃ ke kathana kā upasaṃhāra karate hue gāthā meṃ saṃketa kiyā gayā hai ki mohanīya karma ke baṃdhasthānaṃ dasa, udayasthāna no aura sattāsthāna pandraha hote haiṃ| jinameṃ aura jinake saṃbaṃdha bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana kiyā jā cukā hai | aba āge kī gāthā se nāmakarma ke baṃdha, udaya aura sattā ke saṃvedha bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana prārambha karate haiṃ |
gāmakarma
sabase pahale nāmakarma ke baṃdhasthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ-tevīsa paṇṇavosā chaṭhavīsā aṭṭavīsa guṇatosā | tīse gatīsa mekkaṃ baṃdhadvāṇāṇi

155
arthāt-devagati ke yogya 28, 29, 30 aura 31 prakṛtika baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ kramaśa: āṭha, āṭha, eka aura ekā, kula aṭhāraha bhaṃga hote
hai|
___ abhī taka tiyaṃtra, manuṣya aura deva gati yogya baṃdhasthānoṃ aura unake bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā | atra narakagati ke baṃdhasthānoṃ va unake bhaṃgoṃ ko batāte haiṃ|
narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle jīvoṃ ke eka aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna hotā hai| isameṃ aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtiyā~ hotī haiṃ, ata: unakā samudāya rūpa eka baṃdhasthāna hai| yaha bandhasthāna mithyādṛṣṭi ke hī hotā hai| isameṃ saba aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha hone se yahā~ eka hī bhaṃga hotā hai| aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika prasthāna meṃ grahaṇa kī gaī prakṛtiyā~ isa prakāra haiṃ-narakati, narakānugu:, paṃcendriya jāti, vakriya śarīra, baiṃkriya aṃgopāṃga, tejasa śarīra,kārmaṇa śarīra, huṃḍa saṃsthāna, varṇacatuṣka, agurula cu, upaghāta, parāghāta, ucchavāsa, apraśasta vihāyogati, basa, vādara, paryāpta, pratyeka, asthira, aśubha, durbhaga, duḥsvara, anādeya, ayaśaḥkīti aura nirmāṇa||
ina teīsa ādi uparyukta baṃdhasthānoṃ ke atirikta eka aura baṃdhasthāna hai jo devagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdhaviccheda ho jāne para apūrvakaraṇa ādi tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ hotā hai| isa eka prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ sirpha yaśaḥkīti nāmakarma kā baṃdha hotā hai|' ___ aba kisa baṃdhasthāna meṃ kula kitane bhaṃga hote haiṃ, isakā vicāra karate haiṃ
eka tu baṃdhasthānaṃ yaśaḥkotilakṣaṇam taccha devagatiprāyogya banthe vyacchinne apūrvakāraṇādīnāṃ trayāṇāmavagantavyam |

samasatikā prakaraṇa
cau paṇavīsā solasa naba bāṇauīsayā ya aḍayālā | eyāluttara chāyālasayā ekkeṣaka baṃdhavihī ||25||
śabdārtha-cabāra, paṇavīsā-paccīsa, sosasa-solaha, nava-nau, māṇauīsayā-bānavasau, ga-aura, ajayālā--atālīsa, emāluttara chāyālasayā-chiyālīsa sau ekatālīsa, ekakekaeka-eka, aṃdhaviho-baṃdha ke prakāra, bhaṃga |
gāthārtha teīsa prakṛtika ādi baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ krama meṃ cāra, paccīsa, solaha, nau, bānavasau aḍa़tālīsa, chiyālīrā sau ikatālīsa, eka aura eka bhaṃga hote haiṃ |
viśeṣāyaṃ--pūrva gāthā meṃ nāmakarma ke baṃdhasthānoṃ kā vivecana karake pratyeka ke bhaṃgoṃ kā ullekha kiyā hai| parantu unase pratyeka baṃdhasthāna ke samuccaya rūpa se bhaṃgoṃ kā bodha nahīṃ hotā hai| ataḥ pratyeka baṃdhasthāna ke samuccaya rūpa se bhaṃgoṃ kā bodha isa gāthā dvārā karāyā jā rahā hai|
nāmakarma ke pūrva gāthā meṃ 23, 25, 26, 28, 29, 30, 31 aura 1 prakṛtika, ye āṭha baṃdhasthāna batalāye gaye haiṃ aura isa gāthā meṃ sāmānya se pratyeka baṃdhasthāna ke bhaṃgoṃ kī alaga-alaga saṃkhyā batalā dī gaī hai ki kisa baṃdhasthāna meṃ kitane bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kintu yaha spaṣṭa nahīṃ hotā hai ki ve kisa prakāra hote haiṃ | ata: una bhaṃgoṃ ke hone kā vicāra pūrva meṃ batāye gaye baṃdhasthānoṃ ke krama se karate haiṃ| ___pahalā baṃdhasthāna teīsa prakṛtika hai| isa sthāna meṃ cāra bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kyoṃki yaha sthāna aparyāpta ekendriya ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ ke bāṃdhane vāle jīva ke hī hotā hai, anyatra teīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna nahīṃ pāyā jātā hai| isake cāra bhaṃga pahale batā āye haiṃ| ata: teīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ ve hī cāra bhaṃga jānanā cāhiye |

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
paccīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ kula paccīsa bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kyoṃki ekendriya ke yogya paccīsa pravṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle jīva ke bīsa bhaṃga hote haiṃ tathā aparyāpta dvīndriya, śrīndriya, caturindriya, tiryaca paṃcendriya aura manuṣyagati ke yogya paccīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane bāle jīvoṃ ke eka-eka bhaṃga hote haiṃ| ata: pūrvokta bīsa bhaṃgoṃ meṃ ina pāṃca bhaṃgoṃ ko milāne para paccīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ kula paccīsa bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
chabbīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke kula solaha bhaṃga haiṃ| kyoṃki yaha ekendriya ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle jīva ke hī hotā hai aura ekendriyaprāyogya chabbīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ pahale solaha bhaṃga batā āye haiṃ, ata: ve hī solaha bhaṃga isa chabbīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ jānanā cāhiye|
aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ kula nau bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kyoṃki devagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ ko baṃdha karane vāle jīva ke 28 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna ke āṭha bhaṃga hote haiṃ aura narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle jīva ke aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika prasthāna kā eka bhaṃga| yaha sthāna deva aura nāraka ke sivāya anya jīvoṃ ko kisī bhī prakāra se prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai | ata: isake kula nau bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
unatīsa prakṛtika badhasthāna ke 248 bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki tiryaṃca paṃcendriya ke yogya unatīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke 4608 bhaṃga hote haiṃ tathā manuṣyagati ke yogya unatīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke 4608 bhaṃga haiṃ aura dvīndriya, śrīndriya, caturindriya ke yogya evaṃ tīrthakara nāma sahita devagati ke yogya unatīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke āṭha-āṭha bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isa prakāra ukta saba bhaṃgoṃ ko milāne para unatīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna ke kula bhaṃga 4608+4608++++ 8+8- 6248 hote haiṃ|

saptatikā prakaraṇa
śabdārtha-vīsiṃgavīsā--bīsa aura ikkīsa kā, cavīsagāi-caubīsa se lekara, egāliyā-eka-eka adhika, aura, igatīsā-ikatīsa taka, udayaṭṭhāṇāṇi-udayasthāna, bhave-hote haiṃ, nava aṭṭhama -nau aura āṭha prakṛti kā, haṃti --hote haiṃ, nāmassanāmakarma ke|
gāthārya-nāmakarma ke bīsa, ikkīsa aura caubīsa se lekara eka, eka prakṛti adhika ikatīsa taka tathā āṭha aura nau prakṛtika, ye bāraha udayasthāna hote haiṃ| viśeṣārtha-nāmakarma ke baṃdhasthāna batalāne ke bāda isa gāthā meṃ udayasthāna batalāye haiṃ| ve udayasthāna bāraha haiṃ| jinakī prakṛtiyoṃ ko saṃkhyā isa prakāra hai-20, 21, 24, 25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31, 8 aura ha| ina udayasthānoṃ kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa tiryaṃca, manuṣya, deva aura narakagati ke ādhāra se nīce kiyā jā rahā hūṃ|
nāmakarma ke jo bāraha udayasthāna kahe haiṃ, unameṃ se ekendriya jīva ke 21, 24, 25, 26 aura 27 prakṛtika, ye pāṃca udayasthāna hote haiṃ| yahā~ tejasa, kārmaṇa, agurula ghu, sthira, asthira, śubha, aśubha, varṇacatuṣka aura nirmāṇa ye bāraha prakṛtiyā~ udaya kī apekṣā dhra ba haiṃ| kyoṃki terahaveṃ sayogikevalī guṇasthāna taka inakā udaya niyama se sabako hotā hai | ina dhruvodayā bāraha pravatiyoṃ meṃ tiryaṃcagati, tiryacānupūrvī, sthāvara, pavendriya jāti, bādara-sūkṣma meṃ se koī eka, paryāpta aparyāpta meṃ se koī eka, durbhaga, anādeya tathā yaśa:kītiayaśaḥkīti meṃ se koī eka, ina nau prakṛtiyoṃ ke gilā dene para. ikkīsa prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha udayasthāna bhaya ke apāntarāla meṃ vidyamāna ekendriya ke hotā hai| __isa udagrasthāna meṃ pāṃca bhaṃga hote haiṃ, jo isa prakāra haiṃ-yādara paryāpta, bādara aparyāpta, sūkṣma paryāpta, sukṣma aparyāpta, ina cāroṃ

aba vaikriya śarīra karane vāle tiryaṃca paṃcendriya kī apekṣā baṃdhasthāna aura unake bhaṅgoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ | __vaikriya śarīra karane vāle tiryaṃca paṃcendriyoṃ ke 25, 27, 28, 26 aura 30 prakṛtikā, ye pāṃca udayasthāna hote haiṃ|
pahale jo tithaṃca paṃcendriya ke 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna batalāyā hai, usameṃ vaikriya śarīra, baiṃkriya aṃgopāṃga, samacaturastra saṃsthāna, upaghāta aura pratyeka ina pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne tathā tiryaṃcānupūrvī ke nikāla dene para paccīsa prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | isa 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ subhaga aura dubhaṃga meṃ se kisī eka kā, ādeya aura anādeya meṃ se kisī eka kā tathā yaśaḥkīti aura ayaśaḥkīti

166
ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
meṃ se kisī eka kā udaya hone ke kāraṇa 2x22 = 8 bhaṅga
hote haiṃ|
anantara śarīra paryāpti se paryāpta hue jīva ke parāghāta aura praśasta vihāyogati ina do prakṛtiyoṃ ko 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ milā dene para 27 prakṛtika udaya hotā hai. yahā~ bhī pūrvavat ka bhaṅga hote haiṃ |
ukta 27 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ prāṇāpāna paryāpti se paryāpta hue jīva ke ucchavāsa prakṛti ko milā dene para 28 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yahā~ bhī pahale ke samāna āṭha bhaṅga hote haiṃ | athavā śarīra paryāpti se paryāpta hue jīva ke yadi udyota kā udaya ho to bhī 28 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai, yahā~ bhī āṭha bhaṅga hote haiṃ| isa prakāra 28 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke solaha bhaṅga hote haiṃ |
anantara bhāṣā paryāpta se paryāpta hue jīva ke ucchavāsa sahita 28 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ susvara ke milāne para 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yahā~ bhī āṭha bha hote haiṃ | athavā prāṇāpāna paryāpta se paryāpta hue jīva ke ucchavāsa sahita 28 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ udyota ko milāne para bhī 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| isake bhī āṭha bhaṅga hote haiṃ | isa prakāra 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kula solaha bhaṅga hote haiṃ |
anantara susvara sahita 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ udyota ko milāne para 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | isake bhī āṭha bhaṅga hote haiṃ |
isa prakāra baikiya śarīra ko karane vāle paṃcendriya tiryaṃcoṃ ke kula udayasthāna 25, 27, 28, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika aura unake kula bhaṅga 8+6+16+16+656 hote haiṃ| ina 56 bhaṅgoṃ ko pahale ke sāmānya paṃcendriya tiryaṃca ke 4606 bhaṅgoṃ meṃ milāne para saba tima~coṃ ke kula udayasthānoṃ ke 4962 bhaṅga hote haiṃ |

170
saptatikā prakaraṇa
isa prakāra se tiryacoṃ ke ekendriya se lekara paṃcendriya taka ke bhedoṃ meṃ udayasthāna aura unake bhaṅgoṃ ko batalāne ke paścāt aba manuṣyagati kī apekṣā udayasthāna va bhaṅgoṃ kā kāthana karate haiṃ|
manuṣyoṃ ke padāsthānoṃ kā kathana sāmānya, vakriyaśarīra karane bāle, āhāraka śarīra karane vāle aura kevalajñānī kī apekṣā alagaalaga kiyā jā rahā hai ||
sāmānya manuṣya-sāmānya manuṣyoṃ ke 25, 26, 28, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika, ye pā~ca udayasthāna hote haiṃ| ye saba udayamyāna tiyaṃ va paṃcendriyoṃ ke pūrva meṃ jisa prakāra kathana kara āye haiṃ, usī prakāra manuṣyoṃ ko bhī samajhanā cāhiye, kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki manuṣyoṃ ke niyaṃcagati, tirya cānupūrvī ke sthāna para manuṣyagati aura manuṣyānupūrvī kā udaya kahanā cāhiye aura 29 va 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna udyota rahita kahanā cāhiye, kyoṃki vaikriya aura āhāraka saṃyatoṃ ko choḍa़kara śeṣa manuṣyoṃ ke udyota kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai| isaliya tiyaṃcoṃ ke jo 26 prakṛtika udayasyāna meṃ 1152 bhaṅga kahe unake sthāna para manuṣyoṃ ke kula 576 bhaṅga hote haiṃ| isī prakāra tiryacoṃ ke jo 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ 1728 bhaṅga kahe, unake sthāna para manuṣyoṃ ke kula 1152 bhaṅga prāpta hoṃge|
isa prakāra sāmānya manuṣyoṃ ke pūrvokta pā~ca udayasthānoṃ ke kula E+2 +576+576+ 1152= 2602 bhaṅga hote haiṃ|
paṃkriya śarīra karane vāle manuṣya-vaikriya śarīra ko karane vāle manuṣyoṃ ke 25, 27, 28, 26 aura 30 prakṛtikā, ye pā~ca udayasthāna hote haiṃ| bāraha dhruvodaya prakṛtiyoṃ ke sātha manuṣyagati, paṃcendriya jāti, vakriya śarīra, vakriya aṃgopāṃga, samacaturastra, saṃsthāna, upaghāta, trasa, bādara, paryāpta, pratyeka, subhaga aura dubhaṃga meṃ se koī eka, ādeya aura anādeya meṃ se koī eka tathā yaśaḥkīti aura ayaśa kīti meṃ se koī

28 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ susvara ke milāne para 26 prakṛtika jdayasthāna hotā hai | yahā~ bhī āṭha bhaṅga hote haiṃ| athavā saṃyatoṃ ke svara ke sthāna para udyota ko milāne para 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| isakā eka hī bhaṅga hotā hai| isa prakāra 29 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kula 6 bhaṅga hote haiṃ|

172
āhāraka saṃyata- āhāraka saṃyatoṃ ke 25, 27, 25, 26, aura 30 prakṛtika, ye pāṃca udayasthāna hote haiṃ|
pahale manuṣyagati ke udayayogya 21 prakṛtiyā~ batalāī gaī haiṃ, unameṃ āhāraka śarīra, āhāraka aṃgopāga, samacaturasra saṃsthāna, upaghāta aura pratyeka, ina pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne tathā manuṣyānupūrvī ko kama karane para 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | āhāraka śarīra ke samaya praśastai prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī udaya hotā hai, kyoṃki āhāraka saṃyatoṃ ke apraśasta prakṛtiyoṃ - dubhaṃga dusvara aura ayaśaḥkīti prakṛti kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai| isalie yahā~ eka hī bhaja hotā hai |
anantara ukta 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ śarīra paryāpta se paryāpta
1 go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ vaipi vaśarīra aura vaikriya aṃgopāṃga kā udaya deva aura nārakoṃ ko batalāyā hai, manuṣyoṃ aura tiryoṃ ko nahīṃ | ataeva yahā~ baikriya śarīra kī apekṣā se manuṣyoṃ ke 25 ādi prakṛtika udayasthāna aura unake maṃgoṃ kā nirdeśa nahīṃ kiyā hai| isī kāraṇa se vahā~ vāyukāyika aura paṃcendriya tiyaṃca ke bhī vaikiya śarīra kī apekṣā udayasthānoṃ aura unake maṃgoṃ ko nahīṃ batāyā| yadyapi isa saptatikā prakaraṇa meṃ ekendriya ādi jīvoṃ ke udayaprāyogya nāmakarma kī baṃdha prakṛtiyoṃ kā nirdeśa nahīṃ kiyā hai tathāpi ṭīkā se aisā pratīta hotā hai ki vahā~ devagati aura narakagati kī udayayogya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ hī vaikriya śarīra aura vaikriya aṃgopāṃga kā grahaṇa kiyā gayā hai| jisase aisā jñāta hotā hai ki tica aura manuṣyoṃ ke vaikriya śarīra aura vaikriya aṃgopāṃga kā udaya nahīṃ honā cāhie, tathāpi karmaprakṛti ke udīraṇā prakaraṇa kī gāthā meṃ se isa bāta kā samarthana hotā hai ki yathāsambhava tiyaṃtra aura manuṣyoṃ ke bhī ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya va udīraṇā hotī hai |

kevalajñānī- kevalī jīvoṃ ke 20, 21, 26, 27, 18, 29, 30, 31, 6 aura 8 prakṛtika ye dasa udayasthāna hote haiṃ |
nāmakarma kī bāraha dhruvo dayā prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ manuṣyagati, paṃcendriya jāti, sa, bādara, paryāpta, subhaga, ādeya, yaśaḥ kīrti ina āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ ke milāne se 20 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| isakā eka bhaṅga hotā hai | yaha udayasthāna samudghātagata atīrthaṃ kevalī ke kāma kāya yoga ke samaya hotā hai |
ukta 20 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ tīrthakara prakṛti ko milāne para 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha udayasthāna samudghātagata tīrthakara kevalī ke kārmaṇakāyayoga ke samaya hotā hai| isakā bhī eka bhaṅga hai|

pṛṣṭha pharmagrantha
175
26 prakṛti udavasthāna meṃ tīrthakara prakṛti ko milāne para 27 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yaha sthāna tīrthaṃkara kevalī ke audārika miśra kāyayoga ke samaya hotā hai | isa udayasthāna meṃ samacaturasra saṃsthāna kā hī udaya hone se eka hī bhaṅga hotā hai|
pūrvokta 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ parādhāta uccha bāsa, praśasta vihāyogati aura apraśasta vihāyogati meṃ se koī eka tathā susvara aura duḥsvara meṃ se koī eka ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ ke milāne se 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha sthāna atīrthakara sayogi kevalī ke audārika kāyayoga ke samaya hotā hai | yahā~ chaha saṃsthāna, praśasta aura apraśasta vihāyogati tathā susvara aura duḥsvara kī apekṣā 6×2x2=24 bhaṅga hote haiṃ | kintu ve sāmānya manuṣyoṃ ke udayasthānoṃ meṃ prāpta hote haiṃ, ata: inakī pṛthak se gaṇanā nahīṃ kī gaī hai |
30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ tīrthaṃkara prakṛtika ko milā dene para 31 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yaha tīrthaṃkara sayogikevalī ke audārika kāyayoga ke samaya hotā hai tathā tīrthaṃkara kevalī jaba vākyoga kā nirodha karate haiṃ taba unake svara kā udaya nahīṃ rahatā hai, jisase pūrvokta 31 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ se eka prakṛti ko nikāla dene para tīrthaṃkara kevalī ke 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | jaba ucchavāsa kā nirodha karate haiṃ taba ucch vāsa kā udaya nahīṃ rahatā, ataḥ ucchavāsa ko ghaṭā dene para 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | kintu atīrthaṃkara kevalī ke tīrthaṃkara prakṛtika kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai ataḥ pūrvokta 30 aura 26 prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ se tīrthakara prakṛti ko kama kara dene para anīrthakara kevalī ke vacanayoga kā virodha hone hone para 26 prakṛtika aura ucchavāsa kā nirodha hone para 28 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | atīrthaṃkara kevalī ke ina donoṃ udayasthānoṃ meṃ chaha saṃsthāna aura praśasta va apraśasta vihāyogati, ina donoṃ kī apekṣā

176
saptatikā prakaraṇa
12,12 bhaṅga hote haiṃ| kintu ve sāmānya manuṣyoṃ ke udayasthānoṃ meṃ sambhava hone se unakī alaga se ginatī nahīṃ kī hai|
prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ manuṣyagati, paṃcendriya jāti, asa, bādara, paryāpta, subhaga, ādeya, yaśaḥkīti aura tīrthakara, ina nau prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai| yaha nau prakṛtika udayasthāna tīrthaṃkara kevalī ke ayogikevatī guṇasthāna meṃ prāpta hotā hai| isa udayasthāna meṃ se tīrthakara prakṛti ko ghaṭā dene para āṭha prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ atīrthaṃkara kevalī ke hotā hai|
yahā~ kevalī ke udagrasthānoṃ meṃ 20,21,27,26,30,31, 6 aura 8 ina āṭha udayasthānoṃ kā eka-eka viśeṣa bhaṅga hotā hai| ata: āṭha bhaṅga hue| inameṃ se 20 praśika, aura nika, do udayasthānoṃ ke do bhaṅga atīrthaṃkara kevalī ke hote haiṃ tathā śeṣa chaha bhaṅga tīrthaṃkara kevalī ke hote haiṃ|'
isa prakāra saba manuṣyoṃ ke udarasthāna sambandhī kula bhaṅga 2602+35+7+8= 2652 hote haiṃ |
aba devoṃ ke udayasthāna aura unake bhaṅgoṃ kā kathana karate haiṃ |
devoṃ ke 21, 25, 27, 28, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika, ce chaha udayasthāna hote haiṃ|
nāmakarma kī dhruvodayā bāraha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ devagati, devānupūrvī, paṃcendriya jāti, asa, bādara, paryāpta, subhaga aura durbhaga meṃ se koī eka, ādeya aura anādeya meṃ se koī eka tathā yaśa:kīti aura ayaśaḥkīrti meṃ se koī eka, ina nau prakṛtiyoṃ ke milā dene para 21 prakṛtika 1 śaha kevalyudayasthānamadhye viśati ekaviṃśati-rāptaviṃśati,ekonaviśat-priśad
ekatriśad-navā'ṣṭarūpaiyaṣṭasūdayasthāneṣu pratyemekakako viśeṣamaṃgaḥ prāpyate imaṣṭo bhaṃgāḥ | tatra viśatyaṣṭakayobhaṃgāvatīrtha kṛtaḥ śeṣeṣu ṣaṭsu udapasthāneṣu tīrtha kṛtaḥ ṣaḍ bhaṃgāḥ| --saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 186

anantara śarīrastha nāraka ke vaikriya śarīra, vaikriya aṃgopāṃga, huṃḍasaṃsthāna, upaghāta aura pratyeka ina pā~ca prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne aura narakānupūrvī ke nikāla dene para 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yahā~ bhī eka bhaṃga hotā hai|

granthakāra ne jīvasthāna pada ke artha kā bodha karāne ke liye gāthā meṃ 'jīvasaṃkhevaesu' pada diyā hai arthāt jina aparyāpta ekendriyatva ādi dharmoṃ ke dvārā jīva saṃkṣipta yānī saṃgṛhīta kiye jāte haiṃ, unakī jīvasaṃkṣepa saṃjñā hai— unheṃ jīvasthāna kahate haiṃ | isa prakāra jīvasaṃkṣepa pada ko jīvasthāna pada ke artha meṃ svīkāra kiyā gayā hai | ekendriya sūkṣma aparyāpta ādi jīvasthānoṃ ke caudaha bheda caturthaṃ karmagrantha meṃ batalāye jā cuke haiṃ|

śamvārtha-tara-taraha jojasthānoṃ me, nava-nāṃ prakṛtika baṃdha, ghāu paṇagaṃ-cāra athavā pāṃca prakṛtika udaya, manasaṃta-nau kī sattā, egambhi-eka jīvasthāna meṃ, bhaṃgakārā-gyāraha maṃga hote haiṃ, veṇiyāuyagoe-vedanīyā, āyū aura gośa karma meṃ, vibhajja--vikalpa karake, mohaṃ-mohanīya karma ke, paraṃ-āge, vocchaṃ-- maheṃge|

darśanāvaraṇa karma ke baṃdhādi vikalpa isa prakāra haiṃ ki ādi ke teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ nau prakṛtika baṃdha, cāra yā pā~ca prakṛtika udaya tathā nau prakṛtika sattā, ye do bhaṃga hote haiṃ| arthāt nau prakṛtika baṃdha, cāra prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā yaha eka bhaṃga aura nau prakṛtika baṃdha, pāṃca prakṛtika udaya tathā nau prakṛtika sattā yaha dūsarā bhaṃga, isa prakāra ādi ke teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ do bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki prārambha ke teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ darśanāvaraṇa karma kī kisī bhī uttara prakṛti kā na to baṃdhaviccheda hotā hai, na udayaviccheda

215 ___arthāt-paryāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvasthāna meṃ vedanīya karma ke āṭha bhaṃga aura śeṣa teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ cāra bhaṃga hote haiṃ tathā gotra karma ke paryāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvasthāna meṃ sāta bhaṃga aura śeṣa teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ tīna bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
ukta kathana kā viśada vivecana nimna prakāra hai-vedanīya karma ke pani saṃjhī ganendriya jījasthāna meṃ daulaha guṇa thāna sambhava haiṃ ata: usameṃ, 1. asātā kā bandha, asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ kī sattā, 2. asātā kā baṃdha, sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ kī sattā, 3. sātā kā bandha, asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī satā, 4. sātā kā bandha, sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ ko sattā, 5. asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ kī sattā, i. sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ kī sattā, 7. asātā kā udaya aura asātā kī sattā aura 8. sātā kā udaya tathā sātā kī sattā, ye āṭha bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kintu prārambha ke teraha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka ke ukta āṭha bhaṃgoṃ meṃ se ādi ke cāra bhaṃga hī prāpta hote haiṃ| kyoṃki inameṃ sātā aura asātā vedanīya ina donoṃ kā yathāsambhava bandha, udaya aura sattā sarvatra sambhava hai | imoline bhāṣya gāthā meṃ kahā gayā hai ki 'pajjattagasanniyare acha caukkaṃ ca veyaṇiyabhaṃgā|' _ vedanīya karma ke ukta āṭha bhaṃgoṃ ko paryāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvasthāna meṃ guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā isa prakāra ghaṭita karanā cāhiye
pahalā bhaṃga --asātā kā baṃdha, asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā ko sattā tathā dūsarā bhaṃga-asātā kā baṃdha, sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī sattā, yaha do bhaṃga pahale mithyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna se lekara chaṭhe pramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna taka pāye jāte haiṃ| kyoṃki āge ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ asātā vedanīya ke baṃdha kā abhāva hai| tīsarā bhaṃga

sātā kā baṃdha, asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī sattā cauthā bhaṃga-sātā kā baṃdha, sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī sattā, yaha do bikalpa pahale mithyā dṛṣṭi guṇasthāna se lekara terahaveṃ sayogikevalī guṇasthāna taka pāye jāte haiṃ| isake bāda baṃdha kā abhāva ho jāne se pā~cā bhaṃga-asātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī sattā tathā chaṭhā bhaṃga--sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asātā donoṃ kī sattā, yaha do bhaṃga ayogi kevalī guṇasthāna meṃ dvicarama samaya taka prāpta hote haiṃ aura carama samaya meṃ sātavā bhaṃga- asātā kā udaya aura asātā kī sattā tathā āṭhavāṃ bhaṃga-sātā kā udaya aura sātā kī sattā, yaha do bhaṃga pāye jāte haiṃ|

meṃ aparyāpta nāma karma kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai tathā inake parabhava saṃbaṃdhī manuṣyāyu tathā tiryaṃcāyu kā hī bandha hotā hai, ataḥ inake manuṣyagati kī apekṣā 5 aura tiryaṃcagati kī apekṣā 5 bhaṃga, isa prakāra kula dasa bhaṃga hote haiṃ| jaise ki tiryaṃcagati kī apekṣā 1- āyubaṃdha ke pahale tiryacāyu kā udaya aura tiyaṃcāyu kī sattā 2 āyubaṃdha ke samaya tiyaṃcāya kā baṃdha, tiryaṃcā kā aura nitidāyu kī manuṣyāyu kā baṃdha, ticāyu kā udaya aura manuṣya tiryacāyu kī sattā, 4. baṃdha kī uparati hone para tiyaṃcāyu kā udaya aura tiryaca tiryaṃcāyu kī sattā athavā 5 tiryatrāyu kā udaya aura manuṣya-tiyaṃ cāya kī sattā | kula milākara ye pā~ca bhaṃga hue |

śabdārtha - anusu - āṭha jīvasthānoṃ ne paṃcasu-pā~ca jīvasthānoṃ meṃ, ege -- eka jīvasthāna meṃ, ega – eka, durgā-do, daladasa aura mohabaṃdha gae - mohanīya karma ke baṃdhagata sthānoṃ meṃ, tiga una-tīna bāra aura nau, udaya gae --- udayagata sthāna, liṃga tiga pannarasa tīna tīna aura pandraha sami-satā ke sthāna |

gāvārtha- āṭha, pā~ca aura eka jīvasthāna meṃ mohanīya karma ke anukrama se eka, do aura dasa baṃdhasthāna, tīna, cāra aura mau udayasthāna tathā tīna tīna aura pandraha sattāsthāna hote haiṃ |
viśeṣārtha - isa gāthā meṃ mohanīya karma ke jīvasthānoṃ meṃ baṃdha, udaya aura sattā sthāna batalāye haiṃ aura jīvasthānoṃ tathā baṃdhasthānoṃ, udayasthānoṃ tathā sattāsthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā kā saṃketa kiyā hai ki kitane jīvasthānoṃ meṃ mohanīya karma ke kitane baṃdhasthāna haiṃ, kitane udayasthāna haiṃ aura kitane sattāsthāna haiṃ| parantu yaha nahīṃ batāyā hai ki ve kauna-kauna hote haiṃ| ataḥ isakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jā rahā hai|
āṭha, pā~ca aura eka jīvasthāna meṃ yathākrama se eka, do aura dasa baṃdhasthāna haiṃ | arthāt āṭha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ eka baṃdhasthāna hai, pā~ca jīvasthānoṃ meṃ do baṃdhasthāna haiṃ aura eka jīvasthāna meṃ dasa baṃdhasthāna haiṃ| inameṃ se pahale āṭha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ eka baṃdhasthāna hone ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ ki paryāpta sukṣma ekendriya, aparyāpta sukṣā ekendriya, aparyāpta bādara ekendriya, aparyāpta dvīndriya, aparyāpta śrīndriya, aparyāpta caturindriya, aparyāpta asaṃjñī paṃcendriya aura aparyāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya, ina āṭha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ pahalā midhyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna hī hotā hai ataḥ inake eka 22 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna hotā hai | ve 22 prakṛtiyā~ isa prakāra haiṃ - midhyātva, anantānubaṃdhī kaṣāya catuṣka ādi solaha kaṣāya, tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda, hāsya rati aura śoka arati yugala meṃ se koī

226
saptatikā prakaraṇa jīvasthānoṃ meṃ mohanīya karma ke baṃdhādi sthānoṃ va saṃvaidha bhaṃgoṃ ko batalāne ke bāda aba nāmakarma ke bhaṃgoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ
paṇa duga paṇagaṃ paṇa cau paṇagaṃ paṇagā havaṃti tinneva | paṇa chappaṇagaṃ chamachappaṇagaṃ adṛṭṭha dasagaṃ ti ||37|| satteva apajjasā sāmī taha suhama bāyarā ceva | viliviyā u tinni u taha ya asamnī ya sannī ya||38||
bhāvārya-paṇa duga paṇarga-pāṃca, do, pā~ca, paṇa cau pagagaṃ--pāca, cāra, pāṃca, pagagā-pāṃca-pāṃca, havaṃti-hote haiṃ, tinmeṣa-tīnoṃ hī (baṃdha, udaya aura sattāsthāna), paṇa chappaṇa-pāṃca, chaha, pāca, chappaṇagaṃ-chaha, chaha, pāca, aṭuṭa-āṭha, āṭha, basagaṃ- dasa, ti–isa prakāra |
satteba-sātoṃ hī, apajjatā aparyāpta, sāmī-svāmī, tahatathā, suhama-sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta, pāparā-bādara ekendriya paryāpta, gheva-aura, vigalikṣyiā-vikalendriya paryāpta, timi-līna, tahavaise hī, pa-aura, asammī-asaṃjī paṃcendriya paryāpta, sannī-saṃjī paṃcendriya paryāpta |
gāpārṣa-pāṃca, do, pā~ca; pā~ca, cāra, pā~ca; pā~ca, pā~ca, pā~ca pāṃca, chaha, pāṃca chaha, chaha, pā~ca aura āṭha, āṭha, dasa; ye baṃdha, udaya aura sattāsthāna haiṃ| .

221
saṃkhyā udayasthāna kī aura tīsarī saṃkhyā sattāsthāna kī dyotaka hai | gāthā meṃ saṃkhyā ke aise kula chaha puṃja haiṃ| dūsarī gāthā meṃ caudaha jīvasthānoṃ ko chaha bhāgoṃ meṃ vibhājita kiyā gayā hai| jisakā yaha tātparya huā ki pahale bhāga ke jīvasthāna pahale puṃja ke svāmī dūsare bhāga ke jīvasthāna dūsare puṃja ke svāmī haiṃ ityādi |
yadyapi gāthāgata saṃketa se itanā to jāna liyā jātā hai ki amuka jīvasthāna meṃ itane baṃdhasthāna, itane udayasthāna aura itane sattāsthāna haiṃ, kintu ve kauna-kauna se haiṃ aura unameṃ kitanī kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā grahaṇa kiyā gayā hai, yaha jñāta nahīṃ hotā hai| ataḥ yahā~ unhīṃ kā bhaṃgoṃ ke sātha ācārya malayagiri kṛta ṭīkā ke anusāra vistāra se vivecana kiyā jātā hai|
'paṇa durgā paṇagaṃ satteva apajjattā' donoṃ gāthāoṃ ke padoṃ ko yathākrama se joḍa़ne para yaha eka pakṣa huā| jisakā yaha artha huā ki caudaha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se sāta aparyāpta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ pā~ca baṃdhasthāna, do udayasthāna aura pā~ca sattāsthāna haiṃ | jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa yaha hai ki sāta prakāra ke aparyāpta jīva manuṣyagati aura tiryaṃcagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, devagati aura narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā nahīṃ, ataḥ ina sāta aparyāpta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ 28, 31 aura 1 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna na hokara 23, 25, 26, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika, ye pāṃca baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ aura inameṃ bhī manuṣyagati tathā tithaṃcagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha hotā hai | ina baṃghasthānoṃ kā viśeṣa vivecana nāmakarma ke baṃghasthāna batalāne ke avasara para kiyā gayā hai, ataḥ vahā~ se samajha lenā cāhiye| yahā~ saba baṃsthānoṃ ke milākara pratyeka jīvasthāna meṃ 13947 bhaṃga hote haiṃ |

230
saptatikā prakaraṇa
ina sāta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ do udayasthāna haiṃ-21 aura 24 prakṛtika | so inameṃ se 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ aparyāpta bādara ekendriya ke tithaṃcagati, tiryaṃcānupūrvī, taṃjasaśarīra, kārmaṇaśarīra, agurulaghu, varṇacatuṣka, ekendriya jāti, sthāvara, bādara, aparyāpta, sthira, asthira, śubha, aśubha, durbhaga, anādeya, ayaśa:kīrti aura nirmāṇa ina 21 prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai | yaha udayasthāna apāntarāla gati meṃ pāyā jātā hai | yahā~ bhaṃga eka hotā hai kyoṃki yahā~ parāvartamāna śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai | ____ aparyāpta sūkṣma ekendriya sī ko bhī yahī śyāta ho | kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki usake bādara ke sthāna meṃ sūkṣma prakṛti kā udaya kahanā cāhie | yahā~ bhī eka bhaṃga hotā hai|
isa 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ audārikaśarīra, huṃḍasaṃsthāna, upaghāta aura pratyeka va sādhāraṇa meṃ se koī eka, ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne aura tiryaṃcānupūrvī isa prakṛti ko ghaṭā dene para 24 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | jo donoṃ sūkṣma va bādara ekendriya aparyāpta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ samāna rūpa se sambhava hai| yahā~ sūkṣma aparyāpta aura bādara aparyāpta meṃ se pratyeka ke sādhāraṇa aura pratyeka nāmakarma kī apekṣā do-do bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isa prakāra do udayasthānoṃ kī apekṣā donoṃ jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka ke tīna-tīna bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
vikalendriyatrika aparyāpta, asaṃhī aparyāpta aura saṃjñī aparyāpta, ina pāṃca jīvasthānoṃ meṃ 21 aura 26 prakṛtika, yaha do udayasthāna hote haiṃ| inameṃ se aparyāpta dvīndriya ke tiryaṃcagati, tiryaṃcānupūrvī, tejasa, kārmaṇa, aguruladhu, varṇacatuṣka, dvīndriya jāti, basa, bādara, aparyāpta, sthira, asthira, śubha, aśubha, durbhaga, anādeya, ayaśaḥkīrti aura nirmāṇa yaha 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | jo apāntarāla gati meṃ

ṣada karmagrantha
vidyamāna jīva ke hī hotā hai, anya ke nahīṃ| yahā~ sabhī prakṛtiyā~ apraśasta haiṃ, ata: eka hī bhaṃga jānanā cāhiye|
isī prakāra trīndriya ādi jīvasthānoṃ meṃ bhī yaha 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna aura 1 bhaṃga jānanā cāhiye 1 kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki pratyeka jīvasthāna meṃ dvīndriya jāti na kahakara śrīndriya jāti ādi apanī-apanī jāti kā udaya kahanā cāhiye|
anantara 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ śarīrastha jīva ke audārika śarīra, audārika aṃgopāṃga, huṃḍasaṃsthāna, sevāta saṃhanana, upadhāta aura pratyeka ina chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ke milāne aura tirya cānupūrvī ke nikāla dene para 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yahā~ bhī eka hī bhaṃga hotā hai| isa prakāra aparyāpta dvīndriya ādi pratyeka jīvasthāna meṃ do-do udayasthānoṃ kī apekṣā do-do bhaṃga hote haiṃ |
lekina aparyāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvasthāna isakā apavāda hai| kyoṃki aparyāpta saṃjño jīvasthāna tiryaṃcagati aura manuṣyagati donoṃ meṃ hotā hai | ata: yahā~ isa apekṣā se cāra bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ|'
ina sāta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ 62, 58, 16, 80 aura 78 prakṛtika pā~ca-pā~ca sattāsthāna haiṃ | aparyāpta avasthā meṃ tīrthakara prakṛti kī sattā sambhava nahīṃ hai ata: ina sātoṃ jīvasthānoṃ meṃ 63 aura 86 prakṛtika, ye do sattāsthāna nahīṃ hote haiṃ kintu mithyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna sambandhī śeṣa sattāsthāna sambhava hone se ukta pā~ca sattāsthāna kahe haiṃ| ___ isa prakāra se sāta aparyāpta jīvasthānoṃ meṃ nāmakarma ke baṃdhasthāna, udayasthāna aura sattāsthāna jānanā cāhiye| aba isake anantara paṇa
1 kevalamaparyāptasaṃjñinaścatvāraḥ, yato dvau maṃgāvaparyāptasaṃjñinastiraścaḥ prādhyete, dvau vāparyāptamaṃ jino manuṣyasyeti |

sāptatikā prakaraṇa
isa prakāra ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha huā ki 21 aura 24 prakṛtika meṃ se pratyeka udayasthāna meṃ to pāṃca-pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ aura 25 va 26 prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ eka apekṣā se cāra-cāra aura eka apekṣā se pāṃca-pā~ca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | apekṣā kā kāraṇa sādhāraṇa va pratyeka prakṛti hai| jisakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa ūpara kiyā gayā hai|
aba gāthā meṃ nirdiṣṭa kramānusāra bādara ekendriya paryāpta jīvasthāna meṃ baṃdhādi sthānoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ ki 'paṇagā havaṃti tinneva' kā sambandha "bāyarā” se joḍa़eṃ | jisakā artha yaha huā ki bādara endriya paryāpta jīvasthāna meṃ pā~ca baṃdhasthāna, pā~ca udayasthāna aura pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | jinakā vivaraṇa nīce ligne anusāra hai--
bādara ekendriya paryāpta nī bhī manumati aura hināti meṃ yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai| isalie usane bhī 23, 25, 26, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika, ye pā~ca baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ aura tadanusāra inake kula bhaṃga 13617 hote haiṃ|
udayasthānoṃ kī apekṣā vicāra karane para yahā~ para bhī ekendriya sambandhī pā~ca udayasthāna 21, 24, 25, 26 aura 27 prakṛtika hote haiṃ | kyoṃki sāmānya se apāntarāla gati kī apekṣā 21 prakṛtika, śarīrastha hone kī apekṣā 24 prakṛtika, śarīra paryāpti se paryāpta hone kī apekṣā 25 prakṛtika aura prāṇāpāna paryāpti se paryāpta hone kī apekṣā 26 prakṛtika, ye cāra udayasthāna to paryāpta ekendriya ko niyama se hote hī haiṃ| kintu yaha bādara ekendriya hai ata: yahā~ ātapa aura udyota nāma meṃ se kisī eka kā udayasthāna aura saṃbhava hai, jisase 27 prakṛtika udayasthāna bhī bana jātā hai| isīliye bādara ekendriya paryāpta jīvasthāna meṃ 21, 24, 25, 26 aura 27 prakṛtika, ye pā~ca udayasthāna māne gaye haiṃ|

235 bādara ekendriya paryāpta jīvasthāna ke 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ 61 prakṛtiyā~ isa prakāra haiṃ-tiryaṃcagati, tirya cānupūrvī, ekendriya jāti, sthāvara, bādara, paryāpta, tejasa, kārmaṇa, agurulaghu, sthira, asthira, śubha, aśubha, varṇacatuṣka, nirmāṇa, dubhaṃga, anādeya, yaśaḥkīti aura ayaśa:kīrti meṃ se koī eka | isa udayasthāna meṃ yaśa:kīti aura ayaśaḥkīrti kā udaya vikalpa se hotā hai| ata: isa apekṣā se yahā~ 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke do bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
ukta 21 prakṛtikā upacana meṃ śarīrasya jīva kī apekṣā audārika śarīra, huṃḍasaṃsthāna, upaghāta tathā pratyeka aura sādhāraṇa meṃ se koī eka, ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne tathā tiryaṃcānupūrvī ko kama karane para 24 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yahā~ pratyeka-sādhāraṇa aura yaśa kīti-ayaśaḥkīti kā vikalpa se udaya hone ke kāraṇa cāra bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki śarīrastha vikriyā karane vāle bādara vāyukāyika jīvoṃ ke sādhāraṇa aura yaśaḥkīti nāmakarma kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai, isaliye vahā~ eka hī bhaṃga hotā hai| dūsarī viśeṣatā yaha hai ki aise jīvoṃ ke audārika śarīra kā udaya na hokara vaikiya śarīra kā udaya hotā hai, ataḥ inake audārika śarīra ke sthāna para baiṃkriya śarīra kahanā cāhie|' isa prakāra 24 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ kula pāṃca bhaṃga hue|
anantara 24 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ parāghāta prakṛti ko milāne se 25 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yaha udayasthāna śarīra paryāpti se

udayaṃ nāgacchataḥ, anyacca vaikiyavāyukāyikacatuciyātāyaudārikaśarīrasthāne daikriyaśarīramiti vaktavyam |

paryāpta hue jīva ko hotā hai| yahā~ bhī 24 prakṛtika udayasthāna kī taraha pā~ca bhaṅga hote haiṃ |

237
vālā eka-eka bhaṅga, isa prakāra ina āṭha bhaṅgoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ uparyukta pāṃcoṃ sattāsthāna hote haiṃ kintu śeṣa 21 meṃ se pratyeka bhaṅga meṃ 78 prakṛtika sattāsthāna ko choḍa़kara śeṣa cāra-cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ |
aba gāthā meṃ kiye gaye nirdeśānusāra paryāpta vikalendriyoṃ meṃ baṃdhādi sthānoṃ aura unake yathāsambhava bhaṅgoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ| gāthāoṃ meṃ nirdeśa hai 'paṇa paṇagaṃ vigaliṃdiyā u tini u' | arthāt vikalatrika - - dvīndriya, śrīndriya aura caturindriya paryāptoṃ meṃ pā~ca baṃdhasthāna, chaha udayasthāna aura pā~ca sattāsthāna haiṃ| jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa isa prakāra hai ki - vikalendriya paryāpta jīva bhī tiryaṃcagati aura manuṣyagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha karate haiṃ| ataḥ inake bhī 23, 25, 26, 26 aura 30 prakṛtika, ye pā~ca baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ aura tadanusāra inake kula bhaṅga 13917 hote haiṃ|
udayasthānoṃ kī apekṣā vicāra karane para yahā~ 21, 26, 28,26, 30 aura 31 prakṛtika, ye chaha udayasthāna hote haiṃ| inameṃ se 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ - tejasa, kārmaṇa, agurulaghu, sthira, asthira, śubha, aśubha, varṇacatuṣka, nirmāṇa, tiryaṃcagati, tiryaṃcānupūrvī, dvīndriya jāti, sa bādara, paryāpta, durbhaga, anādeya aura yaśaḥ kīrti va ayaśa: kīrti meṃ se koī eka - isa prakāra 21 prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai jo apāntarāla gati meṃ pāyā jātā hai| isake yaśaḥkīrti aura ayaśaḥkīrti ke vikalpa se do bhajja hote haiṃ |
anantara śarīrastha jīva kī apekṣā 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ audārika śarīra, audārika aṃgopāṃga, iṃḍasaṃsthāna, sevārta saṃhanana, upaghāta aura pratyeka ina chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne tathā tiryacānupūrvī ko kama karane se 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yahā~ bhī 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna kī taraha do bhaṅga jānanā cāhiye |

saptatikā prakaraṇa
isa 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ śarīra paryāpti se paryāpta hue jīva kī apekṣā parāghāta aura apraśasta vihāyogati, ina do prakṛtiyoṃ ko milāne para 28 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | yahāṃ bhī pūrvavat do bhaṅga hote haiṃ|
28 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke anantara 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna kā krama hai | yaha 26 prakutika udayasthāna do prakāra se hotā hai - eka to jisane prāṇāpāna paryāpti ko prāpta kara liyā hai, usake udyota ke binā kevala ucchvāsa kā udaya hone para aura dūsarā śarīra paryāpti kī prāpti hone ke paścāt udyola kā udaya hone para| ina donoṃ meṃ se pratyeka sthāna meṃ pūrvokta do-do bhaṅga prāpta hote haiṃ | isa prakāra 20 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kula cāra bhaṅga hue|
isī prakāra 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna bhī do prakāra se prāpta hotā hai| eka to jisane bhāṣā paryāpti ko prāpta kara liyā hai, usake udyota kā udaya na hokara susvara aura duḥsbara ina do prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se kisī eka kā udaya hone para hotā hai aura dūsarā jisane śvāsocchvāsa paryāpti ko prāpta kiyā aura abhī bhāṣā paryāpti kī prāpti nahīṃ huī kintu isī bīca meṃ usake udyota prakṛti kā udaya ho gayā to bhī 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna ho jātā hai| inameṃ se pahale prakāra ke 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ yaśa:kīrti aura ayaśa:kīti tathā susvara aura duḥsvara ke vikalpa se cāra bhaṅga prāpta hote haiṃ| kintu dūsare prakāra ke 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ yaśaḥkīti aura ayaśa kīrti ke vikalpa se do hī bhaṅga hote haiṃ| isa prakāra 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ chaha bhaṅga prāpta hue| 1 tataḥ prāṇāpānaparyāpyā paryāptasyocchavāse kṣipte ekotriśat, atrāṣi
tāvena dvau maṅgī; athavā tasyāmevāṣṭā viśatī ucchvāse'nudite udyotanāmni tūdite ekonatriṃśat | ---saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ. 203

236
ūpara jo 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke do prakāra batalāye haiṃ usameṃ se yadi jisane bhāṣā paryāpti ko bhī prāpta kara liyā aura uyota kā bhī udaya hai, usako 31 prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| yahā~ yaśa:kīti aura ayaśaḥkīti tathā donoṃ svaroṃ ke vikalpa se cāra bhaṅga hote haiṃ| isa prakāra paryāpta dvīndriya ke saba udayasthānoṃ ke kula bhaṅga 20 hote haiṃ|
dvīndriya paryāpta jīvasthāna meṃ bhī ekendriya ke samāna 62, 88, 86, 80 aura 78 prakṛtika, ye pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| pahale jo chaha udayasthānoṃ ke 20 bhaṅga batalāye haiṃ unameṃ se 21 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke do bhaṅga tathā 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna ke do bhaṅga, ina cāra bhaṅgoṃ meṃ se pratyeka bhaṅga meṃ pāṃca-pāṃca sattāsthāna hote haiṃ kyoṃki 78 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā vāle jo agnikāyika aura vāyukāyika jīva paryāpta dvīndriyoṃ meṃ utpanna hote haiṃ, unake kucha kāla taka 78 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā saṃbhava hai tathā isa kāla meṃ dvīndriyoṃ ke kramaśa: 21 aura 26 prakṛtika udayasthāna hī hote haiṃ| isīliye ina do udayasthānoṃ ke cāra bhaṅgoṃ meṃ se pratyeka bhaṅga meṃ ukta pāṃca sattāsthāna ka.he haiṃ tathā ina cāra bhaṅgoṃ ke atirikta jo śeṣa 16 bhaṅga raha jāte haiṃ, unameṃ se kisī meṃ bhī 78 prakṛtika sattāsthāna na hone se pratyeka meṃ cāra-cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| kyoṃki agnikāyika aura vāyukāyika jīvoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa jīva śarīra paryāpti se paryāpta hone ke paścāt niyama se manuṣyagati aura manuṣyānupūrvī kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, jisase unake 789 prakṛtika sattāsthāna nahīṃ pāyā jātā hai|
paryāpta dvīndriya jīvoṃ kī taraha trīndriya aura caturindriya paryāpta jīvoṃ ko bhī baṃdhādi sthānoṃ aura unake bhaṅgoṃ ko jānanā cāhiye | itanī viśeṣatā jānanā cāhiye ki udayasthānoṃ meṃ dvīndriya ke sthāna para trīndriya aura caturindriya kā ullekha kara diyā jāye|

saptatikā prakaraṇa
isa prakāra se jīvasthānoṃ meṃ āṭha karmoṃ kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha, udaya va sattā sthāna tathā unake bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana karane ke bāda aba guṇasthānoṃ meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana karate haiṃ |
guṇasthānoṃ meṃ saṃvedha bhaṃga
sarvaprathama guṇasthānoṃ meṃ jñānāvaraṇa aura antarāya karma ke baṃdhādi sthānoṃ kā kathana karate haiṃ
nāṇaṃtarāya tivimayi dasasu vo hoṃti dosu ṭhāṇesuṃ |
śabdārtha - nāṇaṃta rāya - jñānāvaraṇa aura antarādha karma, siṃhimavi-tīna prakāra se (baṃdha, udaya aura sattā kī apekṣā ), basasu ādi ke dasa guṇasthānoṃ meṃ do-do (udaya aura sattā ), hoṃtihotā hai, do-do (upaśāṃtamoha aura kṣīṇamoha meṃ ), ṭhāṇesuṃ— guṇasthānoṃ meṃ |
-
gāthārtha - prārambha ke dasa guṇasthānoṃ meṃ jñānāvaraṇa aura antarāya karma bandha, udaya aura sattā kī apekṣā tīna prakāra kā hai aura do guṇasthānoṃ (upaśāṃtamoha, kṣīṇamoha) meṃ udaya aura sattā kī apekṣā do prakāra kā hai |
viśeṣārtha - pūrva meṃ caudaha jīvasthānoṃ meṃ āṭha karmoṃ ke baṃdha, udaya aura sattā sthāna tathā unake saṃvedha bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā| aba guṇasthānoṃ meṃ unakā kathana karate haiṃ |
jñānāvaraṇa aura antarāya karma ke bāre meṃ yaha niyama hai ki jñānāvaraṇa kī pāṃcoṃ aura antarāya kī pā~coṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandhaviccheda dasaveṃ sūkṣma saṃparāya guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ tathā udaya aura sattā kā viccheda bārahaveṃ kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ hotā hai | ataeva isase yaha siddha ho jātā hai ki pahale mithyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna se lekara dasaveṃ guṇasthāna taka dasa guṇasthānoṃ meṃ jñānāvaraṇa aura antarāya karma ke pā~ca

THE prakaraṇa
apūrvakaraṇa ādi tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ, upaṇa—cāra athavā pā~ca, navaṃsa-nau ko satā, budhu do guṇasthānoṃ (anivṛttibāvara aura sukṣmasaṃparāya) meṃ, jupala-baṃdha aura udaya, chassaṃtā-chaha kī sattā|
unasate-upaśāṃtamoha guṇasthāna meṃ, cau paṇa-cāra athavā pāṃca, nava-nī, khoṇe- kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ, ghasarūma-cāra kā udaya, chanaca uchaha aura cāra kī, saṃtaṃ- sattā | ___ gāṇārya-dūsare darśanāvaraṇa karma kā mithyātva aura sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ nau prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha, cāra yā pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya tathā nau prakṛti kī sattā hotī hai|
miśra guṇasthāna se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna ke pahale saṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga taka chaha kā baṃdha, cāra thā pā~ca kā udraya aura nau kī sattā hotī hai| apūrvakaraṇa ādi tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ cāra kā baṃdha, cāra thā pani kā udaya aura nau kī sattā hotī hai| kṣapaka ke nau aura dasa ina do guṇasthānoṃ meṃ cāra kā baṃdha, cāra kā udaya aura chaha kī sattā hotī hai|
upaśāṃtamoha guṇasthāna meṃ cāra yā pāṃca kā udaya aura nau kī sattā hotī hai | kṣīṇabhoha guṇasthāna meṃ cāra kā udaya tathā chaha aura cāra kī sattā hotī hai|'
1 (ka) micchā sāsayaṇesu naba baṃdha valavistrayā meṃ do maṃgā |
mīsāo ya liyaṭṭī jā chabbaṃdheṇa do do u|| ca ubaṃdhe navasaṃte chogiṇa apurabāu suhamarāgo jā | abbaṃdhe ṇava saṃte vayasate huti ho maṃgā || caubaṃdhe chassase bāyara suhumāṇameguṣasva vayāṇaṃ | chasu ca umu va saṃtesu doṇi abadhaṃmi kha़īṇarasa ||
