257
viśeṣārtha- ina gāthāoṃ meṃ guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā darśanāvaraṇa karma kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha, udaya aura sattā sthānoṃ kā nirdeśa kiyā pāyā hai|
darśanāvaraṇa karma kī uttara prakṛtiyā~ haiṃ haiṃ| inameṃ se satyāddhitrika kā baṃdha sāsādana guṇasthāna taka hī hotā hai tathā cakṣurdarśanāvaraṇa ādi cāra kā udaya apane udayaviccheda hone taka niraṃtara banā rahatā hai kintu nidrā ādi pāṃca kā udaya kadācita hotā hai aura kadācita nahīṃ hotā hai tathā usameṃ bhī eka samaya meṃ eka kā hī udaya hotā hai, eka sātha do kā yā do se adhika kā nahīṃ hotā hai| isīliye mithyātva aura sāsādana ina do guṇasthānoṃ meṃ prakṛtika baṃdha, 4 prakṛtika udaya aura 6 prakṛtika sattā tathā 6 prakṛtika baṃdha, 5 prakṛtika udaya aura prakṛtika rānā, no aṃga na hote haiṃ .-'micchāsāṇe biie nava cau paṇa nava ya saṃtasā |' ___ ina do-mithyātna aura sāsādana guṇasthānoṃ ke āge tīsare miśra guṇasthāna se lekara āṭhaveṃ apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna ke prathama bhāga taka'bhissāi niyaṭṭīo chacau paṇa naba ya saṃtakammaṃsā'---chaha kā baṃdha, cāra yā pāṃca kā udaya aura nau kī sattā hotī hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki styāddhitrika kā baṃdha sāsādana guṇasthāna taka hone se chaha praśika baṃdha hotā hai | kintu udaya aura sattā prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ koī aṃtara nahīṃ paḍa़tā hai | ataḥ ina guṇasthānoṃ meṃ chaha prakṛtika baṃdha, cāra prakṛtika
khīṇo tti cāri ukṣyā paṃcasu giddāsu dosu ṇihāsu | ekke udayaṃ patte khīṇaducarimoti paṃcudayā || micyāduvasaṃto ti ya aṇiyaṭṭī khaga paṭhamamāgotti | pavasattā vīṇassa ducarimosi ya chaccadūvarime ||

saptatikā prakaraga
udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā tathā chaha prakatika baṃdha. pāṃca prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā, ye do bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ| yadyapi styānaddhitrikā kā udaya pramattasaṃgata guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya taka hī ho sakatā hai, phira bhī isase pā~ca prakṛtika udayasthāna ke kathana meṃ koī aṃtara nahīṃ ātā hai, sirpha vikalpa rūpa prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ hī aṃtara paḍa़tā hai | chaṭhe guṇasthāna taka nidrā ādi pāṃcoṃ prakṛtiyāṃ vikalā se prāpta hotī haiṃ, āge nidrā aura pracalā ye do prakṛtiyā~ hī vikalpa se prāpta hotī haiṃ| ___ apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna ke prathama bhāga meṃ nidrā aura prabalā kī bhī baṃdhayucchitti ho jāne se āge sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna paryanta tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ baṃdha meṃ cāra prakṛtiyā~ raha jātī haiṃ, kintu udaya aura sattā pūrvavat prakṛtiyoṃ kī rahatī hai| ataḥ apūrvakaraṇa ke dūsare bhāga se lekara sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna taka tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ cāra prakṛtika baṃdha, cāra prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika satā tathā cāra prakṛtika baṃdha, pā~ca prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā, yaha do bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ--'caubaṃdha tige cau paṇa navaṃsa' |
lekina ukta kathana upaśamaśreṇi kī apekṣā samajhanā cāhiye, kyoṃki aisā niyama hai ki nidrā yā pracalā kā udaya upaśamaśreṇi meṃ hī hotā hai, kṣapakaveṇi meṃ nahīṃ hotā hai | ataḥ kṣapaka śreṇi meṃ apūrvakaraṇa ādi tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ pāṃca prakṛtika udaya rūpa bhaṅga prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai tathā anivattikaraṇa ke kucha bhāgoṃ ke vyatīta hone para styānaddhitrika kī sattā kā kṣaya ho jātā hai| jisase chaha prakṛtiyoṃ kī hī sattā rahatī hai| ata: anivṛttikaraṇa ke aṃtima saṃkhyāta bhāga aura sukṣamasapagaya ina do kṣapaka guṇasthānoṃ meṃ cāra prakṛtika baṃdha, cāra prakṛtika udaya aura chaha prakṛtika sattā, yaha eka bhaṅga prāpta hotā hai---'dusa juyala darasaṃtā'|

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
256
__ upaśamaśreṇi yā kṣapaka śreṇi vāle ke dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ darśanāvaraṇa kama kā baṃdhaviccheda ho jātā hai| isaliye āge gyārahaveṃ ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ baṃdha kī apekṣā darśanāvaraṇa ke bhaṃga prāpta nahīṃ hote haiṃ | ataḥ upaśāṃtamoha guṇasthāna meṃ jo upaśamaśreṇi kā guṇasthāna hai, udaya aura sattā to dasaveṃ guṇasthāna ke samāna banī rahatī hai kintu baṃdha nahīṃ hone se–'uvasaṃte caupaṇa nava'-cāra prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā tathā pāṃca prakṛtika udaya aura nau prakṛtika sattā, yaha do bhaṅga prāpta hote haiṃ|
kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ---‘khīṇe urudaya chacca causaṃta'-cāra kā udaya' aura chaha yā cāra kī sattā hotī hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki bārahavāṃ kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna kṣayakaśreṇi kā hai aura āpaka śreṇi meṃ nidrā yā pracalā kā udaya nahīṃ hone se cāra prakṛtika udayasthāna prāpta hotā hai tathā chaha yā cāra prakṛtika sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | kyoṃki jaba kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ nidrā aura pracalā kā udaya hī nahīṃ hotā hai taba kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ inakī sattā bhī prāpta nahīṃ ho sakatī hai aura niyamānusāra anudaya prakṛtiyā~ jo hotī haiṃ, unakā pratyeka niṣeka stibukāsaṃkramaṇa ke dvārā sajātīya udayavatī prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ pariṇama jātā hai, jisase kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ nidrā aura pracalā kī sattā na rahakara kevala cakṣudaṃrśanāvaraṇa ādi dhāra ko hī sattā rahegī| isakā tātparya yaha huā ki kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ jo cāra prakṛtika udaya aura chaha prakṛtika sattā tathā cāra prakṛtika udaya aura cāra prakṛtika sattā, ina do bhaṅgoṃ meṃ se pahalā bhaṅga cāra prakṛtika udaya aura chaha prakṛtika sattā kā kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna ke upāntya samaya taka jānanā cāhiye aura aṃtima samaya meṃ cāra prakṛtika udaya aura cāra prakṛtika sattā

saptatikā prakaraṇa
kā dūsarā bhaṅga prāpta hotā hai | isa prakāra kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ bhī do bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ|
isa prakāra se jñānāvaraṇa, aṃtarāya aura darśanāvaraṇa karma kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ baṃdha, udaya aura sattā sthānoṃ ko batalāne ke bāda aba vedanīya, āyu aura gotra karmoṃ ke bhaṃgoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ| beyaṇiyāuyagoe vibhajja mohaṃ para boccha ||41||
śabdārtha-veṇiyāuyagoe-vedanīya, āyu aura gotra karma ke, vibhāja-vibhāga karake, mojha--mohanīya karma ke, para- isake bāda, bodha-kaheṃge|
gāthā-bedanīya, āyu aura gotra karma ke bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana karane ke bāda mohanīya karma ke bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana kareṃge |
oivā-gāthā meṃ vedanīya, āyu aura gotra karma ke bhaṃgoṃ ke vibhāga karane kī sūcanā dī hai kintu unake kitane-kitama bhaṃga hote haiṃ yaha nahīṃ batalāyā hai | ata: ācārya pralayagiri kī ṭīkā meṃ bhāṣya kī gāthāoṃ ke ādhāra para vedanīya, āyu aura gotra karma ke jo bhaṃgavikalpa batalāye haiṃ, unako yahā~ spaṣṭa karate haiṃ|
bhāṣya kī gāthā meṃ vedanīya aura gotra karma ke bhaṅgoṃ kā nirdeśa isa prakāra kiyā gayā hai
para ghassu voṇi sattasu ege ghau gugisu śreṇiyabhaṃgā|
goe paṇa ghau thī timu emajyū boṇi ekasmi || arthāt vedanīya karma ke chaha guṇasthānoṃ meṃ cāra, sāta meṃ do aura eka meṃ cāra bhaṅga hote haiṃ tathā gotrakarma ke pahale meṃ pā~ca, dūsare meṃ cāra, tīsare ādi tīna meṃ do, chaṭhe ādi āṭha meṃ eka aura eka meṃ eka bhaṅga hotā hai jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jātā hai |

saptatikā prakaraṇa sthānoṃ ke vikalpoṃ ko batalāne ke bāda aba caudahaveṃ guṇasthāna ke bhaṅgoṃ ko batalāne ke liye kahate haiṃ ki 'ege cau' arthāt eka guṇasthāna-.-caudahaveṃ ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ cāra bhaṅga hote haiṃ| kyoṃki ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ sātā vedanīya kā bhī baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai, ataḥ vahāṃ baṃdha kī apekṣā to koī bhaṅga prāpta nahīṃ hotā hai kintu udaya aura sattā kī apekṣā bhaṅga banate haiṃ| phira bhī jisake isa guṇasthāna meṃ asātā kā udaya hai, usake upāntya samaya meṃ sātā kī sattā kā nāśa ho jāne se tathā jisake sātā kā udaya hai usake upāntya samaya meṃ asātā kī sattā kā nāśa ho jāne se upāntya samaya taka-1. sātā kā udaya aura sātā-asāsā kāsagī, 2. atātā| kā udaya aura sātā-asātā kī sattā, ye do bhaṅga prāpta hote haiṃ| tathā aṃtima samaya meṃ, 3. sātā kā udaya aura sātā kī sattā tathā 4. asātā kā udaya aura asātā kī sattā, yaha do bhaṅga prāpta hote haiṃ| isa prakāra ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ vedanīya karma ke cāra bhaṃga banate haiṃ|

264
māptatikā prakaraṇa guṇasthānoṃ meṃ eka uccagotra kā hī baṃdha hotā hai| isakā yaha artha huā ki mithyātva śudhasthāna ke samāna sārāādana muṇasthāna meṃ bhī kisī eka kā baṃdha kisī eka kā udaya aura donoṃ kī sattā bana jātī hai| isa hisāba se yahā~ cāra bhaṃga pāye jāte haiṃ aura ve cāra bhāga vahī haiṃ jinakā mithyātva guṇasthāna ke bhaṃga 1, 2, 2 aura 4 meṃ ullekha triyā gayā hai|
"do tisu' arthāta tīsare, cauthe, pāṃcave-mitha, avirata samyagdṛṣṭi aura deśavirati guṇasthānoṃ meṃ do mā hote haiṃ| goṃki go ge lekara sarāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna taka baṃdha eka ucca gotra kā hī hotā hai kintu udaya aura sattā donoṃ kī pāī jātī hai | isaliye ina tīna guṇasthānoṃ meṃ-- 1. ucca kā badha, uttaca kā udaya aura ucca-nīca kī sattā, tathā 2 lalaca kā baṃdha, nīca kā udaya aura nīca-ucca kī sattā, yaha do bhaṃga pāye jāte haiṃ| yahāṃ kitane hī ācāryoṃ kā yaha bhī abhigata hai ki pāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ ucca kā baṃdha, uca kā udaya aura raca-nīca kī sanā yahī eka aṃga hotā hai| isa viṣaya meṃ āgaga bacana hai ki---
sāmammeṇaṃ ayajāīe upacāgopassa ubalo hoi | arthāt--sāmānya se saṃyata aura saṃyatāsaṃgrata jāti vāle jīvoṃ ke ucca' gotra kā udaya hotā hai |
'ega'vasu'-yānī chaṭhe pramatasaṃyata gupārathāna se lekara māra guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka guṇasthāna meṃ eka bhaṃga prāpta hotā hai | kyoṃki chaṭhe se lekara dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna taka hī ucca gotra kā baṃdha hotā hai | ataḥ chaṭhe, sātaveṃ, āṭhane, nau, dasaveṃ--pramatasaṃyata, apramattasaṃyata, apūrvaka raṇa, anivṛtti bādara aura mukṣmasaṃparāya -- guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ ucca kā baṃdha, ucca kā udaya aura ucca

﻿________________

bāṭa karmagrandha
265 nīva kī sattā yaha eka bhaṃga prāpta hotā hai tathā dasaveṃ gaṇasthāna meṃ ucca gotra kā baṃdhaviccheda ho jāne se gyārahavaṃ. bārahavaṃ, tarahaupaśāntamoha, kṣīṇamoha aura sayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ uccagotra kā udaya aura ucca-nīca kī sattā, yaha eka bhaṃga prāpta hotā hai| isa prakāra chaṭhe se lekara terahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka pratyeka guṇasthāna meṃ eka bhaṃga prāpta hotā hai, yaha siddha huā |
'doṇi ekkammi'-śeṣa rahe eka caudahaveṃ ayogikevalī muṇasthāna bhe do bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ nīca gotra kī sattā upāntya samaya taka hī hotī hai kyoṃki caudahave guguṇasthāna meṃ yaha udayarūpa prakṛti na hone se upāntya samaya meṃ hī isakā stibuka saṃkramaṇa ke dvārā ucca gotra rūpa se pariṇamana ho jātā hai, ata: isa guṇasthāna ke upāntya samaya taka ucca kā udaya aura uccanīva kī sattā, yaha eka bhaṃga tathā anta samaya meṃ ucca kā udaya aura ucca kī sattā, yaha dūsarā bhaṃga hotā hai| isa prakāra caudahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ do bhaṃgoṃ kā vidhāna jānanā cāhie|
guṇasthānoṃ meṃ vedanīya aura gotra karmoṃ ke bhaṃgoṃ kā vivacana karane ke bāda aba āyukarma ke bhaṃgoṃ kā vicāra bhāṣya gāthā ke ādhāra se karate haiṃ| isa sambandhī gāthā nimna prakāra hai
acchāhiāvīsā solasa cīsaṃ ca bāra gha doṣu |
vo causu tītu ekkaṃ minchāisu bāuge bhaṃgā arthāt mithyātva guṇasthāna meṃ 28, sāsādana meṃ 26, miśra meṃ 16, avirata samyagdṛṣṭi meṃ 20, deśavirata meṃ 12, pramatta aura apramatta meṃ 6, apūrvakaraṇa ādi cāra meṃ 2 aura kṣīṇamoha ādi meṃ 1, isa prakāra mithyādṛṣṭi ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ āyukarma ke bhaṃga jānanā cāhie| jinakā viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa nimna prakāra hai-..

sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ 26 bhaṃga hote haiṃ| kyoṃki narakāyu kā baṃdha mithyātva guṇasthāna meṃ hī hone se sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi tiryaṃca aura manuṣya narakāvu kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ| ataḥ uparyukta 28 bhaṃgoṃ meṃ se1 bhujyamāna tiryaṃcāyu, badhyamāna narakāyu aura tirthaṃca narakāyu kī sattā, tathā bhujyamāna manuṣyāyu badhyamāna narakāyu aura manuṣya- narakāyu kī sattā, ye do bhaṃga kama hone jāne se sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ 26 bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ|"

deśavirata guṇasthāna meṃ 12 bhaṃga hote hai | kyoṃki deśavirati tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ ke hotī hai aura yadi ve parabhava sambandhī āyu kā bandha karate haiṃ to devāyu kā hī bandha karate haiṃ anya āyu kā nahīṃ| deśaviratā āyurvapnanto devāyureya badhnanti na śeṣamāyuḥ | ata: inake āyubandha ke pahale eka-eka hī bhaṃga hotā hai aura ājubandha ke kāla meṃ bhī eka-eka bhaṃga hī hotā hai| isa prakāra tiyaca aura manuṣyoṃ, donoṃ ko milākara kula cāra bhaṃga hue tathā uparata baṃdha kī apekṣā tiryaṃcoṃ ke bhī cāra bhaṃga hote haiṃ aura manuṣyoṃ ke bhī cāra bhaṃga | kyoṃki cāroṃ gati sambandhī āyu kā vadha karane ke paścāta tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ ke deśavirati guṇasthāna ke prāpta hone meṃ kisī prakāra kā virodha nahīṃ hai| isa prakāra uparata baṃdha kī apekṣā tithaṃcoṃ ke cāra aura manuṣyoṃ ke cāra, jo kula milākara āṭha bhaṅga haiṃ| inameṃ pūrvokta cāra bhaṅgoṃ ko milāne para deśavirata guṇasthāna meṃ kula bāraha bhaṅga ho jāte haiṃ |

yadyapi gāthā 11 meṃ mohanīyakarma ke udayasthānoṃ kī sāmānya vivecanā ke prasaṃga meṃ viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa kiyā jā cukā hai, phira bhī guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā unakā kathana karane ke lie gāthānusāra yahā~ vivecana karate haiṃ|
'sanāda damana minṭe' arthāt pahale miśyāiṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ 7, 8, 6 aura 10 prakṛtika, ye cāra udayasthāna hote haiṃ| mithyātva, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa, saṃjvalana, krodhādi meṃ se anyatama tīna krodhādi, tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī eka veda, hāsya-rati yugala, śokaarati yugala meṃ se koī eka yugala, ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā dhruva rūpa se udaya hone se sāta prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai | ina dhruvodayā sāta prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhaya athavā jugupsā athavā anantānubaṃdhī kaṣāya catuṣka meṃ se kisī eka kaṣāya ko milāne para āṭha prakṛtika tathā una sāta prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhaya, jugupsā athavā bhaya, anantānubaṃdhī athavā jugupsā, anantānubaṃdhī meṃ se kinhīṃ do ko milāne se nau prakṛtika aura ukta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhaya, jugupsā aura anantānubandhī anyatama eka kaṣāya ko eka sātha milāne para dasa prakṛtika udayasthāna hotā hai| ina cāra udayasthānoṃ meṃ sāta kī eka, āṭha kī tīna, nau kī tīna aura dasa kī eka, isa prakāra bhaṃgoṃ kī āṭha caubīsī prāpta hotī haiṃ|

baṣṭha karmagrantha
281
chaha prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bhī bhaṃgoṃ kī gyāraha caubīsī isa prakāra hai- avirata samyagdṛṣṭi aura apūrvakaraṇa meṃ eka-eka tathā deśavirata, pramattavirata, apramattavirata meṃ tīna-tīna inakā joḍa़ kula gyāraha hotā hai | pā~ca prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī no caubīsī haiṃ| unameṃ se deśabirata meṃ eka, pramatta aura apramatta virata guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ tīna-tīna aura apūrvakaraṇa meṃ do caubīsī hotī haiṃ| cāra prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ pramattavirata, apramattavirata aura apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī eka-eka caubīsī hone se kula tīna caubīsī hotī haiṃ | ina saboṃ kī śuci hotī haiṃ tathā do prakṛtika udayasthāna ke bāraha aura eka prakṛtika udayasthāna ke pā~ca bhaṃga haiṃ— 'bāra durge paṃca ekkammi' jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa pūrva gāthā ke saṃdarbha meṃ kiyā jā cukā hai|
isa prakāra dasa se lekara eka prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ meṃ kula milākara 52 caubīsī aura 17 bhaṃga prāpta hote haiṃ| jinakā guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā antarbhāṣya gāthā meṃ nimna prakāra se vivecana kiyā gayā haiaṭṭaga ca ca cauraṭṭhagā ya cauro ya hoṃti ghaḍavīsā | apuṣavaṃtā vārasa paṇa ca aniyaṭṭe ||
micchā arthāt mithyādṛṣṭi se lekara apūrvakaraṇa taka āṭha guṇasthānoṃ meṃ bhaṃgoṃ kī krama se āṭha, cāra, cāra, āṭha, āṭha āṭha, āṭha, aura cāra cauvīsī hotī haiṃ tathā anivṛttibādara guṇasthāna meṃ bāraha aura pā~ca bhaṃga hote haiṃ |
isa prakāra bhaṃgoṃ ke prāpta hone para kula milākara 1265 udaya vikalpa hote haiṃ, ve isa prakāra samajhanā cāhiye ki 52 caubīsiyoṃ kī kula saṃkhyā 5248 (52 x 24 = 1248 ) aura isameṃ anivṛttibādara guṇasthāna ke 17 maṃgoṃ ko milā dene para 1248 + 17= 1265 saṃkhyā hotī hai tathā 10 se lekara 4 prakṛtika udayasthānoṃ taka ke saba pada 352 hote haiṃ, ata: inheṃ 24 se guṇita karane dene para 8448 prāpta hote

aba yadi yogoṃ kī apekṣā dasoṃ guṇasthānoṃ ke padavṛnda lānā cāheṃ to do bātoṃ para dhyāna denā hogā - 1. kisa guṇasthāna meṃ padavṛnda aura yogoṃ kī saṃkhyā kitanī hai aura 2 una yogoṃ meṃ se kisa yoga meṃ kitane padavṛnda sambhava hai| inhīṃ do bātoṃ ko dhyāna meṃ rakhakara aba yogāpekṣā guṇasthānoṃ ke padavṛnda batalāte haiṃ |

ke bhaṅga kama kara denā cāhiye | isakā tātparya yaha huā ki 13 yogoṃ kī apekṣā 12 se 32 ko guṇita karake 24 se guṇita kareṃ aura vaikriyamiśra kī apekṣā 32 ko 16 se guṇita kareṃ| isa prakāra 12432 = 354424-- 6216 tathā naikiyamiśra ke 324 16-- 512 hue aura ina 6216 aura 512 kā kula joḍa़ 1728 hotā hai | yahī 1728 padavṛnda sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ hote haiṃ|

Nṣṭa pharmagrantha
263 __ pramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna meṃ yoga 13 aura pada 44 haiṃ kintu āhārakadvika meṃ strīveda kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai, isaliye 11 yogoṃ kī apekṣā to 11 ko 44 se guṇita karake 24 se guṇita karane se 11444 484424=11616 hue aura āhārakadvika kī apekṣā 2 se 44 ko guṇita karake 16 se guṇita kareṃ to 2444 =88416-1408 hue | taba 11616+ 1408 ko joḍa़ne para kula 13024 padavṛnda pramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna meṃ prāpta hote haiṃ |
apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna meṃ bhī yoga 11 aura pada 44 haiṃ, kintu āhāraka kāyayoga meṃ strīveda kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai| isaliye 10 yogoṃ kī apekṣā 10 se 44 ko guṇita karake 24 se guṇita kareṃ aura āhāraka kāyayoga kī apekṣā 44 se 16 ko guṇita kareṃ| isa prakāra karane para apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna meṃ kula padavṛnda 11264 hote haiṃ|
apūrvakaraṇa meṃ yoga aura pada 20 hota haiṃ | 20 ko saṇita karake 24 se guṇita karane para yahā~ kula padavṛnda 4320 prāpta hote haiṃ|
anivṛttibādara guṇasthāna meṃ yoga aura bhaṅga 28 haiṃ| yahā~ yoga pada nahīṃ hai ataḥ pada na kahakara bhaṅga kahe haiṃ| so ina 6 ko 28 se guṇita kara dene para anivṛttibādara meṃ 252 padavṛnda hote haiṃ tathā sūkṣmasaṃparāya meṃ yoga haiṃ aura bhaṅga hai, ataḥ 6 se 1 ko muṇita karane para bhaṅga hote haiṃ|
isa prakāra pahale se lekara dasaveṃ guṇasthāna taka ke padavṛndoṃ ko joḍa़ dene para saba padavṛndoṃ kī kula saṃkhyā 95717 hotī hai | kahā bhī hai

pāṭha karmagrantha
265 isa prakāra se kī apekṣā sunāvoṃ meṃ mohanīyaka ke udayasthānoṃ, bhaṃgoṃ aura padavṛndoṃ kā vicāra karane ke bāda aba āge upayogoṃ kī apekṣā udayasthānoṃ ādi kā vicāra karate haiṃ |
mithyātva aura sāsādana ina do guṇasthānoṃ meṃ matiajñāna, śrutaajñāna, vibhaṃgajñāna, cakṣudarśana aura acakṣudarśana, ye pāṃca upayoga hote haiṃ| miśra meṃ tīna miśra jñāna aura cakṣu va acakṣu darśana, isa prakāra ye pāṃca upayoga haiṃ| avirata samyagdṛṣṭi aura deśavirata meṃ ārambha ke tīna samyagjñāna aura tīna darśana, ye chaha upayoga hote haiṃ tathā chaṭhe pramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna se lekara dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna taka pāṃca guṇasthānoṃ meṃ pūrvokta chaha tathā manaparyāyajñāna sahita sāta upayoga hote haiṃ tathā pratyeka guṇasthāna ke udayasthāna ke bhaṃgoṃ kā kathana pūrva meṃ antarbhāṣya māthā 'aṭṭhaga cau cau cauraṭugā ya' ke saṃdarbha meṃ kiyā jā cukā hai| ata: jisa guṇasthāna meṃ jitane upayoga hoṃ, unase usa guṇasthāna ke udayasthānoṃ ko guṇita karake anantara bhaṃgoṃ se muṇita kara dene para upayogoṃ kī apekṣā usa guṇasthāna ke kula bhaṃga jñāta ho jāte hai| jaise ki mithyātva aura sāsādana meṃ kama se 8 aura 4 caubīsī tathā 5 upayoga haiṃ ataḥ 8+4=12 ko 5 se guṇita kara dene para 60 hue | mitha meṃ 4 caubīsī aura 5 upayoga haiṃ ataḥ 4 ko 5 se guṇita karane para 20 hue | avirata samyagdṛṣṭi aura deśavirata guṇasthāna meṃ āṭha-āṭha caubīsī aura 6 upayoga haiṃ ata: +8= 16 ko 6 se guṇita kara dene para 66 hue| pramatta, apramatta saṃyata aura apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ āṭha, āṭha aura cāra caubīsī tathā 7 upayoga haiṃ, ataḥ 8++4=20 ko sāta se guṇā kara dene para 140 hue tathā ina sabakā joḍa़ 60+20+66+140 =316 huā| inameṃ se pratyeka caubīsī meṃ 24, 24 bhaṃga hote haiṃ ata: ina 316 ko 24 se guṇita kara dene para kula 316 424 =7584 hote haiṃ tathā do prakṛtika udayasthāna

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
303
isa prakāra mohanīyakarma ke pratyeka guṇasthāna sambandhī udayasthāna vikalpa aura padavṛndoṃ tathā vahā~ sambhava yoga, upayoga aura leśyāoṃ se guṇita karane para unake prāpta pramāṇa ko batalāne ke bāda aba saṃvedha bhakoṃ kā kathana karane ke liye sattāsthānoṃ kā vicāra karate haiṃ |
guṇasthānoṃ meṃ mohanīyakarma ke saṃvedha bhaṅga
tiṇṇege egegaṃ tiga mose paṃca causu niyaṭṭie' tini | ekkāra bāyarammī suhame cau tini uvasaṃte || 48 ||

udayasthāna meṃ 62, 88, 86, aura 80 prakṛtika, ye cāra sattāsthāna haiṃ| jinako vikalendriya, liyaṃca paṃcendriya aura manuṣyoṃ kī apekṣā jānanā cāhiye | nārakoṃ ke 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna nahīṃ hotā hai ataḥ yahā~ 86 prakṛtika smaraṇāna nahīṃ kahā gayā pratika udayasthāna meṃ bhī ye hī cāroṃ sattāsthāna hote haiṃ jo vikalendriya aura tiyaṃca paṃcendriyoṃ kī apekṣā jānanā cāhiyo | isa prakāra 26 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle mithyādṛṣṭi jīva ke 45 sattāsthāna hote haiṃ|
manuṣya aura devagati ke yogya 30 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna kī choḍa़phara śeṣa vikalendriya aura tiryaṃca paṃcendriya ke yogya 30 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle mithyādṛṣṭi jīva ke sāmānya se pūrvokta 6 udayasthāna aura 86 ko choḍa़kara śeṣa pā~ca-pāṃtra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | yahā~ 89 prakṛtika sattāsthāna saṃbhava nahīṃ hone kā kāraṇa yaha hai 89 prakṛtika saptāsthāna vāle jīva ke tirya cagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai | yahā~ 21, 24, 25, 26 prakṛtika ina cāra udayasthānoṃ meṃ una pā~ca satāsthānoṃ kā kathana to pahale ke samāna jānanā cāhiye tathā śeṣa rahe 27, 28, 29, 30 aura 31 prakṛtika udayasthāna, so inameṃ se pratyeka meṃ 78 prakṛlika ke sivāya śeṣa cāra sattāsthāna hote haiṃ| isa prakāra 30 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane vāle mithyā dṛṣṭi jīva ke kula 40 sattāsthāna hote haiṃ|

﻿________________

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
(2) sāsādama guṇasthāma
pahale guṇasthāna ke baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ ko batalāne ke bāda aba dūsare guṇasthāna ke baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ ki-'tiga satta dugaṃ' | arthāt 3 baṃdhasthāna haiṃ, 7 udayasthāna haiṃ aura 2 sattāsthāna haiṃ | jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa isa prakāra hai
sāsādana guṇamā meṃ 28, 29 ra 20 maṣita, kīna māmāna haiṃ| inameṃ se 28 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna do prakāra kā hai-jarakagatiprāyogya aura devagatiprāyogya | sāsādana samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ke narakagatiprāyogya kā to baṃdha nahīṃ hotā kintu devagatiprāyogya kā hotā hai| usake baṃdhaka paryāpta tiryaṃca paṃcendriya aura manuṣya hote haiṃ | isake āya bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
26 prakṛtika baṃbhasthāna ke aneka bheda hai kintu unameṃ se sāsādana ke baṃdhane yogya do bheda haiṃ---tiryaṃcagatiprāyogya aura manuṣya gatiprāyogya | ina donoṃ ko sāsādana ekendriya, vikalendriya, tiryaca paṃṣendriya, manuṣya, deva aura māraka jīva bāṃdhate haiṃ| yahā~ usake phūla bhaṃga 6400 hote haiṃ| kyoṃki yadyapi sāsādana tiryacatiprāyogya yā manuṣyagatiprāyogya 26 prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhate haiṃ to bhī ve huṃḍasaṃsthāna aura sevārta yahanana kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ, kyoṃki ina donoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha mithyātva guṇasthāna meṃ hī hotā hai| jisase yahā~ pā~ca saṃhanana, pāṃca saṃsthāna, praśasta aura apraśasta vihāyogati yugala, sthira-asthira yugala, śubha-aśubha yugala, subhaga-durbhaga yugala, susvara-duḥsvara yugala, ādeyaanādeya yugala aura yaśa:kīti-ayaśa:koti yugala, isa prakāra inake paraspara guṇita karane para 3200 bhaṃga hote haiṃ| ye 3200 bhaṃga tiyaṃcagatiśāyogya bhī hote haiṃ aura manuṣyagatiprāyogya bhī hote haiṃ| isa prakāra donoṃ kā joḍa 6400 hotā hai|

saptatikā prakaraṇa
30 prakṛtika udayasthāna prathama samyaktva se cyuta hone vāle paryāpta tiyaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ ke yā uttara dikriyā meṃ vidyamāna devoṃ ke hotā hai| 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna meṃ tithaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ meṃ se pratyeka ke 1152 aura devoṃ ke 6. isa prakāra 1152+-1152++=2312 bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
31 prakṛtika udayasthāna prathama samyaktva se cyuta hone vāle paryāpta tiryacoṃ ke hotā hai| yahā~ isake kula 1152 bhaṃga hote haiṃ | isa prakāra sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ 7 udayasthāna aura unake bhaṃga hote haiṃ| bhāṣya gāthā meṃ bhī inake bhaṃga nimna prakāra se gināye haiṃ
basīsa bonni aṭṭa ya bāsīpa sayā ya paṃca nava udayā| pāhigā vīsā pāpagnekārasa sayā ||
arthāt sāsādana guṇasthāna ke jo 21, 24, 25, 26, 26, 30 aura 31 prakṛtika, sāta udayasthāna haiṃ; unake kramaśaḥ 32, 2, 8, 582, 6, 2312 aura 1152 bhaṃga hote haiṃ |
sāsādana guṇasthāna ke sāta udayasthānoṃ ko batalāne ke bāda aba sattāsthānoṃ ko batalāse hai ki yahā~ 62 aura 88 prakṛtika, ye do sattā. sthāna haiṃ| inameṃ se jo āhāraka catuṣka kā baṃdha karake upaśamaṇi se nyuta hokara sāsādana bhāva ko prāpta hotā hai, usake 62 kī sattā pāī ātī hai, anya ke nahīṃ aura 88 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā cāroṃ gatiyoṃ ke sāsādana jīvoṃ ke pāī jātī hai |
isa prakāra se sāsādana guṇasthāna ke baṃdha, udaya aura sattāsthānoṃ ko jānanā cāhiye | aba inake saṃvedha kā vicāra karate haiṃ|

31 prakṛtika, ye do udayasthāna hote haiṃ| pūrva meṃ baṃdhasthānoṃ kā vicāra karate samaya yaha batāyā jā cukā hai ki sāsādana jīva devagatiprāyogya hī 26 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai, namātitrākora 18 prakṛti kā. nahīṃ| usameṃ bhī karaṇaparyāpta sāsādana jīva hī devagatiprāyogya ko bāṃdhatā hai| isaliye yahā~ 30 aura 31 prakṛtika, ina do udayasthānoṃ ke alāvā anya śeṣa udayasthāna saṃbhava nahīṃ haiṃ| aba yadi manuṣyoṃ kī apekṣā 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna kā vicāra karate haiṃ to vahā~ 62 aura 88 prakṛtika, ye do sattāsthāna saṃbhava haiṃ aura yadi tiryaṃca paṃcendriyoṃ kī apekṣā 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna kā vicāra karate haiṃ to vahā~ 88 prakṛtika, yaha eka hī sattāsthāna saṃbhava haiṃ kyoṃki 62 prakṛtiyoṃ ko sattā usī ko prāpta hotī hai jo upaśamaśreṇi se cyuta hokara sāsādana bhāva ko prāpta hotā hai kintu tiryacoṃ meṃ upaśamatheṇi saṃbhava nahīṃ hai| ata: yahā~ 62 prakṛtika sattāsthāna kā niṣedha kiyā hai|

(3) miśra guṇasthāna
dūsare sāsādana guṇasthāna ke baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karane ke bāda aba tīsare miśra guṇasthāna ke paṃca ādi sthānoṃ kī keyana karate haiṃ | midha guṇasthāna meṃ-'duga tiga durga'-do baṃdhasthāna, tīna udayasthāna aura do sattāsthāna haiṃ| jinakā vivaraṇa isa prakāra hai ki 28 aura 26 prakṛtika, ye baṃghasthāna hote haiṃ| inameṃ se 28 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ ke hotā hai, kyoṃki ye miśra guṇasthāna meṃ devagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ| isake yahā~ 8 bhaṃga hote haiṃ|
26 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna deva aura nārakoṃ ke hotā hai| kyoṃki ve miśra guṇasthāna meṃ manuṣyagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ | isake bhī 8 bhaṃga hote haiṃ | donoṃ sthānoṃ meṃ ye bhaṃga sthira-asthira, śubhaaśubha aura yaśaḥkīti-ayaśaḥkīti ke vikalpa se prāpta hote haiṃ| 24242, śeṣa bhaṃga prāpta nahīṃ hote haiṃ kyoṃki śeṣa śubha parāvartamāna prakṛtiyā~ hī samyaga mithyādṛṣṭi jīva bāṃdhate haiṃ| __yahā~ baṃdhasthānoṃ kā kathana karane ke bāda aba udayasthāna batalāte haiṃ ki 26, 30 aura 31 prakṛtika ye tīna udayasthāna haiṃ | 29 prakṛtika udayasthāna deva aura nārakoṃ ke hotā hai | isa sthāna ke devoṃ ke 8 aura nārakoṃ ke 1 isa prakāra 6 bhaṃga hote haiṃ| 30 prakṛtika udayasthāna tiryaṃca va manuṣyoṃ ke hotā hai| isameṃ tithaṃcoṃ ke 1152 aura manuṣyoṃ ke 1152 bhaṃga hote haiṃ jo kula milākara 2304 haiṃ| 31 prakṛtika udayarathāna tiryaṃca paṃcendriyoṃ ke hī hotā hai | isake yahā~ kula milākara 1152 bhaṃga hote haiṃ| isa prakāra miśra guṇasthāna meṃ tīnoṃ udayasthānoṃ ke 6+2304-+ 1152= 3465 bhaṃga hote haiṃ|

saptatikā prakaraṇa
,
pāye jāte haiṃ jo tiyaṃca paṃcendriya ke manuṣyoṃ ke devoṃ ke da aura nārakoṃ kā 1 hai | isa prakāra kula milākara 6+6+8-|-1=25 haiṃ |
25 aura 27 prakṛtika udayasthāna deva aura nārakoṃ tathā vikriyā karane vāle tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ ke jānanā cāhiye | yahā~ jo 25 aura 27 prakṛtika sthānoṃ kā nāraka aura devoṃ ko svāmī batalāyā hai so yaha nāraka vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi yā kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi hī hotā hai aura deva tīnoṃ meṃ se kisī bhī samyagdarśana vālā hotā hai| cūrṇi meṃ bhī isī prakāra kahā hai
paṇavīsa - satabīsobayā bebaneraie viubbiyatiritha mathue ya paḍucca | nerago - vegasamma hiṭṭhī devo tivihasammaddiṣṭṭī vi ||
arthāt avirata samyagdṛṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ 25 aura 27 prakṛtika udayasthāna deva, nāraka aura vikriyā karane vāle tiyaṃtra aura manuṣyoṃ ke hotā hai| so inameṃ se aisā nāraka yā to kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi hotā hai yā vedaka samyaṣṭi, kintu deva ke tīnoṃ samyagdarśanoṃ meṃ se koī eka hotā hai|
26 prakṛtika udayasthāna kṣāyika samyagdṛṣṭi yā vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi titheca aura manuṣyoṃ ke hotā hai | aupaśamika samyagdṛṣṭi jīva tiryaca aura manuṣyoṃ meṃ utpanna nahīṃ hotā hai| ataḥ yahā~ tīnoṃ prakāra ke samyagdṛṣṭi jīvoṃ ko nahīṃ kahā hai | usameṃ bhī tiyaṃcoṃ ke mohanīya kī 22 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā kī apekṣā hī yahā~ vedaka samyaktva jānanā cāhiye |

āṭhaveṃ apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha ādi sthāna isa prakāra haiṃ'puṇagega pau' arthāta pāṃca baṃdhasthāna, eka udayasthāna aura cāra sattāsthānaḥ | inameṃ se pāṃca baṃdhasthāna 28, 29, 30, 31 aura 1 prakṛtika hai | inameṃ se prārambha ke cāra baṃṣasthāna to sātaveṃ apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna ke samāna jānanā cāhiye, kintu jaba devagatiprāyogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdhaviccheda ho jātā hai taba sirpha eka yaśa:kī ti nāma kā hī badha hotā hai, jisase yahā~ 1 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna bhī hotā hai|
yahā~ udayasthāna eka 30 prakṛtika hī hotā hai| jisake vanaṛṣabhanārāca saṃhanana, chaha saṃsthāna, susvara-dusvara aura do vihāyogati ke vikalpa se 24 bhaṃga hote haiṃ | kintu kucha ācāryoṃ ke mata se upaśamaṇi kī apekṣā apūrvakaraṇa meṃ kevala vajraṛṣabhanārāca saṃhanana kā udaya na hokara prārambha ke tīna saṃhananoṃ meṃ se kisī eka kā udaya hotā hai| ataḥ unake mata se yahā~ para 72 bhaṃga hote haiṃ| isī prakāra

saptatikā prakaraṇa
śamvārṣa—ima vigativiya sagale-ekendriya, vikalendriya aura sakalendriya (paṃcendriya) meṃ, paṇa paṃca meṃ aṭa-pāṃca, pāṃca aura āṭha, saṃghaṭhāṇāṇi-baṃdhasthāna, paṇa chamakerakāra---pāṃca, chaha aura gyāraha, sakyā-udayasthāna, paṇa-paṇa bārasa-pā~ca, pā~ca aura bāraha, ya-aura, saṃtāṇi-sattāsthāna |
gāthārtha-ekendriya, vikalendriya aura paṃcendriya meṃ anukrama se pāṃca, pāṃca aura āṭha baṃdhasthāna; pāṃca, chaha aura gyāraha udayasthāna tathā pāṃca, pāṃca aura bāraha sattāsthāna hote haiṃ | viśeṣāya-- pūrva gāthā meṃ gatimāgaṇā ke cāroṃ bhedoṃ meṃ nāmakarma ke baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ aura unake saṃvedha kā kathana kiyā gayā thā| isa gāthā meṃ indriyamārgaṇā ke ekendriya ādi pāṃca bhedoṃ meṃ baṃdhādi sthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karate hue anukrama se batāyā hai ki 'paṇa paṃca ya aṭṭha baṃdhaṭhāṇāṇi' ekendriya ke pāṃca, vikalendriya (dvīndriya, śrīndriya aura caturindriya) ke pāṃca tathā paṃcendriya ke āṭha baṃdhasthāna hai| isī prakāra anukrama se udayasthānoṃ kā nirdeśa karane ke liye kahā hai ki-'paṇa chakkekkārudayā'- ekendriya ke pāṃca. vikalendriyoṃ ke chaha aura paṃcendriyoṃ ke myāraha udayasthāna hote haiṃ tathā 'paṇa paṇa vārasa ya saṃtāṇi'–ekendriya ke pāṃca, vikalendriyoṃ ke pāṃca aura paṃcendriyoṃ ke bāraha sattāsthāna haiṃ | ina saba baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jā rahā hai| ___kula baṃdhasthāna āṭha haiṃ, unameṃ se ekendriyoṃ ke 26, 25, 26, 26

saptatikā prakaraṇa
isa prakāra indriya mārgaṇā kī apekṣā nāmakarma ke baṃdha, udara aura sattā sthānoṃ tathā unake saṃveghoṃ kā kathana jānanā cāhiye | ___ aba āge kī māthā meṃ baṃdha ādi sthānoṃ ke āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ kathana karane kā saṃketa karate haiṃ...
iya kambhapagaiṭhāṇāī suṭhu baṃdhukyasaṃtammāṇaṃ | gaiāiehi asu cauṣpagāreṇa naiyāṇi ||53||
śavārya-ya-pūrvokta prakāra se, kammapagāṭhāṇāī--karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthānoṃ ko, suku atyanta upayogapūrvaka, baṃdhupapasaṃta
-baṃdha, ra | papī karma na pṛśiyoṃ ke, gahaāiehi-gati ādi mārgaṇāsthānoṃ ke dvārā, aṭusu-āṭha anuyogahāroṃ meṃ, upapagāreṇa--cāra prakāra se. nevāṇi-jānanā cāhiye| ___lācārtha --ye pūrvokta baṃdha, udaya aura sattā sambandhī karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthānoṃ ko atyanta upayogapūrvaka gati ādi mārgaṇāsthānoṃ ke sātha āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ cāra prakāra se jānanā cāhiye|
viśeṣārṣa-~isa māthā se pūrva taka jñānāvaraṇa ādi āṭha vārmoṃ kī mūla aura uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃtra, udaya aura sattā sthānoṃ kā sāmānya rūpa se tathā jīvasthāna, guṇasthāna, gatimārgaṇā aura indriyamārgaṇā meṃ nirdeśa kiyā hai| lekina isa gāthā meṃ kucha viśeṣa saṃketa karate haiṃ ki jaisā pūrva meṃ gati ādi mārgaṇāoṃ meṃ kathana kiyā gayā hai, usake sātha unako āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ ghaṭita kara lenā cāhiye| isake sātha yaha bhī saṃketa kiyā hai ki sirpha prakṛtibaṃdha rūpa nahīṃ kintu 'cauppagāreṇa neyāṇi' prakṛtibaṃdha ke sātha sthiti, anubhāga aura pradeśa rūpa se bhī ghaṭita karanā cāhiye | kyoṃki ye baṃdha, udaya aura

371
sattā rūpa saba karma prakṛti, sthiti, anubhāga aura pradeśa ke bheda se cāra-bāra prakāra ke haiṃ|
ina cāroṃ prakāra rūpa karmoṃ ko kina meṃ aura kisake dvārā ghaṭita karane ke lie gāthā meṃ saṃketa kiyā hai ki-- bhāsada gaddhā gati ādi ghaudaha mārgaṇāoṃ ke dvārā āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ inayā cintana karanā hai| ___mārgaṇā śabda kā artha anveṣaṇa karanā hai | ata: bhārgaṇā kā yaha artha huā ki jinake dvārā yā jinameṃ jīvoṃ kā anveṣaṇa kiyā jātā hai, unheṃ mārgaṇā kahate haiṃ | mārgaṇā ke caudaha bheda isa prakāra haiṃ
gāvie pakāe joe vee kasāya māge |
saṃjama baṃsaṇa letā bhaga samme sammi māmhāre | 1 gati, 2 indriya, 3 kāya, 4 yoga, 5 veda, 6 kaṣāya, 7 jñāna, 8 saṃyama, darśana, 10 leśyā, 11 bhavyatva, 12 samyaktva, 13 saṃjñī aura 14 āhāra | inake 14 bhedoṃ ke uttara bheda 62 hote haiṃ|
varṇana kī yaha paramparā hai ki jīva sambandhī jisa kisī bhī avasthā kā varṇana karanā hai, usakā pahale sāmānya rūpa se varṇana kiyā jātā hai aura usake bāda usakā viśeṣa cintana caudaha mārgaṇāoṃ dvārā āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ kiyā jātā hai| anuyogaddāra yaha adhikāra kā paryāyavācī nāma hai aura viṣaya-vibhāga kī dṛṣṭi se ye adhikāra honādhika bhī kiye jā sakate haiṃ| parantu mārgaṇāoṃ kā vistṛta vivecana mukhya rūpa se āṭha adhikāroṃ meṃ hī pāyā jātā hai, ata: mukhya rūpa se āṭha hī liye jāte haiṃ| ina āṭha adhikāroṃ ke nāma isa prakāra haiṃ..

1 sat, saṃkhyā, 3 kṣetra, 4 sparśana, 5 kāla, 6 antara, 7 bhāda aura 8 alpabahutva | ina adhikāroṃ kā artha inake nāmoṃ se hī spaṣṭa ho jātā hai | arthāt sat anuyogadvāra meṃ yaha batāyā jātā hai ki vivakṣita dharma kina mārgaṇāoṃ meṃ hai aura kina meṃ nahīṃ hai| saṃkhyā anuyogadvāra meṃ usa vivakṣita dharma vāle jīvoṃ kī saṃkhyā batalāī jātī hai | kṣetra anuyogadvāra meṃ vivakṣita dharma bāle jīvoṃ kā vartamāna nivāsa sthāna batalāyā jātā hai| sparśana anuyogadvāra meṃ una vivakṣita dharma vāle jīvoṃ ne jitane kṣetra kā pahale sparśa kiyā ho, aba kara rahe haiṃ aura āge kareṃge usa sabakā samuccaya rūpa se nirdeśa kiyā jātā hai | kāla anuyogadvāra meṃ vivakṣita dharma pāle jīvoṃ kī jaghanya va utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā vicāra kiyā jātā hai | antara śabda kā artha vigraha yā vyavadhāna hai ata: antara anuyogahāra meṃ yaha batāyā jātā hai ki vivakṣita dharma kā sāmānya rūpa se yā kisa mārgaṇā meṃ kitane kāla taka antara rahatā hai yā nahīṃ rahatā hai| bhāva anuyogadvāra meṃ usa vivakṣita dharma ke bhāva kā tathā alpabahutva anuyogadvāra meṃ usake alpabahutva kā vicāra triyā jātā hai | ___yadyapi gāthā meṃ sirpha itanā saṃketa kiyā gayā hai ki isī prakāra baṃdha, udaya aura sattā rūpa karmoṃ kā tathā unake avāntara bheda-prabhedoṃ kā prakṛti, sthiti, anubhāga aura pradeśa rūpa se gati ādi mārgaṇāoṃ ke dvārā āṭha anuyogadvāroṃ meṃ vivecana kara lenā cāhiye jaisā ki pahale varṇana kiyā gayā hai| lekina isa viṣaya meṃ ṭīkākāra ācārya malayagiri kā vaktavya hai ki 'yadyapi āṭhoṃ karmoṃ ke sat anuyogadvāra kā varṇana guṇasthānoṃ meṃ sāmānya rūpa se pahale kiyā hī gayā hai aura saṃkhyā ādi sāta anuyogadvāroṃ kā vyākhyāna karmaprakṛti prābhuta graṃthoṃ ko dekhakara karanā cāhiye| kintu karmaprakṛti prābhṛta ādi graṃtha vartamāna kāla meṃ upalabdha nahīṃ haiṃ, isaliye ina saṃkhyādi anuyoga

dvāroṃ kā vyākhyāna karanā kaṭhina hai| phira bhī jo pratyutpannamati vidvāna haiṃ ve pūrvāpara sambandha ko dekhakara unakā vyākhyāna kareṃ|

koṣā---ga meṃ māmāna, udayana aura saptāsthānoṃ ke sātha ina sabake saṃvedha kā vicāra kiyā gayā| lekina udaya va udīraṇā meṃ yathāsambhava samānatā hone se usakā vicāra nahīṃ kiye jāne ke kāraṇa ko spaṣṭa karane ke liye isa gāthā meṃ batāyā gayā hai ki udaya aura udīraṇā meṃ yadyapi antara nahīṃ hai, lekina itanī viśeṣatā hai ki ikatālīsa karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke ucaya aura udīraṇā meṃ bhinnatā hai| isaliye udayayogya 122 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se 41 prakṛtiyoṃ ko choḍa़kara śeṣa 81 prakṛtiyoṃ ke udaya aura udīraṇā meṃ samānatā jānanī cāhiye|
udaya aura udīraṇā ke lakṣaṇa kramaśa: isa prakāra haiṃ ki kālaprāpta karma paramāṇuoṃ ke anubhava karane ko udaya kahate haiṃ aura udayāvali ke bāhara sthita karma paramāṇuoṃ ko kaṣāya sahita yā kaṣāya rahita yoga saṃjñā bāle vīrya viśeṣa ke dvārā udayāvali meṃ lākara unakā udayaprāpta karma paramāṇuoṃ ke sātha anubhava karanā udīraṇā kahalātā hai | isa prakāra karma paramāṇuoṃ kā anubhavana

376 cakṣudarśanāvaraṇa ādi cāra, kula milākara ina caudaha prakṛtiyoṃ kī bārahaveṃ kṣīṇamoha guṇasthāna meṃ eka āvali kāla śeṣa rahane taka udaya aura udīraṇā barābara hotī rahatī hai | parantu eka āvali kāla kī śeṣa raha jāne para usake bāda ukta caudaha prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hī hotā hai kintu udayāvālagata karmadalika saba kāraṇoṃ ke ayogya hote haiṃ, isa niyama ke anusāra unakī udīraṇā nahīṃ hotī hai|
śarīraparyāpti se paryāpta hue jīyoṃ ke śarīrayāpti ke samāpta hone ke anantara samaya se lekara jaba taka indriyaparyāpti pūrṇa nahīṃ hotī taba taka darśanāvaraṇa kī śeṣa nidrā ādi pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hī hotā hai udīraṇā nahīṃ hotī hai | isake atirikta śeṣa kāla meṃ unakā udaya aura udīraṇā eka sātha hotī hai aura unakā viccheda' bhī eka sātha hotā hai| __ sātā aura asātā vedanīya kā udaya aura udīraṇā pramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna taka eka sātha hotī hai, kintu agale guṇasthānoṃ meṃ inakā udaya hī hotā hai, udīraṇā nahīṃ hotī hai| prathama samyaktva ko utpanna karane vāle jīva ke antarakaraṇa karane ke paścāta prathamasthiti meṃ eka āvali pramāṇa kāla ke śeṣa rahane para mithyātva kā udaya hī hotā hai, udīraṇā nahīṃ hotī hai tathā kṣāyika samyaktva ko utpanna karane vāle jisa vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva ne mithyātva aura samyagamithyātva kā kṣaya karake samyaktva kī sarvaapavartanā ke dvārā apardhatanā karake antarmuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti śeṣa rakhī hai aura usake bāda udaya tathā

ṣaṣṭha karmagranthya
isa prakāra pichalī gāthā meṃ udaya aura udīraṇā meṃ svāmitva kī apekṣā jina ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kī viśeṣatā kā nirdeśa kiyā thā| una ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ke nāma kāraṇa sahita isa gāthā meṃ batalāye haiṃ ki inakī udī ra ṇā kyoṃ nahīṃ hotī hai | aba āge kī gāthāoṃ meṃ guṇasthānoṃ meṃ prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha ko batalāte haiṃ| guṇasthānoṃ meṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha
titthagarāhāragavirahiyāo ajjei savvapagaīo| micchataveyago sāsaṇo vi igudhīsasesāo ||56||
śāmdārtha-- tityārāhāraga-tīrthakara nāma aura āhārakaḍika, virahiyāo-binā, aleha-upārjita, baṃdha karatā hai, samvapagaīyosabhī prakṛtiyoṃ kā. mihattaveyago-mithyāṣṭi, sāsaṇo–sāsādana guṇasthāna yālā, vi-mī, iguvosa:-unnīsa, sesāo-śeṣa, bākī kī|
gāthārya-mithyādṛṣṭi jīvaṃ tīrthaṃkara nāma aura āhārakadvika ke binā śeṣa saba prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai tathā sāsādana guṇasthāna bālā unnīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ke binā śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhatā hai|
viśeṣārtha-guṇasthāna mithyātva, sāsādana ādi caudaha haiṃ aura jñānāvaraṇa ādi āṭha mūla karmoṃ kī uttara prakṛtiyā~ 148 haiṃ| unameṃ se baṃdhayogya prakṛtiyoṃ kī saṃkhyā 120 mānī gaī hai | baṃdha kī apekṣā 120 prakṛtiyoṃ ke mānane kā matalaba yaha nahīṃ hai ki śeṣa 28 prakṛtiyā~ choḍa़ dī jātī haiṃ | lekina isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki pāṃca baṃdhana aura pā~ca saṃghātana, ye dasa prakṛtiyāṃ śarīra kī avinābhāvī haiṃ, ataḥ jahā~ jisa śarīra kā baṃdha hotā hai, vahā~ usa baṃdhana aura saṃghātana kā baṃdha avaśya hotā hai | jisase ina dasa prakṛtiyoṃ ko alaga se nahīṃ gināyā

jātā hai | isa prakāra 148 meṃ se dasa prakṛtiyoṃ ko kama kara dene para 138 prakṛttiyā~ raha jātī haiṃ tathā varṇacatuṣka ke avāntara bheda 20 haiṃ kintu baṃdha meṃ avāntara bhedoṃ kī vivakṣā na karake mūla meṃ varṇa, gandha, rasa aura sparśa, ye cāra prakṛtiyāṃ grahaṇa kī jātī haiṃ | ataeva 130 meṃ se 20-4=16 ghaṭā dene para 122 prakṛtiyā~ śeṣa raha jātī haiṃ| darśana mohanīya kī samyaktva, samyagamithyāsva aura mithyātva, ye tīna prakṛtiyā~ haiṃ| unameṃ se samyaktva aura samyamithyātva ye do prakṛtiyā~ baṃdha prakṛtiyāṃ nahīṃ haiṃ| kyoṃki baṃdha mithyātva prakṛti kā hotā hai aura jīva apane samyaktva guṇa ke dvārā hī mithyātva ke dalikoṃ ke tīna bhāga banā detā hai| inameṃ se jo atyanta viśuddha hotā hai use samyaktva aura jo kama viśuddha hotā hai use samyagamithyātva saṃjñā prāpta hotī hai aura ina donoṃ ke atirikta śeṣa aśuddha bhāga mithyātva kahalātā hai | ataḥ 122 meṃ se samyaktva va samyagamithyātva ina do prakṛtiyoṃ ko ghaṭā dene para śeṣa 120 prakṛtiyā~ baṃdhayogya mānī jātī haiṃ |

1 mithyātva, 2 napuṃsakaveda, 3 narakagati, 4 narakānupūrvī, 5 narakāyu, 6 ekendriya jāti,7dvīndriya jāti, 8 śrīndriya jāti, caturindriya jāti, 10 huṃḍasaṃsthāna, 11 sevāta saṃhanana, 12 ātapa, 13 sthāvara, 14 sūkṣma, 15 sādhāraṇa aura 16 aparyāpta | mithyātva se baṃdhane vālī 117 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se ukta 16 prakṛtiyoṃ ko ghaṭā dene para sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ 101 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai |

chāyālasesa moso avirayasammo tiyālaparisesā | sevaṇa desavirao viro sagavaṇṇasesāo ||57||

viśeṣārtha - - pahale aura dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ baṃghayogya prakṛtiyoṃ ko pūrva gāthā meṃ batalāyā hai| isa gāthā meṃ miśra ādi cāra guṇasthānoṃ kī baṃdha prakiyoṃ kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ| śivakā vivaraṇa likhe nīce anusāra hai

tīsare miśra guṇasthāna meṃ 'chāyālasesa mīso' baṃdhayogya 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se chiyālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ko ghaṭāne para śeṣa rahīṃ 120 - 46 = 74 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki dūsare sāsādana guṇasthāna taka anantānubaṃdhī kā udaya hotā hai. lekina tīsare miśra guṇasthāna meṃ anantānubaṃdhī kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai | ataḥ anantānubandhī ke udaya se jina 25 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai, unakā yahā~ baṃdha nahīṃ hai| arthāt tīsare miśra guṇasthāna meṃ sāsādana guṇasthāna kī baṃghayogya 101 prakṛtiyoṃ se 25 prakṛtiyā~ aura ghaṭa jātī haiṃ| ve 25 prakṛtiyā~ ye haiṃ -- styānaddhitrika, anantānubaṃdhīcatuṣka, strīveda, tiyaṃcagati, ticānupūrvī, tiryaṃcāyu, prathama aura antima ko choḍa़kara madhya ke cāra saṃsthāna, prathama aura antima ko choḍa़kara madhya ke cāra saṃhanana, udyota, apraśasta vihāyogati, dubhaṃga, duḥsvara, anādeya aura nīca gotra | isake atirikta yaha niyama hai ki miśra guṇasthāna meṃ kisī bhī āyu kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai ataḥ yahā~ manuṣyāyu aura devāyu, ye do āyu aura kama ho jātī haiṃ| manuṣyāyu aura devāyu, ina do āyuyoṃ ko ghaṭāne kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki narakāyu kā baṃdhaviccheda pahale aura ticā kā baṃdhaviccheda dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ ho jātā hai| ataḥ āyu karma ke cāroṃ bhedoṃ meṃ se śeṣa rahī manuṣyāyu aura devāyu, ina do prakṛtiyoṃ ko hī yahā~ kama kiyā jātā hai| isa prakāra sāsā -

tak
dana guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ ba~dhane vālī 19 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ ina 25+2=27 prakṛtiyoṃ ko milā dene para 46 prakṛtiyāṃ hotī haiṃ jinakā miśra guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| kintu 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se 46 prakṛtiyoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa rahī 74 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai |
cauthe avirala samyagdṛṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ 43 prakṛtiyoṃ ke binā śeṣa 77 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai- 'avirayasammo tiyālaparisā |' isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki aviratasamyagdṛṣṭi jīva ke manuṣyāyu, vāyu aura tīrthaṃkara nāma, ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha sambhava hai| ataḥ yahā~ baṃdhayogya 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se 46 na ghaṭākara 43 prakṛtiyā~ hī baṭāī haiṃ| isa prakāra aviratisamyagdṛṣṭi guṇasthāna meṃ 77 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha batalāyā hai |
deśavirata nāmaka pā~caveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ 53 ke binā 67 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha batalāyā hai- 'tevaraṇa desaviro'| isakā artha yaha hai ki apratyāsthānāvaraṇa kaṣāya ke udaya se jina dasa prakṛtiyoṃ kā gaṃja aviratasamyagdṛṣṭi jīva ke hotā hai, apratyāsthānāvaraṇa kavāya kā udaya na hone se unakā yahā~ baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| ataḥ cīce guṇasthāna meṃ kama kī gaī 43 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ 10 prakṛtiyoṃ ko aura oḍa़ dene para deśavirata guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha ke ayogya 53 prakṛtiyāṃ ho jātī haiṃ aura inake atirikta śeṣa rahīṃ 67 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai|
apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya ke udaya se baṃdhane vālī 10 prakṛtiyoṃ ke nāma isa prakāra haiṃ- apratyākhyānāvaraṇa krodha, māna, māyā, lobha, manuṣyagati, manuṣyānupūrvī manuṣyāyu, audārika śarīra, mauvārika aṃgopāṃga aura banārāca saṃhanana |
śramatavirata guṇasthāna meṃ 57 ke binā 63 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai| isakā āśaya yaha hai ki pratyākhyānāvaraṇa ke udaya se jina

pratyākhyānāvaraṇa catuṣka (krodha, māna, māyā, lobha) kā baṃdha dehāvirata guṇasthāna taka hotā thā, unakā pramattavirata guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| ata: jina 53 prakṛtiyoṃ ko deśavirata guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdhane ke ayogya batalāyā hai, unameṃ ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ ke aura milā dene para pramattavirata guṇasthāna meṃ 57 prakṛtiyoṃ baṃdha ke ayogya hotī haiṃ-'virao sagavaṇṇasesāo|' isaliye pramattavirata guṇasthāna meṃ 63 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai |
aba āge kī gāthā meṃ sātaveṃ aura āṭhaveṃ gaṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha prakatiyoṃ kī saṃkhyā kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ|
igusadiṭhamappamaso baṃdhai devājyassa iyaro vi | aṭṭhāvaṇṇamapurakho chappaṇṇaṃ vā ci chavīsaṃ ||5||
śāvārtha-iguṣṭi--unasaṭha prakṛtiyoṃ ke, appamato-apramatta. saṃpata, dhara-baṃṣa karatā hai, vāuyassa-devāyu kā baṃdhaka, isarotri - apramala bhī, aṭṭhāṣaṇa -- aṭṭhāvana, apugdho-- apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna ghālā, chappaṇṇaṃ-- appana, kā vi--- ayavā bhī, ayvīsaṃ-~~chatrīsa |
gāthārṣa-apramattasaṃyata guṇasthānavartī jīva unasaṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai| yaha devāyu kā bhī baṃdha karatā hai| apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna vālā aṭṭhāvana, chappana athavā chabbīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai| viśeṣārtha- isa gāthā meṃ sātaveṃ apramattasaṃyata aura āṭhaveṃ apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdhayogya prakṛtiyoṃ kī saṃkhyā kā nirdeśa kiyā hai| lekina yahāṃ kathana śailī kī yaha viśeṣatā hai ki pichalī gāthāoṃ meṃ to kisa guṇasthāna meṃ kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai-isako mukhya mānakara baṃdha prakṛtiyāṃ batalāī thīṃ kintu isa gāthā se usa krama ko badala kara yaha batalāyā hai ki kisa guṇasthāna meṃ kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ

ka! aṃśa hotā hai| ataḥ aba pāpā ke saṃketānusāra guṇadānoṃ meṃ baṃdha akāliyoṃ kī saṃkhyā kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ| ___ sātada lāpānavirata guṇasthāna meṃ unasaṭha prakṛtigoṃ kā baṃdha malā hai- 'marisaramāṇalo' | yaha to pahale batalāyā jā jhukā hai ki
pramattacintata guṇasthāna meṃ 63 prakṛttiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai, unameṃ se asālāvedanīya, arati, śoka, asthira, aśubha aura ayaśa-kīti, ina chaha prakaliyoṃ kā sātave guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai, chaṭhe guṇasthāna taka baṃdha hotā hai| ata: pūrvokta 63 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se ina 6 prakṛtiyoṃ ko kama kara dene para. 57 prabhātiyoṃ śeṣa rahatī haiṃ, lekina isa guṇasthāna meṃ āhārakaddhika kā baṃdha hotā hai jisase 57 meṃ 2 prakṛtiyoṃ ko laura milā dene para apramattasaṃyata ke 56 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha kahā gayā hai|
ukta pratiyoṃ meṃ detāyu bhī sammilita hai lekina andhakāra meṃ apamatasaṃgata devāyu kā bhī baṃdha karatā hai... 'baṃdhai devālayassa idhara ni-rā prakāra śaka se nirdeśa kiyā hai| usakā abhiprāya yaha hai kaḍāyu ke saṃga kā prārambha samatasaṃpata hī karatā hai phira bhī yaha
lāyu kā vara karate hue apramattasaṃyata bhī ho jātā hai aura isa prakāra asamatapata bhī devāyu kā baṃdhaka hotā hai| parantu isase
baha na samajhaṃ ki anamatasaṃyata bhī devāyu ke baṃdha kā prārambha rahā hai| apramatasaṃpata devāyu ke baṃdha kā prārambha karatā hai| yadi yaha abhiprāya liyā jātā hai to aisā socanā ucita nahīṃ hai| isī bāta ko spaṣṭa karane ke liye graṃthakāra ne 'apramattasaṃyata bhī devāyu kā baṃdha karatā hai' yaha nirdeśa kiyā hai|' --- . .-.- -. .. . 1 tenaitat sūcyate --pramattasaṃyata śavāyurvandhaṃ pradhabhata sārabhase, ārabhya va

saptatikā prakaraṇa apūrvakaraṇa nāmaka bhāu puṇasthāna meṃ aṭṭhāvana, appana aura jambīsa pratiyoṃ kā hotā hai| prahatiyoṃ ko saṃsthā meṃ bhinnatā kā kāraṇa mahata pūrṇakāniga meṃ romeśana ke hāra kā vindhera ho jāne para apūrṇakaraṇa guṇasthāna bālā jīpa pahale saṃsthātaveṃ bhāga meṃ 5 kṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai| anantara nimā aura praghalā kā adhika ho jāne para saṃśyāta bhāga ke śeṣa rahane taka 56 prahatiyoṃ kāma karatā hai aura usake bāda devagati, devānupūrvī, papaṇiya jāti, kriya karīra, hiya aṃgopāṃga, āhāraka śarīra, bhāhāraka aṃgopāṃga, tejasaśarīra, kārmaṇaśarīra, samacaturasra saṃsthāna, paṇe pasuṣka, agurulā, upamāta, parāpāta, upavāsa, praśasta bihāyogati, basa, pāvara, paryāpta, pratyeka, hipara, śubha, subhaga, susbara, ādeya, nirmāṇa aura sīkara, ina tīsa pratiyoṃ kā baṃdhavinoda ho jāne para aṃtima bhāga meṃ 21 prakṛtiyoṃ kā karatā hai| isī kā saṃketa karane
liye gāyā meṃ nirdeśa hai ki-advābalamapumyo chappaṇṇaṃ para vi ghanīse|
isa prakāra se āṭha puṇasthāna taka kī medha prakṛtiyoṃ kā kathana kiyā jā cukā hai| aba āge kī gāthā meṃ śeṣa rahe chaha guṇasthānoṃ kī bedha prakṛtiyoṃ kī saṃkhyā ko patalāte hai|
bābīsā epūrṇa baṃdhā aḍārasaṃtamaniyaṭṭī| sattara muṭumasarāgo sābamamoho sabogi ti ||6||

་
gāthārtha – anivṛttivāvara guṇasthāna bālā bāīsa kā aura usake bāda eka-eka prakṛti kama karate hue aṭhāraha pra tiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai| sūkṣmarūparāma bālā samaha prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhatā hū~ upaśāṃśī koṇī upa kevalī guṇasthāna vāle sirpha eka sātāvedamīya prakṛti kā baṃdha karate haiṃ|
ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
viśeṣārtha - maudheṃ anivṛttimāvara guṇasthāna ke pahale bhāga meṃ bāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki yadyapi āṭhaveṃ apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ 26 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai, phira bhī usake aṃtima samaya meṃ hāsya, rati, bharata aura jugupsā, ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdhaviccheda ho jāne se nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke pahale samaya meṃ 22 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha batalāyā hai | isake bāda pahale bhāga ke aṃta meṃ puruṣameva kā dūsare bhāga ke aṃta meṃ saṃjvalana krodha kā tīsare bhāga ke aṃta meṃ saṃjjalana māna kā, cauthe bhāga ke aṃta meṃ saṃcalana māyā kā biccheda ho jāne se pāṃcaveṃ bhāga meṃ 18 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai, arthāt mau anivṛttibādara guṇasthāna ke baṃdha kī apekṣā pāṃca bhāga haiṃ mataḥ prāraṃbha meṃ to 22 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai aura usake bāda pahale, dūsare, sosare, cauthe bhāga ke aṃta meṃ kramaśa: eka-eka prakṛti kā baṃdhaviccheda hote jāne se 21, 20, 19 aura 18 prakṛtiyoṃ kā maṃca hotā hai| isī āśaya ko spaṣṭa karane ke liye gāthā meṃ saṃketa kiyā hai- bābīsā eguṇaṃ baṃdha aṭṭhārasaṃtamaniyaṭṭī |'
J
lekina jaba anivṛttibāvara guṇasthāna ke pāṃcaveṃ bhāga ke aṃta meṃ saṃjvalana lobha kā baṃdhaviccheda hotā hai taba dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna meṃ 17 prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha batalāyā hai- 'satara sumasarāgī' |

saptatikā prakaraṇa
dasaveṃ guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ jñānāvaraṇa kī pāṃca, darśagāvaraṇa kī cāra, aṃtarāya kī pāṃca, yaśaḥkīti aura ucca gotra, ina solaha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdhaviccheda hotā hai| arthāt dasadhai guṇasthāna taka mohanīyakarma kā upaśama yā kṣaya ho jāne se amoha daśā prāpta ho jātī hai jisase mohanīyakarma se vihīna jo upaśāṃtamoha, kṣīṇamoha aura sayogi-- kevalī-gyārahaveṃ, bārahaveṃ aura terahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ sirpha eka sātāvedanīyakarma kā baṃdha hotā hai-'sāyamamoho sogi ni|'
se rahaveṃ sayogikevali guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ sātāvedalīya kā bhī baṃdhaviccheda ho jāne se caudahaveṃ ayomikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha ke kāraṇoṃ kā abhāva ho jāne se kisī bhī maiṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai : arthāt caudahavāṃ guṇasthāna karmabaṃdha se rahita hai|
yadyapi gāthā meṃ ayogikevalī guṇasthāna kā nirdeśa nahīṃ kiyā hai tathāpi gāthā meṃ jo yaha nirdeśa kiyā hai ki eka sātāvedanīya kā badha moharahita aura sayogikevalī jīva kārale haiṃ, usase paha phalitārtha nikalatā hai ki ayogiphevalī guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdha ke mukhya kāraṇa kaṣāya aura yoga kā abhāva ho jātā hai aura kāraṇa ke abhāva meṃ kārya nahīṃ hotā hai| ataḥ ayogikevalī guṇasthāna meṃ karma kā leśamātra bhī baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|
isa prakāra cāra gāthāoṃ meṃ kisa guṇasthāna meṃ kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai aura kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai isakā vicāra kiyā gayā| jinakā saṃkṣepa meṃ vivaraṇa isa prakāra jānanā cāhiye

saptatikā prakaraṇa pratyeka guṇasthāna meṃ kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha aura vicacheda hotā hai aura unake nāma māvi kā ullekha ditīya karmagraṃtha meṃ viśeṣa rūpa se kiyā gayā hai| ataḥ jijñāsu jana usako dekha leveṃ|| ___ guṇasthānoṃ meṃ apasvāmitva kā upasaṃhāra karate hue mārgaṇāoṃ meṃ bhī sāmānya se baṣasvāmitva ko batalāne ke liye kahate haiṃ kieso ubaṃdhasāmittamoṣo gāyāesu vi saheva | mohāmo sāhiejā japa mahā parisambhāṣo ||60||
pā-elopa pūpoṃkta guṇasthāna kā babhava, - bhora, pitāpita- pāmitva kā, bhaugo-koṣa (sāmāgya) se, nāmāāe-gati māvi mārgaṇāmoṃ meṃ, vi-bhī, sahaba-vaise hī, isī prakāra, kopa magere, hamā pāhiye, nava-jisa mārgaṇāsthāna meṃ, mahā-jisa prakāra se, pagacitāmāpī-mahati kā sammāna |
mānārtha-yaha pūrvokta guṇasthānoṃ kā baṃdhabheda, svāmitva kā śrīṣa kamana jānanā cāhiye| gati ādi mārgaṇāoṃ meṃ bhī isī prakāra (sāmānya se) jahā~ jitanī pravṛttiyoṃ kā baṃdha
hotā hai, tabanusāra yahā~ bhī moṣa ke samāna baṃdhasvāmitva . kā kapata karanā cāhiye||
vivā-pichalī bāra gāthāoṃ meṃ pratyeka guṇasthāna meṃ prakṛttiyoṃ saṃbha kara aura dhanahīṃ karane kā kathana kiyā gayā hai | jisase sāmāmpasamā svāmisma kā kāma ho jātā hai| tathāpi gati ādi māraṇāmoṃ meṃ kitanī-timī pratiyoṃ kā vadha hotā hai aura kitanīkitanī prātiyoṃ kā nahīṃ hotā hai, isako jānanā śeṣa raha jātā hai| isakā lima gāthā meṃ sanī pūṣanā dī gaī hai ki jahāṃ jitanī prātiyoṃ kā hotā ho isakā vicāra karake oṣa ke samāna māgaṇāsthānoṃ meṃ bhī svāmitva kā kaṣama kara lenā cāhiye|

yadyapi ukta saṃketa ke anusāra yaha āvaśyaka ho jātā hai ki yahā~ mārgaṇāoṃ meṃ baṃdhasvāmitva kā vicāra kiyā jāye lekina tīsare karmagraṃtha meṃ isakā vistāra se vicāra kiyā jā cukā hai ata: jijñāsu jana vahā~ se jāna leve| ___ aba kisa gati meṃ kitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā hotī hai, isakā kathana āge kī gāthā meṃ karate haiṃ| tisthagaradevānarayāugaṃ ca tisu tisu gaīsu bāddhagvaṃ | avasesā payaḍīo havaṃti samāsu vi gaīsu||61||
śagārma-tispagaravāniravāḍā....tīrthakara, devāyu aura narakāyu, 1-aura, tituti--tīna-tīna, gaīmu-gatiyoṃ meṃ, bovaśva -mānanā cāhiye, bhavaseptā-kṣeṣa, bākī kī, papaḍīo-prakṛtiyā~, harati--hotī haiṃ, sambarasu-samī, vi-mī, gaīmugatiyoṃ meṃ |
bhāvārtha-tīrthakara nāma, devāyu aura narakāyuinakī sattā tīna-tīna matiyoṃ meṃ hotī hai aura inake sivāya śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā sabhī gatiyoṃ meṃ hotī hai|
viśeṣārṣa-aba jisa gati meṃ jitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā hotī hai, usakā nirdeśa karate haiṃ ki tīrthaṃkara nāma, devāyu aura narakāyu, ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā sīna-tīna gatiyoṃ meṃ pāī jātī hai| arthāta sīrthakara nāmakarma kī naraka, deva aura manuṣya ina tīna gatiyoṃ meṃ sattā pāī jātī hai, kintu tiyaṃcagati meṃ nahīṃ| kyoṃki tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma kī sattā bālā tithaṃcagati meṃ utpanna nahīṃ hotā hai, tathā tiyaṃcagati meṃ tīrthakara nāmakarma kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai | ataḥ naraka, deva aura manuṣya, ina tīna gatiyoṃ meṃ hī tīrthakara prakṛti kī sattā vatalāī hai|
tiryaca, manuṣya aura deva gati meṃ hī devāyu kī sattā pāī jātī hai, kyoṃki narakagati meṃ nārakoṃ ke devāyu ke baṃdha na hone kā niyama hai|

isī prakāra tiyaṃca, manuṣya aura naraka gati meṃ hī narakāyu kī sattā hotī hai, devagati meṃ nahīṃ kyoṃki devoṃ ke narakāyu kā baṃdha sambhava nahīṃ hai |
ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa sabhī prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā cāroṃ gatiyoṃ meṃ pāī jātī hai| āśaya yaha hai ki devāyu kā baṃdha to tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke baṃdha ke pahale bhī hotā hai aura pīche bhī hotā hai, kintu narakāyu ke saṃbaṃdha meṃ yaha niyama hai ki jisa manuṣya ne narakāyu kā baṃdha kara liyā hai, vaha samyagdṛṣṭi hokara tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā bhī baṃdha kara sakatā hai| isī prakāra tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kī sattā vālā jīva - deva aura nāraka - manuṣyāyu kā hī baṃdha karate haiṃ tiryaṃcāyu kā nahīṃ. yaha niyama hai | ataḥ tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kī sattā tiyaṃcagati ko choḍa़kara śeṣa tīna gatiyoṃ meṃ pāī jātī hai|
isī prakāra nāraka ke devāyu kā deva ke narakāyu kā baṃdha nahīṃ karane kā niyama hai, ataḥ devāyu kī sattā narakagati ko choḍa़kara śeṣa tīna galiyoṃ meṃ aura narakā kī sattā devagati ko choḍa़kara doṣa tīna gatiyoṃ meṃ pāī jātī hai |
ukta āśaya kā yaha niṣkarṣa huā ki tīrthaṃkara, devāyu aura narakāyu ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa saba prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā saba gatiyoṃ meṃ hotī hai| yānī nānā jīvoṃ kī apekṣā narakagati meṃ devāyu ke binā 147 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā hotī hai, tiyaṃcagati meṃ tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke binā 147 prakṛtiyoṃ kī aura devagati meṃ narakāyu ke binā 147 prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā hotī hai| lekina manuṣyagati meṃ 145 prakṛtiyoṃ ko hī sattā hotī hai|
pūrva meṃ guṇasthānoṃ meṃ karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha, udaya, sattā sthānoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā hai tathā guṇasthāna prāya: upaśamaśreṇi, kṣapakaśreṇi

paṣṭha karmagrantha vāle haiṃ | ata: upaśamaṇi aura kṣapakaṇi kā svarūpa batalānā jarūrī hai | yaha pahale upaśamaṇi kā svarūpa kathana karate haiṃ| pakṣamaphasāyacaurpha baṃsatiga sattagā vi uvasaṃtā || adhiratasammattāmo jāva niyaṭṭi ti nāyacyā ||62||
śabdārtha---padamaphasāyacamaka-prathama. kaṣāya catuṣka (anaṃtānubaṃdhīkaṣāyacatuṣka), vasatiga-darśanamohanīyatrika, sasagā visātoṃ prakṛtiyā~, upasaṃtā-upaśānta huī, aviratasammatāo avirata samyagara ṣṭi guṇasthāna se lekara, āva niyaṭṭi li---apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna taka, nāyabvā jānanā cāhiye|
gāthārya-prathama kaṣāya catuṣka (anaṃtānubaṃdhī vāṣāya catuṣka) darśanamohanika, ye sāta prakṛtiyāṃ avirata samyagdṛṣṭi se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna taka niyama se upaśāṃta ho jātī haiṃ, aisā jānanā cāhiye | viśeṣārṣa-upaśamaṇi kā svarūpa batalāne ke liye gāthā meṃ yaha batalāyā hai ki upaśamaṇi kā prārambha kisa prakāra hotā hai|
karma śakti ko niṣkriya banāne ke liye do śreṇi haiṃ--upaśamaśreṇi aura kṣapakaśreṇi | ina donoṃ śreṇiyoṃ kā mukhya lakṣya mohanīyakarma ko niṣkriya banāne kā hai| usameṃ se upaśamaṇi meṃ jīva cāritra mohanīyakarma kā upaśama karatā hai aura kṣapaśreṇi meṃ jīva cāritramohanīya aura yathāsaṃbhava anya karmoṃ kā kṣaya karatā hai| inameṃ se jaba jīva upaśamaṇi ko prāpta karatā hai taba pahale anaṃtānubaṃdhī kaṣāya catuṣka kā upaśama karatā hai, tadanantara darśanamohanīya kī tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā upaśama karake upaśamaṇi ke yogya hotā hai | ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke upaśama kā prāraṃbha to avirata samyādṛṣṭi, deśavirata, pramattasaṃyata, apramattasaṃyata aura apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se kisī

saptatikā prakaraṇa bhī guṇasthāna meṃ kiyā jā sakatā hai kintu apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ to niyama se inakā upaśamana ho hī jātā hai| ____ gāthā meṃ anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka ādi sāta prakṛttiyoṃ ke upaśama karane kā nirdeśa karate hue pahale anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka ko upakṣama karane kī sūcanā dī hai ata: pahale isī kā vivecana kiyā jātā hai | anaṃtānubaṃdhī kī upaśamanā ____ anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kī upaśamanā karane vāle svāmī ke prasaṃga meṃ batalāte haiṃ ki avirata samyagdṛṣṭi, deśavirata, virata (pramatta aura apramatta) guṇasthānavī jīvoṃ meṃ se koī bhī jīva kisī bhī yoga meṃ vartamāna ho athāt jisake cāra manoyoga, cāra vacanayoga aura audārika kāyayoga, inameṃ se koī eka yoga ho, jo pīta, padma aura śukla, ina tīna śubha leśyāoṃ meṃ se kisī eka leśyā vālā ho, jo sākāra upayoga vālā (jñānopayoga vālā) ho, jisake āyukarma ke binā sattā meṃ sthita śeṣa sāta karmoṃ kī sthiti antaḥkoḍa़ā-koḍa़ī sāgara ke bhītara ho, jisakī cittavṛtti antarmuhūrta pahale se uttarottara nirmala ho, jo parāvartamāna aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ko choḍa़kara śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha karane lagā ho, jisane aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke sattā meṃ sthita catu:sthānī anubhāga ko dvisthānī kara liyā ho aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke sattā meṃ sthita dvisthānī anubhāga ko catu:sthānī kara liyā ho aura jo eka sthitibaṃdha ke pūrṇa hone para anya sthitibaṃdha ko pūrva-pūrva sthitibaṃdha kī apekṣā uttarottara palya ke saṃkhyātarphe bhāga kama bāṃdhane lagā ho-aisā jīva hī anaṃtānubaṃdhīcatuṣka ko upaśamātā hai |

anaṃtānubaṃdhīcatuṣka kī upaśamanā ke lie vaha jīva yathāpravṛttakaraṇa, apūrvakaraṇa aura anivṛttikaraṇa nāma ke tīna karaṇa karatā | yathāprakaraṇa meṃ to karaṇa ke pahale ke samāna avasthā banī rahatī hai | apūrvakaraṇa meṃ sthitibaṃdha ādi bahuta-sī kiyāyeṃ hone lagatī haiṃ, isaliye ise apūrvakaraṇa kahate haiṃ aura anivṛtikaraṇa meṃ samāna kāla bāloṃ kī viśuddhi samāna hotī hai isīliye ise anivṛttikaraṇa kahate haiṃ | aba ukta viṣaya ko viśeṣa spaṣṭa karate haiṃ ki yathāpravṛttakaraṇa meṃ pratyeka samaya uttarotara anaṃtaguṇī viśuddhi hotī hai aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā yaṃtra ādi pūrvavat cālū rahatā hai | kintu sthitighāta, rasaghāta, guṇaśreṇi aura guṇasaṃkama nahīṃ hotā hai, kyoṃki yahā~ inake yogya viśuddhi nahīṃ pāī jātī hai aura nānā jīvoṃ kī apekṣā isa karaṇa meṃ prati samaya asaṃkhyāta loka pramāṇa pariṇāma hote haiṃ jo chaha sthāna patita hote haiṃ |

ye hāni rūpa chaha sthāna haiṃ | vṛddhi kī apekṣā chaha sthāna isa prakāra haiṃ—1. anaṃta bhāgavṛddhiḥ 2. asaṃkhyāta bhāgavṛddhi, 3. saṃkhyāta bhāgati 4. saṃkhyāta guṇavṛdhi, 5. asaṃkhmāsa guṇavṛddhi aura 6. anta guṇavṛddhi | ___ina ṣasthānoṃ kā āśaya yaha hai ki jaba hama eka jīva kī apekṣā vicāra karate haiṃ taba pahale samaya ke pariṇāmoṃ se dūsare samaya ke pariṇāma anantaguṇī viśuddhi ko liye hue prāpta hote haiṃ aura jaba nānā jīvoṃ kī apekṣā se vicāra karate haiṃ taba eka samayavartī nānā jīvoṃ ke pariṇāma chaha sthāna patita prāpta hote haiṃ tathā yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ke pahale samaya meṃ mānā jīvoṃ kī apekṣā jitane pariṇāma hote haiṃ, usase dūsare samaya ke pariṇāma viśeṣādhika hote haiṃ, dūsare samaya se tīsare samaya meṃ aura tīsare samaya se cauthe samaya meṃ isī prakāra yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ke carama samaya taka viśeṣādhika-viśeṣādhika pariṇāma hote haiṃ | isameṃ bhī pahale samaya me jaghanya viśuddhi sabase thoḍa़ī hotī hai, usase dūsare samaya meṃ jaghanya viśuddhi abhaṃtaguṇī hotī hai, usase tīsare sāra meṃ jaghanya viśuddhi anaṃtaguṇī hotī hai | isa prakāra yaśāpravṛttakaraṇa ke saṃkhyātave bhāga ke prāpta hone taka yahī krama calatā rahatā hai | para yahā~ jo jaghanya viśuddhi prāpta hotī hai, usase pahale samaya kī utkṛṣṭa viśuddhi anaṃtaguṇī hotī hai| ___ tadanantara pahale samaya kī utkṛṣṭa viśuddhi se yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ke saṃkhyāta bhāga ke agale samaya kī jaghanya viśuddhi anaṃtaguṇī hotī hai| punaḥ isase dūsare samaya kī utkṛṣṭa viśuddhi anaṃta guṇī hotī hai | punaḥ usase yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ke saṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga ke āge dūsare samaya kī jaghanya viśuddhi anaṃtaguṇī hotī hai|

śeṣa raha gaye haiṃ, kevala unheṃ uttarottara anaṃtaguṇa karanā cāhiye | yathāpravṛttakaraṇa kā samaya antarmuhūrta pramāṇa hai|
isa taraha antarmuhūrta kāla meṃ yathāpravṛttakāraṇa samāpta hone ke bāda dūsarā apūrvakaraṇa hotā hai | jisakā vivecana isa prakāra hai ki isameṃ pratisamaya asaṃkhyāta loka pramāṇa pariṇāma hote haiṃ jo prati samaya chahasthāna patita hote haiṃ| isameṃ jī rahī samaya meṃ jaghaya viśulisabase thoḍa़ī hotī hai jo yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ke antima samaya meṃ kahī gaī utkṛṣṭa viśuddhi se anantaguṇī hotī hai | puna: isase pahale samaya meṃ hī utkṛṣṭa viśuji anantaguṇī hotī hai | tadanantara isase dūsare samaya meṃ jaghanya viśuddhi anantaguṇī hotī hai | isa prakāra apūrvakaraṇa kā antima samaya prāpta hone taka pratyeka samaya meṃ uttarottara isī prakāra kathana karanā cāhiye| ____ apūrvaka raṇa ke pahale samaya meṃ hī sthitighāta, rasaghāta, guṇaśreṇi, guṇasaṃkrama aura pūrva sthitibandha, ye pā~ca kārya eka sātha prārambha ho jāte haiṃ | jinakā āśaya nimnānusāra hai
sthitighāta meṃ sattā meṃ sthita sthiti ke agrabhāga se adhika se adhika saikaḍa़oṃ sāgara pramāṇa aura kama se kama palya ke saṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa sthitikhaṇḍa kā antamahataṃ kāla ke dvārā ghāta kiyā jātā hai tathā yahāṃ jisa sthiti kā āge calakara bāta nahīṃ hogā, usameṃ prati samaya dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa kiyā jātā hai aura isa prakāra eka antamuhūrta kāla ke bhītara usa sthiti khaṇḍa kā ghāta ho jātā hai | anantara isake nīce ke dūsare patya ke saṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa sthitikhaṇḍa kā ukta prakāra ghāta kiyā jātā hai| isa prakāra apūrvakaraṇa ke kāla meṃ ukta krama se hajāroṃ sthitikhaṇḍoṃ kā ghāta hotā hai| jisase pahale , samaya kī sthiti se anta ke samaya kī sthiti saṃkhyātaguṇī hīna raha jātī hai |

sthitighāta ke āśaya ko spaṣṭa karane ke bāda aba rasadhāta kā
vivecana karate haiṃ |
rasaghāta meṃ aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā sattā meṃ sthita jo anubhāga hai, usake anaṃtaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa anubhāga ko choḍa़kara śeṣa kā antarmuhūrta kāla ke dvārā ghāta kiyā jātā hai | anantara jo anaṃta bhāga anubhāga śeṣa rahā thā usake anaṃtaveṃ bhāga ko choḍa़kara śeṣa kā antarmuhūrta kāla ke dvārā ghāta kiyā jātā hai | isa prakāra eka-eka sthitikhaṇḍa ke utkīraṇa kāla ke bhītara hajāroṃ anubhāga khaṇḍa khapā diye jāte haiṃ |
guṇaśreṇi kā rūpa yaha hotā hai ki guṇaśreṇi meṃ anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kī antarmuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti ko choḍa़kara ūpara kī sthiti vāle dalikoṃ meṃ se prati samaya kucha dalika lekara udayāvali ke ūpara kī antarmuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti meṃ unakā nikṣepa kiyā jātā hai| jisakā kama isa prakāra hai ki pahale samaya meṃ jo ki grahaṇa gi unameṃ se sabase kama dalika udayāvali ke ūpara pahale samaya meṃ sthāpita kiye jāte haiṃ| inase asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalika dūsare samaya meṃ sthāpita kiye jāte haiṃ| inase asaṃkhyātamuṇe damika tīsare samaya meṃ sthāpita kiye jāte haiṃ| isa prakāra antarmuhūrta kāla ke antima samaya taka uttarotara asaṃkhyātaguṇe asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa kiyā jātā hai | yaha prathama samaya meṃ grahaṇa kiye gaye dalikoṃ kī nikṣepa vidhi hai | dūsare ādi samayoṃ meṃ jo dalika grahaṇa kiye jāte haiṃ, unakā nikṣepa bhī isī prakāra hotā hai, kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki suśreṇi kī racanā ke pahale samaya meṃ jo dalika grahaṇa kiye jāte haiṃ ve sabase thoḍa़e hote haiṃ| dūsare samaya meṃ jo dalika grahaṇa kiye jāte haiṃ ve inase asaṃkhyātaguṇe hote haiṃ| isī prakāra guṇaśreṇikaraṇa ke antima samaya ke prāpta hone taka tṛtīyādi samayoṃ meṃ jo dalika grahaṇa kiye jāte haiṃ ve uttarotara asaṃkhyāta yuge hote haiṃ | yahā~ itanī viśeṣatā aura hai ki apūrvakaraṇa

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
4.1
aura anivṛttikaraṇa kā kāla jisa prakāra uttarottara vyatīta hotā jātā hai, tadanusāra guṇaśreṇi ke dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa antarmuhūrta ke uttarotara śeṣa bace hue samayoṃ meṃ hotā hai, antarmuhūrta se ūpara ke samayoṃ meṃ nahīṃ hotā hai| jaise ki māna lo guṇadheṇi ke antarmuhurta kā pramāṇa pacāsa samaya hai aura apūrvakaraṇa tathā anivṛttikaraṇa ina donoṃ ke kāla kā pramāṇa cālīsa samaya hai| aba jo jīva apūrvakaraṇa ke pahale samaya meṃ guṇaśreṇi kī racanā karatā hai vaha guṇaśreṇi ke saba samayoṃ meṃ dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa karatā hai tathā dūsare samaya meṃ śeṣa unacāsa samayoṃ meṃ dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa karatā hai| isa prakāra jaise-jaise apūrvakaraṇa kā kāla vyatīta hotā jātā hai vaise-vaise dalikoṃ kā nikṣepa kama-kama samayoṃ meṃ hotā jātā hai| ___ guṇasaṃkrama meṃ karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke dalikoṃ kā saṃkrama hotā hai| ata: guṇasaṃkrama pradeśasaṃkrama kā eka bheda hai| isameṃ pratisamaya uttarottara asaṃkhyāta guṇita krama se abadhyamāna anaṃtānubaṃdhī ādi aśubha karma prakṛtiyoṃ ke karma dalikoṃ kā usa samaya baṃdhane vālī sajātīya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ saṃkramaṇa hotā hai | yaha kriyā apūrvakaraṇa ke pahale samaya se hī prārambha ho jātī hai|
sthitibaṃdha kā rūpa isa prakāra hotā hai ki apūrvakaraṇa ke pahale samaya se hī jo sthitibaṃdha' hotā hai, vaha apūrva arthāt isake pahale hone vāle sthitibaṃdha se bahuta thoḍa़ā hotā hai| isake sambandha meṃ yaha niyama hai ki sthitibaṃdha aura sthitighāta ina donoṃ kā prārambha eka sātha hotā hai aura inakī samāpti bhī eka sātha hotī hai| isa prakāra ina pāṃcoṃ kāryoṃ kā prārambha apūrvakaraṇa meṃ eka sātha hotā hai || ____ apūrvakaraṇa samāpta hone para anivattikaraṇa hotā hai| isameṃ praviṣṭa hue jīvoṃ ke pariṇāmoṃ meṃ ekarūpatā hotī hai arthāt isa

saptatikā prakaraṇa
karaṇa meṃ praviṣṭa hue jīvoṃ ke jisa prakāra śarīra ke ākāra ādi meṃ pharaka dikhāī detā hai, usa prakāra unake pariṇāmoṃ meṃ pharaka nahīṃ hotā hai, yānī sama : dāle la. sātha meṃ paḍa़e hue jīyoṃ ke pariṇāma samāna hī hote haiṃ aura bhinna samaya vāle jīvoṃ ke pariṇāma sarvathā bhinna hī hote haiṃ| tātparya yaha hai ki anivṛttikaraṇa ke pahale samaya meṃ jo jīva haiṃ, the aura hoṃge, una sabake pariṇāma eka se hī hote haiṃ| dūsare samaya meṃ jo jīva haiṃ, the aura hoṃge, unake bhī pariṇāma ekase hī hote haiṃ| isī prakāra tṛtīya ādi samayoṃ meṃ bhī samajhanā cāhiye | isaliye anivattikaraṇa ke jitane samaya hai, utane hī isake pariṇāma hote haiṃ, nyūnādhika nahīṃ | kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki isake prathama ādi samayoṃ meṃ jo viśuddhi hotī hai, dvitīya ādi samayoṃ meṃ vaha uttarottara anaṃtaguṇī hotī hai| ___apūrvakaraṇa ke sthitighāta ādi pāṃcoṃ kārya anivṛttikaraṇa meṃ bhī cālū rahate haiṃ| isake antarmuhūrta kāla meṃ se saṃkhyāta bhāgoṃ ke bīta jāne para jaba eka bhāga śeṣa rahatā hai taba anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣva ke eka āvali pramāṇa nīce ke niṣekoṃ ko choḍa़kara antarmuharta pramāṇa nivekoṃ kā antarakaraṇa kiyā jātā hai | isa kriyā ko karane meṃ nyūnatama sthitibaṃdha ke kāla ke barābara samaya lagatā hai| yadi udayavālī prakṛtiyoṃ kā antaraka raṇa kiyā jātā hai to unakī sthiti antarmuhūrta pramāṇa aura yadi anudayavālī prakṛtiyoṃ kā antaraka raṇa kiyā jātā hai to unakī nīce kī sthiti āvali' pramāṇa choḍa़ dī jātī hai |
1 sthitidhāta ādi pāpoṃ kāryoṃ kā vivaraṇa apūrvakaraṇa ke prasaṃga meṃ batāyā
jā cukā hai, tadanurūpa yahā~ bhī samajhanā cāhiye || 2 eka āvali yā antarmuhūrta pramāṇa nīce kī aura ūpara kī sthiti ko choḍa़
kara madhya meṃ se antarmuhūrta pramāṇa dalikoṃ ko uṭhākara unakā baṃdhane vālī anya sajātīya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ prakṣepa karane kā nāma antarakaraṇa hai |

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha cūṃki yahā~ anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kā antarakaraṇa karanā hai kintu usakā cauthe ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ udaya nahīṃ hotā hai isaliye isake nīce ke āvali pramāṇa dalikoṃ ko choḍa़kara ūpara ke antarmuhurta pramāṇa dalikoṃ kā antarakaraṇa kiyā jātā hai|
aṃtarakaraṇa meṃ antara kā artha vyavadhāna aura karaṇa kā artha kriyā hai | tadanusāra jina prakṛtiyoṃ kā antarakaraṇa kiyā jātā hai, unake dalikoṃ kī paṃkti ko madhya se maṃga kara diyā jātā hai| isase dalikoṃ kī tīna avasthāyeṃ ho jātī haiṃ—prathama sthiti, sāntarasthiti aura uparitama yā dvitīyasthiti | prathama sthiti kā pramāṇa eka āvali yā eka antarmuhurta hotā hai| isake bāda sānta rasthiti prāpta hotī hai | yaha dalikoṃ se śūnya avasthā hai | isakā bhī samaya pramāṇa antarmuhūrta hai | isake bāda dvittīyasthiti prāpta hotī hai| isakā pramāṇa dalikoṃ kī śeṣasthiti hai|
antarakaraṇa karane se pahale dalikoṃ kī paṃkti0 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 ... isa prakāra avicchinna rahatī hai kintu antarakara kara lene para usakī avasthā 00000000000 isa prakāra ho jātī hai| yahā~ madhya meṃ jo riktasthāna dikhatā hai, vahā~ ke kucha dalikoṃ ko yathāsaṃbhava baṃdhane vālī anya sajātīya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ milā diyā jātā hai| isa aṃtara sthāna se nīce (pahale) kī sthiti ko prathamasthiti aura ūpara (bāda) kī sthiti ko dvitīya sthiti kahate haiṃ | udayabālī prakṛtiyoṃ ke antarakaraṇa karane kā kāla aura prathamasthiti kā pramāṇa samāna hotā hai kintu anudayavālī prakṛtiyoṃ kī prathamasthiti ke pramāṇa se antarakaraṇa karane kā kāla bahuta baḍa़ā hotā hai | antarakaraṇa kriyā ke cālu rahate hue udayabālī prakṛtiyoṃ kī prathama sthiti kā eka-eka dalika udaya meṃ ākara nirjīrṇa hotā jātā hai aura anudayavālī prakṛtiyoṃ kī prathama

404
saptatikā prakaraṇa sthiti ke eka-eka dalika kā udaya meṃ āne vālī sajātīya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ stibuka saṃkramaṇa ke dvārā saṃkrama hotā rahatā hai |
yahā~ anaṃtānubaṃdhī ke upaśama kā kathana kara rahe haiṃ kintu usakā udaya yahī nahīṃ hai, ata: isake prathama sthagita pratyeka dalika kā bhī stibukasaṃkramaṇa dvārā para-prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ saṃkramaṇa hotā rahatā hai| isa prakāra antarakaraṇa ke ho jāne para dūsare samaya meṃ anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kī dvitīyasthiti vāle dalikoṃ kā upaśama kiyā jātā hai | pahale samaya meṃ ghoḍa़e dalikoṃ kā upaśama kiyā jātā hai| dūsare samaya meṃ usase asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalikoṃ kā, tīsare samaya meṃ usase bhī asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalikoṃ kā upaśama kiyā jātā hai| isī prakāra antarmuhataṃ kāla taka asaṃkhyātaguṇe-asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalikoṃ kā pratisamaya upaśama kiyā jātā hai| itane samaya meṃ samasta anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kā upaśama ho jātā hai | jisa prakāra dhūli ko pānī se sīṃca-sauṃce kara duramuṭa se kūṭa dene para vaha jama jātī hai, usī prakāra karma raja bhī viśuddhi rūpī jala se sīṃca-soṃca kara anivṛttikaraṇa rūpī duramuṭa ke dvārā kūṭa diye jāne para saṃkramaṇa, udaya, udīraṇā, nitti aura nikācanā ke ayogya ho jātī hai | isī ko anaṃtānubaṃdhī kā upaśama kahate haiṃ|
lekina anya ācāryoṃ kā mata hai ki anantānubaṃdhī catuṣka kā upaśamana hokara visaṃyojamā hī hotī hai| visaṃyojanā kṣapaṇā kā
1 karmaprakṛti grantha meṃ anaṃtānubaṃdhī kī upaśamanā kā spaṣṭa niṣedha kiyā hai vahā~
batāyā hai ki cauthe, pāṃcaveṃ aura chaṭhe guṇasthānavartī yathāyogya cāroṃ gati ke paryāpta jīva tīna karaṇoṃ ke dvārā anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kā visaṃyojana karate haiṃ | kintu visaṃyojana karate samaya na to antarakaraṇa hotā hai aura na anaṃtānubaṃdhī catuṣka kā upaśama hī hotā hai
caugaiyā pajatā tini vi saṃyojaṇe viyojati | karaṇeti tīhi sahiyā naṃtarakaraṇaṃ usamo vā ||

ṣaṣṭha pharma grantha
405 hī dūsarā nāma hai, kintu visaṃyojanā aura kṣapaṇā meṃ sirpha itanā aṃtara hai ki jina prakṛtiyoṃ kī visaṃyojanā hotī hai, unakī puna: sattā prāpta ho jātī hai, kintu jina prakṛtiyoṃ kī kṣapaṇā hotī hai, unakī puna: sattā prāpta nahīṃ hotī hai |
anantānubaṃdhī kī visaṃyojanā avirata samyādṛṣṭi guṇasthāna se lekara apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna taka kisī eka guṇasthāna meṃ hotī hai | cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ cāroṃ gati ke jīva anantānubandhī kī visaṃyojanā karate haiṃ| pāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ tithaṃca aura manuṣya anantānubandhī kī visaṃyojanā karate haiṃ aura chaṭhe va sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ manuṣya hī anantānubaṃdhī kī visaṃyojanā karate haiṃ| isake liye bhī pahale ke samāna yaśāttakaraṇa bāda tīna bāra kire jo haiṃ| lekina itanī viśeṣatā hai ki visaṃyojanā ke liye antarakaraṇa kī āvaśyakatā nahīṃ hotī hai kintu āvali pramāṇa dalikoṃ ko choḍa़kara ūpara ke saba dalikoṃ kā anya sajātīya prakṛti rūpa se saṃkramaṇa karane aura āvali pramāṇa dalikoṃ kā vedyamāna prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ saṃkramaṇa karake unakā vināśa kara diyā jātā hai|
isa prakāra anantānubandhī kī upaśamanā aura visaṃyojanā kā vicāra kiyā gayā aba darśanamohanīya kī tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kī upaśamanā kā vicāra karate haiṃ|
mātā hā
digambara paramparā meṃ kaṣāyapāhuḍa, usakī cūṇi, pakhaṃḍāgama aura lakṣisāra meṃ bhī anantānubaṃdhī ke visayojana vāle mata kā hī ullekha milatā hai| karmaprakṛti ke samāna kāmapāhura kī pūṇi meṃ bhī anantānubaṃdhī ke upakāma kā spaṣṭa niṣedha kiyā hai, lekina digambara paramparā meṃ pracalita saptatikā meṃ upaśama yālā mata bhī pāyā jātā hai aura go0 karmakāṃḍa se isa bāta kā avaśya patā lagatā hai ki ve anaṃtānubaṃdhī ke upaśama vāle mata se paricita the |

406
saptatikā prakaraṇa
varśanamohanīya kī upaśamanā ___darśanamohanīya kī tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kī upaśamanā ke viṣaya meṃ yaha niyama hai ki mithyātva, rāmyagmidhyātva aura samyaktva yaha darśana bhāhanoca kī tīna prakālaya hai| unameṃ se mithyātva kā upaśama to mithyāṣṭi aura vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva karate haiṃ, kintu samyaktva
aura samyagamithyātva ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kā upaśama vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva hī karate haiṃ | isameṃ bhī cāroṃ gati kā mithyādṛṣṭi jīva jānna prathama samyaktva ko utpanna karatā hai taba mithyāla kā upaśama karatā hai| mithyātva ke upaśama karane kī vidhi pūrva meṃ batāī gaī anantānubaṃdhī catuṣkā ke upaśama ke samāna jānanā cāhiye kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki isake apūrvakaraṇa meṃ guṇasaṃkrama nahīṃ hotā kintu sthitighāta, rasaghāta, sthitibaṃdha aura guṇaśreṇi, ye pyāra kārya hote haiṃ|
1 digambara karbhagranthoṃ meṃ isa viṣaya ke nirdeśa māca yaha hai ki mithyādṛṣṭi
eka mithyātva kā, mithyātva aura samyamithyātva ina donoṃ kā yā mithyātva, sampamithyātva aura samyaktva, ina tīnoṃ kā tathā sammādṛṣṭi dvitīyopaśama samyaktva kī prāpti ke samaya tīnoṃ kā upaśāma karatā hai| jo jīva sampavasva se cyuta hokara mithyātva meṃ jākara vedakakāla kā ullaṃghana kara ātā hai, yaha madi samyaktva kī udavalanā hone ke kāla meṃ hī upaśama sambakāva ko prāpta hotā hai to usake tīnoṃ kā upaśama hotā hai | jo jīva samyaktva kī ubalanā ke bāda samyagamithyātva kī udalanā hote samaya yadi upaśamasamyaktva ko prāpta karatā hai to usake mithyāba aura samyagamithyātva ina do kā upaśama hotā hai aura jo mohanīya kī sabbīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā vālā miśyādṛṣṭi hotā hai, usake eka miśyātva
kā hī upaśama hotā hai| 2 tatra mithyātvasyopaśamanā mithyādṛṣ vedakasamyagdṛṣṭeśca | samyaktva sampagmithyātvayostu vedakasamyāhāṭereva |
-saptatikā prakaraṇa ṭīkā, pṛ0 246

ṣaṣṭha karmagrantha
407 mithyā dṛṣṭi ke niyama se mithyātva kā udaya hotā hai| isaliye isake guṇadheṇi kī racanā udaya samaya se lekara hotī hai | apūrvakaraṇa ke bāda anivṛttikaraṇa meṃ bhī isī prakāra jānanā cāhiye | kintu isake saṃkhyāta bhāgoṃ ke bīta jāne para jaba eka bhāga śeṣa raha jātā hai taba mithyātva ke antarma harta pramāṇa nīce ke niṣekoṃ ko choḍa़kara, isase kucha adhika annālā pramāṇa para ke nirenoṃ kā anta kā kiyā jātā hai| isa kriyā meṃ nūtana sthitibaṃdha ke samāna antarmuhūrta kāla lagatā hai| yahāṃ jina dalikoṃ kā anta rakaraṇa kiyā jātā hai, unameṃ se kucha ko prathama sthiti meṃ aura kucha ko dvitīya sthiti meṃ ḍāla diyā jātā hai, kyoṃki mithyāiṣṭi ke mithyātva kā para-prakṛti rūpa saṃkramaṇa nahīṃ hotā hai| isake prathamāsthiti meṃ āvali pramāṇa kāla śeṣa rahane taka prathamasthiti ke dalikoṃ kī udīraṇā hotī hai kintu dvitīya sthiti ke dalikoṃ kī udīraṇā prathama sthiti meṃ do āvali pramāṇakāla śeṣa rahane taka hī hotī hai| yahā~ dvitīya sthiti ke dalikoṃ kī udīraṇā ko āgāla kahate haiṃ|
isa prakāra yaha jīva prathamasthiti kā vedana karatā huā jaba prathamasthiti ke antima sthāna sthita dalika kā bedana karatā hai, taba vaha antarakaraṇa ke ūpara dvitīya sthiti meṃ sthita mithyātva ke dalikoṃ ko anubhāga ke anusāra tīna bhāgoṃ meṃ vibhakta kara detā hai| inameṃ se viśuddha bhāga ko samyaktva, ardhaviśuddha bhāga ko sampamithyātva aura sabase aviśuddha bhāga ko mithyātva kahate haiṃ | karmaprakṛti cūrṇi meṃ kahā bhī hai
caramasamamamiyādiṅī sekāse upasamasammaviṭṭhī hohiha tāhe viyiṭhiī tihānubhāgaṃ karei, saṃjahā.-sammataṃ sammāmiṭataṃ micchattaṃ bha |
isa taraha prathamasthiti ke samāpta hone para mithyātva ke dalika kā udaya nahīṃ hone se aupaśamika samyaktva prāpta hotā hai | isa

408
saptatikā prakaraṇa
samyaktva ke prāpta hone para alabdha pūrva ātmahita kī upalabdhi hotī hai
minabae prope lahae sammattamogasamiyaṃ so|
labhega bassa lambhara āpahiyamalayapuvaṃ jaṃ || yaha prathama samyaktva kā lābha mithyātva ke pūrṇa rūpeṇa upaśama se prāpta hotā hai aura isake prāpta karane vāloṃ meṃ se koī deśavirata aura koī sarvavirata hotā hai| arthāt samyaktva prāpti ke paścāt saṃyama lābha ke lie prayāsa kiyā jātā hai|
kintu isa prathamopaśama samyaktva se jīva upaśamaṇi para na caḍha़kara dvitīyopaśama samyaktva se caḍha़tā hai| ata: usake bāre meṃ batāte haiṃ ki jo vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva anantānubandhī kaṣāya catuṣka aura darśanamohatrika kā upaśama karake upaśamasamyaktva ko prāpta hotā hai, use dvitīyopaśama samyaktva kahate haiṃ | inameṃ se anantānubandhī ke upaśama hone kā kathana to pahale kara āye haiṃ | aba yahā~ darśanamohanīya ke upaśama hone kī vidhi ko saṃkṣepa meṃ batalāte haiṃ| ___ jo vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi jīva saṃyama meṃ vidyamāna hai, vaha darśanamohanīya kī tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā upaśama karatā hai| isake yathāpravṛttakaraṇa ādi tīna karaṇa pahale ke samāna jānanā cāhiye kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki anivṛttikaraṇa ke saṃkhyāta bhāgoṃ ke bīta jāne para antarakaraṇa karate samaya samyaktva kī prathama sthiti antarmahurta pramāṇa sthāpita kī jātī hai, kyoṃki yaha vedyamāna prakṛti hai tathā samyagmithyātva aura mithyātva kī prathama sthiti ābali pramāṇa sthāpita kī jātī hai kyoṃki vedaka samyagdṛṣṭi ke ina donoṃ kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai | yahā~ ina tīnoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ ke jina dalikoṃ kā aṃtarakaraṇa kiyā jātā hai, unakā nikṣepa samyaktva kī prathamasthiti meṃ hotā hai | 1 kramaprakṛti, mā0 330
